However, existing hybrid text-graph RAG methods typically rely on heuristic and LLM-based routing, resulting in unnecessary overhead and strong dependence on the underlying LLM. To address these challenges, we propose R$^{2}$Adapter, a lightweight plug-in Routing and Rewriting Adapter designed to allocate queries between vanilla and graph-based RAG dynamically. By routing only the queries that genuinely benefit from graph-based reasoning, R$^{2}$Adapter reduces unnecessary graph retrieval overhead. Additionally, uncertain graph-routed queries are rewritten to better expose their multi-hop reasoning requirements, improving retrieval quality without additional supervision. Extensive experiments on three multi-hop QA benchmarks demonstrate that R$^{2}$Adapter reduces graph-based RAG usage by up to 59% while maintaining comparable answer accuracy. This adapter is model-agnostic and can be seamlessly integrated into diverse vanilla and graph-based RAG pipelines, providing an efficient and adaptive solution for hybrid RAG systems.

</details>


### [108] [BharatGather: A Culturally-Informed Benchmark Dataset for Misinformation and Fake News Detection in Indian Public Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02895)
*Parth Bramhecha,Smit Deshmukh,Sairaj Bodhale,Adwait Borate,Raviraj Joshi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了BharatGather，一个针对印度大型公共活动中虚假信息分类的多源数据集，包含14,646条记录，旨在填补现有基准在印度社会文化背景和事件特定动态方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有虚假信息检测基准未能捕捉印度背景下大型公共活动（如宗教节日、政治集会和文化聚会）中的社会文化细微差别和事件特定动态，而这些活动正因虚假信息快速传播而面临公共安全和社会凝聚力的风险。

Method: 通过混合管道构建数据集，包括系统性地抓取知名事实核查平台的网络数据、多媒体转录提取，以及使用大语言模型（LLM）进行合成增强以确保叙事多样性。

Result: 创建了一个包含14,646条记录的 curated 多源数据集BharatGather，专门用于印度大众聚集环境中的二元虚假信息分类。

Conclusion: 该工作为开发文化知情的检测系统提供了资源，并为评估高风险公共环境中虚假信息检测系统的性能建立了严格的基准。

Abstract: Large-scale public events, such as religious festivals, political rallies, and cultural gatherings, are increasingly vulnerable to the rapid dissemination of misinformation, posing substantial risks to public safety and social cohesion. While automated fake news detection has seen significant methodological progress, existing benchmarks frequently fail to capture the socio-cultural nuances and event-specific dynamics characteristic of the Indian context. This paper introduces BharatGather, a curated, multi-source dataset specifically engineered for binary misinformation classification within the ecosystem of Indian mass gatherings. The corpus comprises 14,646 records constructed through a hybrid pipeline involving systematic web scraping of prominent fact-checking platforms, multimedia transcript extraction, and Large Language Model (LLM)-mediated synthetic augmentation to ensure narrative diversity. By providing a resource tailored to the unique complexities of event-aware misinformation in India, this work facilitates the development of culturally informed detection systems and establishes a rigorous benchmark for evaluating their performance in high-stakes public environments.

</details>


### [109] [PiPMRE: A Pipeline Based on Language Model for Medical Relation Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02896)
*Jiaxin Duan,Fengyu Lu,Junfei Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于语言模型的新型管道框架PiPMRE，用于医疗关系抽取，通过关系生成器和关系过滤器两个组件，无需标签方案即可实现高效抽取，在两个公共数据集上超越了现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗关系抽取研究多将其视为序列标注任务，导致标签方案设计困难或因医疗实体间复杂关系而抽取失败，因此需要从语言学视角重新审视该任务并改进方法。

Method: 提出PiPMRE管道框架，包含关系生成器和关系过滤器：生成器首先从文本中生成多个关系三元组，过滤器对每个三元组进行评分并保留通过阈值的作为最终结果；使用简单模板重构输入文本，确保实体和关系按上下文顺序生成，无需标签方案。

Result: 在两个公共数据集上的实验结果表明，PiPMRE平均超越现有最先进方法5.6个召回率点和4.4个准确率点，并在少样本设置下也表现出优越性。

Conclusion: PiPMRE框架通过语言模型和管道设计有效提升了医疗关系抽取性能，无需复杂标签方案即可处理实体间复杂关系，在常规和少样本场景下均具有显著优势。

Abstract: Medical relation extraction (MRE) is commonly known for extracting entities and their relations jointly from a medical text, which has attracted considerable attention in recent years. Previous studies treat MRE as a sequence tagging task, which results in either a challenging design of the tagging schema or a failed extraction of multiple relations, due to intricate relationships among medical entities. In this work, we review the task from the linguistic perspective and propose a novel pipeline framework, PiPMRE, developed on language models to enhance MRE performance. Specifically, PiPMRE consists of a relation generator and a relation filter. Given a text, the generator first yields multiple relational triplets, and then the filter scores each triplet and retains only those that pass the borderline as the final results. Implementing PiPMRE requires no tagging schema; instead, we use a simple template to reformulate the input text, ensuring that entities and relations are generated in a contextual order. Extensive experimental results on two public datasets demonstrate the advancement of PiPMRE. It surpasses the previous state-of-the-art by an average of 5.6 recall points and 4.4 accuracy points. PiPMRE's superiority is also demonstrated in few-shot settings.

</details>


### [110] [Margins, Not Windows: Training-Free Per-Step Lossy Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02897)
*Oszkár Urbán,Young D. Kwon,Stylianos I. Venieris,Cecilia Mascolo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaptiveSpec，一种无需训练的自适应推测解码方法，通过动态调整验证规则和草稿树结构，显著提升了大语言模型的推理吞吐量，同时保持了高准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的树注意力草稿器（如EAGLE-3）通常固定两个决策：严格的令牌匹配验证规则和静态的草稿树形状。先前的工作仅在孤立且受限的假设下放松了其中一项，缺乏能同时自适应调整两者的通用方法。

Method: 提出AdaptiveSpec方法，利用解码过程中产生的内部信号进行每步自适应：1) 引入“每步边际规则”，当目标模型对草稿令牌的概率与最高概率之比超过阈值时，允许不匹配的草稿令牌通过，该规则不依赖草稿长度或架构；2) 设计“每步树策略”，结合草稿最高置信度和滚动接受历史，动态调整草稿树的深度、宽度和节点数，使总草稿数量可变而非仅重新分配。

Result: 在SGLang生产级服务引擎上实现，相比最先进的EAGLE-3方法，AdaptiveSpec将吞吐量最高提升了56%。在GSM8K、MATH-500和HumanEval基准测试及三个目标模型上，恢复了93%至完全无损的任务准确率。

Conclusion: AdaptiveSpec通过在正交轴上协同优化验证规则和树结构，有效克服了现有推测解码方法的局限性，在大幅提升推理效率的同时保证了任务精度，具有显著的实用价值。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates LLM inference by drafting candidate tokens and verifying them in parallel. Tree-attention drafters such as EAGLE-3 are widely adopted, yet typically hold two decisions fixed: (1) a strict token-match verification rule and (2) a static draft-tree shape. Prior work relaxes each in isolation under limiting assumptions: long draft chains for training-free lossy verification, and adaptive tree shaping under a fixed token budget. We introduce AdaptiveSpec, a training-free per-step speculative decoding method that adapts both decisions from internal signals already produced during decoding. A per-step margin rule promotes a mismatched draft-proposed token when the ratio of the target's probability on the drafted token to its top-1 probability exceeds a threshold with no dependence on draft length or underlying drafter architecture. A per-step tree policy adjusts the draft tree's depth, width, and node count directly from a fused signal of draft top-1 confidence and a rolling acceptance history capturing recent draft-target agreement, allowing the total draft count to vary rather than only be redistributed. The two adaptations operate on orthogonal axes and compound in effect. Implemented on the SGLang production-grade serving engine, AdaptiveSpec improves throughput over the state-of-the-art autoregressive speculative decoding method EAGLE-3 by up to 56%, recovering 93% to fully lossless task accuracy across GSM8K, MATH-500, and HumanEval on three target models (DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B, Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, Qwen3-8B).

</details>


### [111] [Distilled Rapid Embedding Transfer (DRET): Parameter-Efficient Biomedical Domain Adaptation via Priority-Based Embedding Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02898)
*Girish Sundaram,Daniel Berleant*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DRET框架，将大型生物医学模型（如BioBERT）的领域知识注入轻量级模型（如DistilBERT），在无需重训原始语料的情况下，使小模型在PICO分类任务上达到与大模型相当甚至更优的性能，同时保持高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型领域专用模型（如BioBERT）虽性能强但计算成本高，难以部署；而轻量级通用模型（如DistilBERT）缺乏生物医学领域知识，难以胜任PICO等专业任务。需在保持效率的同时注入领域知识。

Method: 提出迭代式知识迁移范式DRET：1.x统一词表合并，2.0混合嵌入平均，3.x基于优先级的嵌入迁移（按权威源分层选择），4.x结合嵌入层冻结、差异学习率、标签传播及不平衡感知损失函数。

Result: 在严重类别不平衡的EBM-NLP数据集上，DRET增强的DistilBERT（66M参数）在平衡准确率、召回率及ROC-AUC等12项指标上，媲美甚至超越大一个数量级的模型；嵌入层迁移效果经余弦相似度、语义偏移及t-SNE分析验证。

Conclusion: DRET提供了一条可扩展、资源高效的路径，使轻量级模型获得接近领域专家的性能，可直接应用于自动系统文献综述和临床决策支持。

Abstract: Large domain-specific language models such as BioBERT and ClinicalBERT achieve strong performance on biomedical NLP tasks, but their computational demands make them impractical for many real-world deployments. General-purpose, parameter-efficient models such as DistilBERT are lightweight yet lack the domain knowledge required for specialized tasks such as PICO (Population, Intervention, Comparison, Outcome) classification. We introduce Distilled Rapid Embedding Transfer (DRET), a knowledge-transfer paradigm that injects biomedical domain knowledge from large specialized models into a smaller general-purpose model without retraining on the original specialized corpora. DRET is developed as an iterative family of strategies: a unified tokenizer-merge strategy (DRET 1.x), hybrid embedding averaging (DRET 2.0), and a priority-based embedding-transfer mechanism (DRET 3.x) that hierarchically selects embeddings from the most authoritative source models, further combined with embedding-layer freezing, differential learning rates, label propagation, and imbalance-aware loss functions (DRET 4.x). We evaluate DRET on token-level PICO classification using the EBM-NLP corpus under severe class imbalance, across a twelve-metric battery. DRET-enhanced DistilBERT (66M parameters) attains balanced accuracy, recall, and ROC-AUC competitive with, and on several class-wise metrics exceeding, models an order of magnitude larger, while retaining DistilBERT's efficiency. We further show that transfer occurs at the embedding level through cosine-similarity, semantic-shift, and t-SNE analyses. DRET offers a scalable, resource-efficient route to near-domain-expert performance for biomedical text mining, with direct application to automated systematic literature reviews and clinical decision support.

</details>


### [112] [Contamination Inflates Scores but Rarely Reorders Large Language Model Leaderboards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02899)
*Xingyao Xiao,Yihong Cheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出基准污染主要影响绝对分数而非模型排名，并开发了基于锚定项不变性的校准审计方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决当前对大语言模型排行榜中基准污染问题的担忧，区分污染是否抬高绝对分数与是否改变模型排名这两个不同问题。

Method: 将污染重新定义为锚定项不变性的违反，通过原始项与语义等价改写项的差异功能来测量，在固定技能测量的同时隔离记忆化与能力。使用47个公开模型和74个已知污染剂量的微调模型在四个基准上进行测试。

Result: 测量方法能剂量响应性地恢复注入的污染（校正效应+0.187准确率点），阴性对照模型无异常（-0.012）。标准排行榜与改写控制排行榜的排名相关性为0.997，仅3/188个模型 - 基准案例显示差异污染。

Conclusion: 公共模型中的污染主要是均匀的，抬高绝对分数但不重新排序排行榜；排名扭曲需要罕见的差异污染情况。建议排行榜报告改写控制排名及置信区间。

Abstract: Benchmark contamination, the leakage of test items into training data, is widely described as a threat to the reliability of large language model (LLM) leaderboards. We argue that this concern conflates two distinct questions: whether contamination inflates absolute scores, and whether it reorders the ranking of models. We recast contamination as a violation of anchor-item invariance and measure it through the differential functioning of original versus semantically equivalent paraphrased items, a within-item contrast that holds the measured skill fixed and isolates memorization from capability. Using per-instance responses from 47 publicly released models and 74 models finetuned with a known dose of contamination, across four benchmarks (ARC, GSM8K, HellaSwag, MMLU), we first calibrate the measure against ground truth: it recovers injected contamination dose-responsively (a corrected effect of +0.187 accuracy points for test-set leakage) and never flags a negative-control model trained only on the legitimate training split (-0.012). We then quantify leaderboard impact: the rank correlation between a standard leaderboard and a paraphrase-controlled leaderboard is 0.997, and a sensitivity analysis shows that the observed differential contamination is far below the level needed to move rankings, with only 3 of 188 model-by-benchmark cases showing differential contamination corroborated across two references. Contamination among these public models is therefore largely uniform: it inflates absolute scores without reordering the leaderboard, and ranking distortion requires the rare case of differential contamination. We provide a calibrated invariance audit, released as a reference implementation, and recommend that leaderboards report paraphrase-controlled rankings alongside confidence intervals.

</details>


### [113] [Unifying Conformal Language Tasks with In-Context Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03005)
*Xiao Shi Huang,Chen-Yuan Lin,Bruce Kuwahara,Kin Kwan Leung,Jesse C. Cresswell*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了“保形相关性”（Conformal Relevance）框架，利用上下文学习示例筛选和集成技术，在无需大量人工提示工程的情况下，为NLP任务构建了既能保证信息覆盖率又能提高简洁性的评分函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的保形预测方法虽能保证覆盖率，但需依赖人工设计的提示工程来优化简洁性，这一过程耗时且特定于任务，缺乏通用性和自动化。

Method: 提出Conformal Relevance框架，通过精心策划的上下文学习（in-context learning）示例和集成（ensembling）策略来自动生成评分函数，并理论上分析了集成分数的多样性影响，提出了互补性条件和饱和界限。

Result: 该框架在七种NLP任务上得到了验证，证明了其在保持覆盖率的同时有效提升了简洁性；理论分析揭示了集成方法改善最坏情况句子分数的条件及收益上限。

Conclusion: Conformal Relevance框架成功解决了手动提示工程的痛点，通过自动化和理论指导的集成方法，实现了在多种NLP任务中覆盖率与简洁性的更优平衡。

Abstract: Many NLP tasks, such as summarization and extractive question answering, reduce to retrieving relevant content from documents under two constraints: coverage, retaining enough pertinent information to achieve some goal, and conciseness, removing as much irrelevant information as possible. Conformal prediction methods have been used to guarantee coverage, and must be optimized for conciseness through design of a score function. State-of-the-art scoring functions use hand-engineered LLM prompts asking the model to rate the importance of content, but manual prompt engineering is labor-intensive and task-specific. We introduce the Conformal Relevance framework which uses in-context learning example curation and ensembling to create a score function which maintains coverage while improving conciseness with minimal manual input. We demonstrate this framework's application on seven NLP tasks, and also theoretically study the impact of diversity for ensembled conformal scores, giving a complementarity condition that characterizes when ensembling improves worst-case sentence scores, and a saturation bound on ensemble improvement.

</details>


### [114] [Dual-Form ASR: Semantics-Aware Inverse Text Normalization for Chinese Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02901)
*Fengrun Zhang,Li Fu,Wangjin Zhou,Lu Fan,Youzheng Wu,Xiaodong He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Dual-Form ASR (DF-ASR) 框架，通过配对口语和书面语监督，将口语ASR能力扩展到语义感知的书面语ITN，同时保留提示级的输出形式选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ASR-ITN采用级联模块，口语ASR输出由独立ITN组件重写，导致书面语输出易受识别错误影响，且归一化与声学上下文建模脱节，特别是语义依赖的数值表达式。

Method: 提出DF-ASR框架，通过LLM驱动的生成-判断工作流构建双形式监督，采用ITN-MWER序列级目标增强训练，并引入决策感知的REQUIRE-ITN/FORBID-ITN协议评估。

Result: 在SpeechIO手动标注的中文子集上，DF-ASR一致优于开源ASR-ITN系统，与强闭源参考系统保持竞争力，并保留可靠的提示级输出形式控制。

Conclusion: DF-ASR通过双形式监督和语义感知训练，有效解决了级联ASR-ITN的错误传播和建模脱节问题，实现了高质量的书面语输出和灵活的形式控制。

Abstract: Modern automatic speech recognition (ASR) scenarios require both spoken-form transcripts for faithful transcription and readable written-form transcripts with inverse text normalization (ITN). However, these forms are typically produced by cascaded modules, where a spoken-form ASR output is rewritten by a separate ITN component, making written-form ASR-ITN vulnerable to recognition errors and decoupling normalization from acoustic-contextual modeling, especially for semantically dependent numeric expressions. In this paper, we propose Dual-Form ASR (DF-ASR), a framework that extends spoken-form ASR capability to semantics-aware written-form ITN through paired spoken-form and written-form supervision while retaining prompt-level selection between transcript forms. The dual-form supervision is constructed via a large language model (LLM)-driven generate-and-judge workflow, and training is further enhanced by ITN-MWER, a sequence-level objective that assigns higher cost to errors on normalization-sensitive spans. We also introduce a decision-aware REQUIRE-ITN/\FORBID-ITN protocol to separately measure required normalization and forbidden-span preservation. On manually annotated Chinese subsets from SpeechIO, DF-ASR consistently outperforms open-source ASR-ITN systems, remains competitive with strong closed-source references, and preserves reliable prompt-level control between spoken-form and written-form outputs.

</details>


### [115] [How Perturbations Propagate: A Multi-Level Analysis of Robustness in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03322)
*Dun Li Chan,Emily Liu,Niyathi Allu,Christian Hoang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了六类输入扰动在解码器语言模型中的传播机制，发现仅靠输出行为评估鲁棒性具有误导性，需结合隐藏状态几何与注意力头功能进行多层级评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常仅通过输出行为评估语言模型对拼写错误、文本损坏及词序紊乱等输入扰动的鲁棒性，缺乏对扰动在模型内部（如隐藏状态几何和注意力机制）传播过程的深入理解。

Method: 在四个GPT-2和两个Qwen2.5检查点上，利用中心核对齐（CKA）和内在维度分析层级几何结构，并检查GPT-2的注意力头响应；对比了自然扰动与梯度引导的HotFlip扰动对模型行为及表征的影响。

Result: 不同扰动类型产生可区分的指标特征，且不完全一致于输出度量；复制分数与令牌替换及洗牌下的激活修补恢复高度相关；HotFlip扰动比随机替换引起更强的行为与表征破坏，且该效应在所有检查点上一致。

Conclusion: 基于单一行为或表征指标的鲁棒性主张可能产生误导，必须采用多层级评估框架来全面理解扰动如何改变语言模型的内部计算过程。

Abstract: Language models encounter typos, corrupted text, altered words, and disrupted token order, yet robustness is usually evaluated only through output behavior. We study how six naturalistic and synthetic input perturbations propagate through decoder-only language models at three levels: output behavior, hidden-state geometry, and attention-head function. We evaluate behavioral effects across four GPT-2 and two Qwen2.5 checkpoints by analyzing layerwise geometry using centered kernel alignment and intrinsic dimension, and examine attention-head responses in GPT-2. Perturbation types produce distinguishable metric profiles that are not fully captured by output measures and are only partly consistent across the tested checkpoints. Copying scores are especially associated with activation-patching recovery under token substitution and shuffling. Gradient-guided HotFlip perturbations also cause stronger behavioral and representational disruption than rate-matched random token substitutions in GPT-2; their behavioral effects are consistent across all six tested checkpoints. Our results show that robustness claims based on a single behavioral or representational metric can be misleading, and motivate multi-level evaluation of how perturbations alter language-model computation.

</details>


### [116] [RL-ADA: A World-Feedback Framework for Adversarially Robust Enterprise Dialogue Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02902)
*Ram Narayanan,Harshit Rajgarhia,Abhishek Mukherji*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出RL-ADA框架，通过对抗性对话代理的协同进化，利用基于交互结果的“世界反馈”替代人工标注，解决了企业客服对话代理训练中的数据标注瓶颈，在无标注数据情况下显著提升了任务完成率并发现了“上下文伪装”策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决企业级任务导向型对话代理部署中的标注瓶颈：高质量的训练需要大规模标注数据，但企业对话日志涉及隐私且标注成本高昂，同时用户行为演变速度快于标注流程的更新速度。

Method: 提出RL-ADA（基于对抗性对话代理的强化学习）协同进化框架。包含一个客服代理（DA，3B参数）和一个对抗性客户代理（CA，7B参数），在固定自动化评判器的指导下进行对抗训练。DA因成功解决多轮对话获得奖励，CA因生成导致路由错误的真实意图隐藏话语获得奖励。通过隔离健身房（isolation gym）机制，利用先前的失败转录迭代重训较弱的一方，全程无需人工标注。

Result: 在银行客服概念验证中，经过五个协同进化周期，工具路由错误被消除，严格的端到端通过率（PASS rate）翻倍。此外，观察到CA自发涌现出“上下文伪装”（Contextual Camouflage）策略，即在密集的真实客户细节中嵌入意图。

Conclusion: RL-ADA框架成功消除了对人工标注数据的依赖，仅通过自动化竞技场奖励即可实现模型性能的显著提升。该方法不仅优化了客服代理的鲁棒性，其发现的“上下文伪装”现象也为企业红队测试（red-teaming）和鲁棒性评估提供了新的视角和依据。

Abstract: Deploying task-oriented dialogue agents in enterprise customer support faces a persistent annotation bottleneck: robust training requires labelled interaction data at scale, yet enterprise conversational logs are privacy-sensitive and expensive to annotate, while user behaviour evolves faster than labelling pipelines can keep pace. We present RL-ADA (Reinforcement Learning with Adversarial Dialogue Agents), a co-evolutionary training framework that eliminates this bottleneck by replacing human labels with \emph{world feedback}: consequence-based reward signals derived directly from measurable interaction outcomes. A Customer Support Agent (DA, 3B parameters) and an Adversarial Customer Agent (CA, 7B parameters) co-evolve in an adversarial arena guided by a fixed automated judge: the DA is rewarded for correctly handling multi-turn customer conversations to successful resolution, while the CA is rewarded for producing realistic, intent-concealing utterances that cause misroutes, creating asymmetric adversarial pressure through opposing but independently structured rewards. An isolation gym iteratively retrains the weaker agent on prior-failure transcripts, requiring no human annotation at any stage. In a banking customer support proof of concept, tool-routing errors are eliminated and the strict end-to-end PASS rate doubles over five co-evolutionary cycles, driven solely by automated arena reward with no labelled data. We additionally observe the emergence of \textbf{Contextual Camouflage}, an adversarial strategy in which the CA learns to embed intent within dense realistic customer detail purely from reward pressure, with direct implications for enterprise red-teaming and robustness evaluation.

</details>


### [117] [Listen to the Latents: Self-Correcting Speech Recognition in Large Audio Language Models Through Hidden-State Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02940)
*Chan-Jan Hsu,Jaeyeon Kim,Chao-Han Huck Yang,Shinji Watanabe,Hung-yi Lee,Carlos Busso*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出“混合搜索”（Hybrid Search）策略，通过利用预适应基础大语言模型（Base LLM）的交互特征，针对性地修正高语义依赖的token，从而在LoRA适配设置下显著提升基于LLM的自动语音识别（ASR）系统的性能，效果优于传统的全局修正方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于大语言模型（LLM）的自动语音识别（ASR）系统主要通过外部logit融合或内部warm initialization（热初始化）来利用LLM的语义知识，但如何有效结合这两种策略尚缺乏深入探索。特别是在LoRA适配场景中，如何进一步利用被保留的基础LLM来优化已进行热初始化的ASR模型是一个未充分解决的问题。

Method: 提出“混合搜索”（Hybrid Search）策略。该方法基于两个核心观察：1）LLM-based ASR隐藏状态与基础LLM隐藏状态之间的交互特征能反映token的语义依赖程度；2）仅对高语义依赖的特定token进行选择性修正。该方法在推理阶段利用基础LLM对ASR模型的输出进行针对性纠正，而非采用全局性的重评分或晚期融合。

Result: 实验表明，选择性修正高语义依赖token的方法，其ASR性能提升幅度远超包括重评分（rescoring）和晚期融合（late fusion）在内的朴素全局LLM修正方法。

Conclusion: 即使通过热初始化完成了语义知识迁移，基于LLM的ASR模型在推理阶段仍能通过利用其基础LLM进一步性能优化。交互特征是识别需修正token的关键信号，针对性修正策略比全局策略更有效。

Abstract: Recent automatic speech recognition (ASR) systems increasingly integrate large language models (LLMs) to leverage their semantic knowledge, either externally through logit fusion or internally through warm initialization. However, how to effectively combine these two strategies remains underexplored. In this work, we refine warm-initialized LLM-based ASR models by leveraging their own pre-adaptation base LLMs, focusing on LoRA-adapted settings where the base LLM is preserved. To achieve this, we propose Hybrid Search, a targeted correction strategy motivated by two observations. First, interaction features that characterize the relationship between LLM-based ASR hidden states and base-LLM hidden states provide informative signals about a token's degree of semantic dependence. Second, selectively refining targeted tokens with high semantic dependence improves ASR performance far beyond naive global LLM-correction methods including rescoring and late fusion. Our analysis suggests that, even after semantic knowledge transfer through warm initialization, LLM-based ASR models can still leverage their base LLM to further improve inference-time performance.

</details>


### [118] [Judging LLM-as-a-Judge: Concerning Rubric Artifacts in LLM-based Automated Text Generation Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02942)
*Anshul Bagaria,Sowmya S Sundaram,Gokul S Krishnan,Balaraman Ravindran*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现，基于大语言模型（LLM）的评估器（LLM-as-a-Judge）在仅依据评分标准（rubric）文本而无候选回答的情况下，仍能预测评估结果，且对回答或标准的反转缺乏敏感性，揭示了当前基于标准的自动化评估方法存在可靠性隐患。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前广泛使用的“LLM-as-a-Judge”评估范式基于一个核心假设：即评估结果是模型针对候选回答并依据评分标准进行推理得出的。本文旨在检验这一假设的稳健性，探究评估分数是否真的完全依赖于对具体回答的分析，还是部分内嵌于评分标准的表述中。

Method: 1. 训练仅基于评分标准文本（无候选回答输入）的分类器，测试其对评估结果的预测能力；2. 实施反事实扰动（counterfactual perturbations）实验，分别反转候选回答的质量或评分标准的具体准则，观察评估器决策更新的可靠性。

Result: 1. 仅利用评分标准文本训练的分类器取得了非平凡的预测性能，表明评分标准的表述本身编码了可恢复的评估信号，使得分数可在无模型输出的情况下被部分预测；2. 反事实实验显示，当候选回答或评分标准被反转时，评估器往往无法可靠地更新其决策，表现出逻辑不一致性。

Conclusion: 研究结果对基于评分标准的LLM自动化评估的可靠性提出了严峻挑战，表明当前的评估流水线可能并未真正执行基于内容的推理。这凸显了亟需对LLM自动化评估方法进行更深入的方法论研究和改进。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-Judge pipelines are increasingly used to evaluate AI-generated text, based on the assumption that judgments arise from reasoning over candidate responses with respect to a rubric. We show that this assumption warrants further scrutiny. Classifiers trained only on rubric text, without access to any evaluated response, achieve nontrivial predictive performance on judge outputs. This suggests that rubric formulations encode recoverable evaluative signals, allowing scores to be partially anticipated independently of model outputs. Finally, counterfactual perturbations reveal that judges often fail to reliably update their decisions when either the candidate response or the rubric criterion is reversed. Our findings raise concerns about the reliability of rubric-based LLM evaluation and highlight the need for further methodological study of automated evaluation via LLMs.

</details>


### [119] [LexIssue: Benchmarking Legal Issue Identification in Chinese Civil Litigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02954)
*Huiyuan Xie,Yuqin Huang,Zhicheng Hao,Yida Cai,Shaochun Wang,Zhenghao Liu,Yuxiao Ye*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了法律争议点识别的计算建模方法，构建了包含真实案例的LexIssue基准数据集和法律知识库，实验表明基于检索增强的生成方法能显著提升识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别诉讼双方争议的法律问题是现实诉讼的关键环节，但在法律AI研究中，法律问题的计算建模尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 提出基于法律的分层模式，将法律问题表示为自由形式描述和结构化类别，并定义为法律议题生成和分类两个互补任务；构建包含430个中国民事案件和1303个专家标注争议点的LexIssue基准，以及覆盖27个案由和441个候选条目的议题中心法律知识库，用于支持检索增强推理。

Result: 在多种模型上的实验结果显示，利用构建的法律议题知识库进行检索增强生成，能一致地提升在识别争议法律问题及其对应法律属性方面的性能。

Conclusion: 通过构建高质量基准数据集和领域知识库，并结合检索增强生成技术，有效解决了法律争议点识别的计算建模难题，为法律AI应用提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Identifying the issues disputed between litigating parties is a crucial component of real-world litigation. However, legal issues remain comparatively underexplored in legal AI research. In this work, we study the computational modelling of legal issue identification in litigation. We introduce a legally grounded hierarchical schema that represents legal issues through both free-form issue descriptions and structured legal categories, and formulate legal issue identification as two complementary tasks: legal issue generation and legal issue classification. Based on this formulation, we construct LexIssue, a benchmark containing 430 real-world Chinese civil litigation cases and 1,303 expert-annotated disputed legal issues. We further develop an issue-centric legal knowledge base spanning 27 causes of action and 441 candidate legal issue entries to support retrieval-augmented reasoning. Experimental results across a diverse set of models show that retrieval-augmented generation using the constructed legal issue knowledge base consistently improves performance in identifying disputed legal issues and their corresponding legal attributes.

</details>


### [120] [SHELF: A Synthetic Harness for Multi-Task Bibliographic Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03047)
*Michael J. Bommarito*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SHELF是一个Python系统，用于为图书馆和档案馆生成受控的基准数据和评估任务，以测试不同方法在书目工作中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图书馆和档案馆在人员和计算预算有限的情况下管理大量馆藏，但现有基准测试没有系统性地评估其书目工作的方法，因此需要了解哪些方法有效及其运行要求。

Method: SHELF系统利用标记的分类体系、写作规范和生成预算，生成62,899份基于美国国会图书馆词汇表的模型生成文档，并设计分类、聚类、检索、配对分类和指令检索等任务，比较TF、TF-IDF、BM25、编码器和零解码器等方法。

Result: 主题分类准确率达0.8887，体裁形式分类仅0.2605，部分配对和聚类任务接近随机水平；稀疏方法在分类任务上仍有竞争力，TF-IDF在主题计时实验中速度最快；模型排名比绝对分数更可靠，但SHELF分数无法准确估计生产目录数据的准确性。

Conclusion: SHELF提供了可验证的未见文档生成能力和独立变化的书目面，源代码和数据已在GitHub和Hugging Face上以宽松许可发布，为图书馆和档案馆评估方法提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Libraries and archives manage large collections with limited staff and computing budgets, yet common benchmarks do not systematically test their bibliographic work. They need to know which methods work for their tasks and what those methods require to run. SHELF, the Synthetic Harness for Evaluating LLM Fitness, addresses this gap. It is a Python system that turns labelled taxonomies, writing specifications, and a generation budget into controlled benchmark data and evaluation tasks. This first release contains 62,899 model-written documents based on Library of Congress vocabularies, with tasks for classification, clustering, retrieval, pair classification, and instruction retrieval. We compare TF, TF-IDF, BM25, popular encoders, and, on subject classification only, zero-shot decoders; each method appears only on tasks that support it. Subject classification reaches 0.8887, while genre-form classification reaches only 0.2605, and several pair and clustering tasks remain near chance. Sparse methods remain competitive on classification, while TF-IDF is the fastest measured arm in the subject timing experiment. SHELF also varies bibliographic facets independently and can generate new, verifiably unseen documents after a model's training cutoff. Comparisons with LCSHBench and Project Gutenberg show that model rankings transfer more reliably than absolute scores, but SHELF scores do not estimate accuracy on production catalogue data. We release all source code and data under permissive licenses on GitHub and Hugging Face.

</details>


### [121] [Large Language Models in Resolving Contextual Knowledge Conflicts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03148)
*Xinye Yang,Zhenyang Liu,Ruisi Li,Yuanyuan Lei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型（LLM）如何处理上下文知识内部的冲突，构建了包含六类冲突的ContextConflict数据集，揭示了模型对早期证据的偏好偏差，并提出了一种无需训练和标签的激活引导方法以提升冲突解决能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究主要关注LLM内部参数知识与外部上下文之间的冲突，而忽视了上下文知识内部存在的矛盾。为了填补这一空白，本文旨在系统性地探究LLM在面对上下文内部冲突时的表现、机制及改进策略。

Method: 1. 构建了包含六种冲突类型（事实、推理、时间、粒度、视角、歧义）的ContextConflict数据集，涵盖5,781个样本，涉及推理和摘要任务，包含显性矛盾和需多步推理的隐性冲突。
2. 在九个LLM上进行实验评估。
3. 利用机械可解释性技术分析模型处理冲突的潜在意识及表示几何结构。
4. 提出一种简单的、无需训练和标签的激活引导（steering）方法，通过调整激活状态促进证据的全面整合。

Result: 1. 当前模型在解决上下文知识冲突方面表现不足。
2. 发现模型存在一致的“早期证据偏好”（positional preference），这是阻碍有效冲突解决的关键因素。
3. 提出的激活引导方法在推理任务上显著提高了准确率，并在摘要任务中生成了质量更高、更平衡的摘要。

Conclusion: LLM在处理上下文内部冲突时存在明显的局限性，主要受限于对早期信息的偏好。通过机械可解释性分析揭示的机制，采用简单的激活引导策略可以有效缓解这一偏差，无需额外训练即可显著提升模型在复杂冲突场景下的推理和摘要能力。

Abstract: Most prior works focused on conflicts between an LLM's internal parametric knowledge and externally provided context. In contrast, we investigate how LLMs handle conflicts that arise within contextual knowledge itself. We introduce a taxonomy of six types of contextual conflicts (factual, inferential, temporal, granularity, perspective, and ambiguity) and contribute a comprehensive dataset ContextConflict for this setting. The dataset contains 5,781 samples, covers both reasoning and summarization tasks, and includes both explicit contradictions and implicit conflicts that require multi-step reasoning. Experiments on nine LLMs show that current models still fall short in resolving contextual knowledge conflicts. We further provide mechanistic interpretability insights into how LLMs process such conflicts, revealing their latent awareness of conflicts and the representational geometry underlying conflict processing. In addition, our analysis uncovers a consistent model bias towards earlier evidence, and this positional preference serves as a key obstacle to effective conflict resolution. Motivated by these findings, we further propose a simple training-free, label-free steering method that steers activations to encourage a more comprehensive incorporation of evidences for better conflict resolution. On our dataset, the method consistently improves accuracy on reasoning tasks and generates higher-quality, more balanced summaries for summarization tasks.

</details>


### [122] [No country for old linguists: LLM-brain alignment underdetermines neural computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03160)
*Elliot Murphy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文批评Nastase等人(2026)的观点，指出虽然LLM与大脑的表征对齐能约束机制假设，但仅凭对齐无法直接确立共享的计算机制或算法，其从“对齐”推导至“完全机制模型”存在逻辑、因果及计算上的不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对Nastase等人主张大型语言模型（LLM）可作为自然语言的“完全机制模型”并实例化与生物大脑相同计算原则的观点，本文旨在澄清表征对齐研究的推论边界，防止过度解读。

Method: 逻辑分析与理论批判：通过剖析Nastase等人论证中的内在矛盾，特别是其方法论警告（对齐不等于共享架构）与最终结论（LLM为机制模型）之间的张力，识别逻辑、因果和计算层面的“欠决定性”（underdetermination）问题。

Result: 研究发现，表征对齐原则上可以约束机制假设，但其本身不足以识别具体机制；Nastase等人在从“特征捕捉”跃迁至“共享计算原则”及“机制模型”的推论过程中存在逻辑断层。

Conclusion: LLM与大脑的表征对齐不能作为确立共享架构或算法的充分证据，将LLM视为生物语言处理的完全机制模型缺乏严谨的推论支持，需警惕从相关性对齐到因果机制推断的过度跨越。

Abstract: Nastase et al. (2026) argue that large language models (LLMs) may illuminate language processing because both rely on distributed, context-sensitive representations shaped by statistical learning. Their rejection of simple cortical "boxology" is persuasive, and they articulate a strong case for the value of LLM-brain alignment research. The key question is what kind of inference LLM-brain alignment licenses. My claim here will be narrow: representational alignment can in principle constrain mechanistic hypotheses, but it does not by itself identify a mechanism. Nastase et al. acknowledge that an encoding model can capture features represented in neural activity without establishing a shared architecture or algorithm. Yet the authors sometime move from alignment to "shared computational principles" and ultimately to LLMs as mechanistic models of natural language. Indeed, their methodological caveat that alignment does not establish a shared architecture or algorithm sits uneasily with their conclusion that LLMs might instantiate the same computational principles as biological brains and provide a "fully mechanistic model" of language. I discuss what I consider to be problems of logical, causal, and computational underdetermination in Nastase et al.'s (2026) proposal.

</details>


### [123] [Jina-OCR-v1: Efficient Document Parsing with Speculative Decoding and Dense Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03181)
*Alejandro Barón García,Feng Wang,Emilia Garcia Casademont,Han Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Jina-OCR-v1 是一个面向低预算 GPU 的端到端文档解析模型，结合压缩视觉编码器与 3B 混合专家解码器，采用 FastMTP 推测解码技术，在 OmniDocBench 和 olmOCR-Bench 上分别取得 91.14 和 83.4 分，推理速度达 2.57 页/秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在构建一个能在低预算 GPU 上高效运行的端到端文档解析模型，解决现有模型在资源受限环境下推理速度慢、成本高的问题。

Method: 结合压缩视觉编码器与 DeepSeek-OCR 的 3B 混合专家解码器（每 token 激活约 570M 参数），引入 FastMTP 推测解码头（K=3 预测步长共享单一草稿块），采用贪婪验证确保无损解码；后训练阶段结合指令对齐、困难文档鲁棒性微调及基于密集可验证奖励的 GRPO（公式、表格、结构检查给予部分奖励）；训练数据混合清洗后的公开语料与针对性合成页面。

Result: 在默认动态分辨率设置下，OmniDocBench v1.6 得分为 91.14，olmOCR-Bench 得分为 83.4；在低预算 GPU（如 NVIDIA L4）上，FastMTP 使解码速度较贪婪自回归解码提升一倍，达到 2.57 页/秒的最高页面吞吐量。

Conclusion: Jina-OCR-v1 在保持高精度的同时显著提升了低预算 GPU 上的推理效率，模型已开源可供使用。

Abstract: We present Jina-OCR-v1, an end-to-end document parsing model built to serve on low-budget GPUs. It combines the compressed-vision encoder and the 3B mixture-of-experts decoder of DeepSeek-OCR, which activates about 570M parameters per token, with a FastMTP speculative decoding head that shares a single draft block recursively across K=3 prediction steps. Greedy verification makes decoding lossless. Post-training combines instruction alignment, robustness fine-tuning on difficult documents, and GRPO under dense verifiable rewards: deterministic formula, table, and structural checks that award partial credit. The training data mixes cleaned public corpora with targeted synthetic pages. At the default dynamic-resolution setting, Jina-OCR-v1 scores 91.14 on OmniDocBench v1.6 and 83.4 on olmOCR-Bench, and reaches the highest page throughput in our comparison at 2.57 pages per second. On a low-budget GPU such as the NVIDIA L4, FastMTP doubles decoding speed over greedy autoregressive decoding. The model is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/jinaai/jina-ocr-v1.

</details>


### [124] [MemoryLACE: Memory Lifecycle-Aware Consolidation and Evidence Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03201)
*Meriem Yacoubi,Pia Schmidt,Nenad Petrovic,Ahmed Frikha,Martin Kirchhoff,Alois Knoll*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MemoryLACE（MemLACE），一种轻量级记忆框架，通过显式建模文本证据的生命周期（稀疏合并、替代和矛盾关系）来提升长期LLM代理的记忆推理能力，在保持原子自然语言记忆的同时显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM长期记忆系统存在不足：文本记忆系统虽能高效检索语义相关记忆，但常使证据间关系（如重复证据、历史状态、更新、未解决矛盾）隐式化；结构化方法虽能建模这些关系，但依赖全局图、层次抽象或反思机制，复杂度高。需要一种轻量级方案显式建模证据生命周期。

Method: 提出MemLACE框架，核心机制包括：1）显式建模文本证据生命周期，通过稀疏合并（sparse merge）、替代（supersession）和矛盾（contradiction）关系；2）保留原子自然语言记忆及其来源（provenance）；3）重构关系感知证据单元，暴露当前、历史、支持和冲突证据供下游推理使用，而非独立检索记忆。

Result: 在BEAM和StructMemEval基准测试中，使用开源和专有LLM backbone，MemLACE在同backbone比较中取得最高整体性能；在BEAM上端到端运行时间较最强反思记忆基线Hindsight减少66.6%。消融实验表明生命周期扩展和时序意识是性能提升的主要因素。

Conclusion: 显式建模文本证据的局部生命周期足以显著提升长期记忆推理能力，无需依赖全面的知识图谱或全局反思机制，证明了轻量级结构化记忆方法的有效性。

Abstract: Long-term LLM agents must preserve information across interactions while distinguishing repeated evidence, historical states, updates, and unresolved contradictions. Existing textual memory systems retrieve semantically relevant memories efficiently but often leave these relationships implicit, whereas richer structured approaches model them through global graphs, hierarchical abstractions, or reflection at greater complexity. We introduce MemoryLACE (MemLACE), a lightweight memory framework that explicitly models the lifecycle of textual evidence through sparse merge, supersession, and contradiction relations while preserving atomic natural-language memories and their provenance. Rather than retrieving memories independently, MemLACE reconstructs relation-aware evidence units that expose current, historical, supporting, and conflicting evidence for downstream reasoning. Across BEAM and StructMemEval, using open-weight and proprietary LLM backbones, MemLACE achieves the highest overall performance in same-backbone comparisons while reducing end-to-end runtime on BEAM by 66.6% relative to Hindsight, the strongest reported reflective-memory baseline. Ablation studies identify lifecycle expansion and temporal awareness as the principal contributors to these gains. Together, the results demonstrate that explicitly modeling the local lifecycle of textual evidence is sufficient to substantially improve long-term memory reasoning without requiring comprehensive knowledge graphs or global reflection.

</details>


### [125] [LLMs Learn Better In-Context from Rules than from Examples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03213)
*Xiang Fu,Seungmin Cho,Yukyung Lee,Najoung Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 大语言模型在基于规则描述（指令遵循）的上下文学习中表现通常优于基于示例（少样本提示）的学习，尤其在涉及代数抽象和计算的任务中；增加示例数量或在规则基础上添加示例并未带来显著增益，而指令微调进一步放大了规则学习的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型上下文中两种主要学习模式（规则描述 vs. 输入输出示例）的相对有效性，并分析模型属性与任务特性如何调节这种学习效能，以明确何种情境下哪种模式更优。

Method: 选取涵盖游戏、算术和语言推理等五个不同领域的任务，对比在相同底层任务下“规则”与“示例”两种学习模式的表现；进一步考察模型规模、指令微调状态以及任务类型（如是否涉及代数抽象或分布敏感性）对学习效果的调节作用。

Result: 模型从规则中学习通常比仅从示例中学习更可靠；在规则基础上增加示例或单纯增加示例数量未产生一致且显著的增益；指令微调增强了规则学习的优势，同时保留了基于示例的学习能力；基础模型中未发现示例学习具有特权效应，规则在代数任务中仍具优势；规则相对于示例的优势在涉及代数抽象和计算的任务中更大，而在需要分布敏感性或参数知识的任务中较小。

Conclusion: 规则驱动的上下文学习在大语言模型中普遍优于示例驱动的学习，特别是在需要抽象推理的任务中；指令微调强化了这一趋势，表明明确的任务描述比单纯的数据示例更能有效激发模型的泛化与推理能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit in-context learning capabilities, where they can learn new tasks from prompt contexts without weight updates. We compare the learning efficacies of two prominent modes of in-context learning: (1) learning from descriptions of rules (instruction following); and (2) learning from examples of input-output demonstrations (few-shot prompting). Through five learning tasks that cover diverse domains (games, arithmetic, linguistic inferences), we compare two modes of learning (rules vs. examples) specifying the same underlying task. We furthermore explore model and task properties that modulate the learning efficacies. We find that models generally learn more reliably from rules than from examples alone, and additional examples on top of rules or simply scaling up the number of examples do not lead to consistent and significant gains. Instruction tuning amplifies the benefit of rule-based learning while keeping example-based learning capacities intact. Surprisingly, we find no privileged effect of example-based learning in base models, and rules still lead to gains in algebraic task domains. Overall, the comparative efficacy of rules over examples is larger when the task recruits algebraic abstractions and computations, and smaller when the task requires distributional sensitivity and/or recruits parametric knowledge.

</details>


### [126] [SWIM: Student Writing Simulation via Proficiency-Conditioned Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03215)
*Heejin Do,Jakub Kontak,Mrinmaya Sachan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨了大语言模型（LLM）能否真实模拟不同水平的学生写作，提出了SWIM任务，并通过实验发现提示（prompting）方法控制力有限，监督微调（SFT）和强化学习（RL）能显著提升模型在不同写作特征上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于LLM的学生模拟研究日益增多，但LLM是否能在扩展写作中重现学生在内容发展、思想组织、词汇选择和语言使用等多维度上的差异，目前尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 提出SWIM（Student Writing sIMulation）任务，将学生写作模拟定义为基于能力水平的作文生成。评估了提示、监督微调（SFT）和强化学习（RL）三种方法，使用自动作文评分作为衡量模型与真实学生写作特征一致性的指标。

Result: 实验表明，提示方法对写作能力的控制有限，即使是强大的专有LLM也难以复现不同水平下的词汇、语法和组织变化。SFT显著改善了对齐效果，而结合提出的能力对齐奖励的RL方法在所有写作特征和作文提示上进一步提升了表现。

Conclusion: 明确的监督（如SFT和RL）比单纯提示能实现更强的写作特征对齐，但真实低水平写作的模拟仍然具有挑战性。

Abstract: Writing proficiency manifests in how students develop content, organize ideas, choose words, and use language. Despite growing interest in LLM-based student simulation, whether LLMs can reproduce such multidimensional variation in extended writing remains largely unexplored. In this work, we explore if language models can realistically simulate student writing, and introduce SWIM, a task that formulates Student Writing sIMulation as proficiency-conditioned essay generation. We evaluate prompting, supervised fine-tuning (SFT), and reinforcement learning (RL) methods for writing simulation using automated essay scoring as a measure of profile alignment. Extensive experiments reveal that prompting provides limited proficiency control, even for strong proprietary LLMs with rubric-grounded strategies. In particular, while models can adjust content-oriented traits, they struggle to reproduce the lexical, grammatical, and organizational variation in different proficiency levels. SFT substantially improves alignment, while RL with the proposed proficiency-alignment reward yields further gains across all writing traits and essay prompts. Our findings suggest that explicit supervision enables substantially stronger profile alignment than prompting alone, while authentic low-proficiency writing remains challenging to reproduce.

</details>


### [127] [The Analyst in the Prompt: Role, Retrieval, and Memory Biases in LLM Financial Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03218)
*Ahmed Asaad,Amr Mohamed,Yang Zhang,Omneya Abdelsalam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 大型语言模型（LLMs）在个性化回复时使用用户上下文（如记忆、档案和角色提示）可能会影响基于证据的判断。本研究通过3,575份SEC文件测试了12个LLMs，发现用户上下文溢出主要源于模型在不同角色下对相同证据的解读差异，而非检索不同证据。两种缓解策略（将投资者心态表达为用户档案而非助手角色，以及分离基于证据和个性化的输出）能减少但不能完全消除溢出，且效果因模型而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究大型语言模型在使用用户上下文进行个性化回复时，如何影响基于证据的判断，特别是在金融等高利益相关领域，因为决策往往依赖于对长而复杂文档的解读。

Method: 使用3,575份SEC filings（美国证券交易委员会文件）在12个大型语言模型上进行测试。比较角色条件检索、中性检索和记忆框架上下文，以区分证据选择效应和解释效应。测试两种缓解策略：将投资者心态表达为用户档案而非助手角色，以及分离基于证据和个性化的输出。

Result: 大多数用户上下文溢出源于模型在不同角色下对相同证据的解读差异，而非检索不同证据。两种缓解策略都能减少溢出，但都不能完全消除，且效果在不同模型间差异显著。

Conclusion: 用户上下文对LLMs基于证据判断的影响主要来自解释层面而非检索层面。虽然提出的缓解策略有效，但需要进一步研究以完全解决此问题，且不同模型的表现差异提示需要模型特定的优化方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly use user context such as memory, profiles, and role prompts to personalize their responses. This personalization can affect evidence-based judgment: the same evidence may lead to different conclusions under different user contexts. Finance provides a high-stakes setting to study this problem because decisions often depend on interpreting long and complex documents. We test this using 3,575 SEC filings across twelve LLMs. We compare persona-conditioned retrieval, neutral retrieval, and memory-framed context to separate the effect of evidence selection from the effect of interpretation. We find that most user-context spillover comes from how models interpret the same evidence under different roles, rather than from retrieving different evidence. We then test two simple mitigation strategies: expressing the same investor mindset as a user profile instead of an assistant role, and separating evidence-based and personalized outputs. Both reduce spillover, but neither removes it completely, and their effectiveness varies substantially across models.

</details>


### [128] [Counterfactual Fairness Audits of Multi-Step Clinical LLM Agents Require a Measured Per-Action Instability Floor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03221)
*Rohith Reddy Bellibaltu,Manpreet Singh,Deepak Parashar,Rahul Joshi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出反事实审计中的翻转率指标本身不可解释，因为临床智能体存在固有的不稳定性（基准翻转率），未考虑该基准的公平性评估无法作为差异证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的反事实审计方法通过计算翻转率来评估临床智能体是否存在人口学差异对待，但作者发现该指标忽略了模型自身的不稳定性，导致结果不可靠。

Method: 使用 FairMedAgent 评估框架，对同一条件重复运行多次（10 次/16 个场景），计算不同动作的基准翻转率，并通过多数投票聚合和零模拟分析不稳定性的来源。

Result: 发现基准翻转率约为 6.7%-8.7%，不同动作间不稳定性差异达 8 倍；多数投票可减少 39% 的不稳定性但无法完全消除；第二模型验证了该基准的普遍性。

Conclusion: 任何未附带每动作基准翻转率的反事实公平性评估都不能被视为差异证据，需建立包含基准校正的评估标准以确保结果可信。

Abstract: Counterfactual audits are the standard tool for checking whether a clinical agent treats demographically distinct but clinically identical patients differently. They report a flip rate: how often an action changes when only the patient descriptor changes. We show that this quantity is uninterpretable on its own. Re-running an identical condition ten times over sixteen vignettes (same narrative, same descriptor string, nothing varied) moved a clinical agent's action in 8.7% of outcome-vignette cells, and instability was heterogeneous across actions by a factor of eight, from 0.022 for ICU escalation to 0.179 for controlled-substance caution. No demographic contrast in our data was distinguishable from that floor. A second model gives a pooled floor of 6.7% and ranks the six actions almost identically (Spearman 0.94, exact p=0.017), so the floor is not one system's artefact. Majority-vote aggregation over five draws removes 39% of it and then flattens, and a null simulation attributes the residue to heterogeneous per-cell rates, so replication mitigates without eliminating. Any counterfactual fairness estimate reported without a per-action floor beside it therefore cannot be read as evidence of disparity. The measurements were taken with FairMedAgent, an evaluation harness for disparity in the actions of clinical LLM agents whose estimand, the within-range counterfactual flip rate, counts only flips between actions a published decision rule admits and a clinician has adjudicated. That estimand requires band adjudication, which is under way; no disparity result is claimed here. Each synthetic vignette runs a six-stage trajectory (five model-facing decisions around a deterministic environment step) under fixed-form conditions spanning race, sex, age, insurance, English proficiency, and their intersections. The harness, the floor protocol, and every analysis script are released.

</details>


### [129] [SGD-KV: Summarization Guided KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03235)
*Zeyu Liu,Woomin Song,Xuandi Fu,Sai Muralidhar Jayanthi,Vivek Govindan,Aram Galstyan,Sravan Babu Bodapati,Srikanth Ronanki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SGD-KV 是一种感知注意力头的 KV 缓存压缩框架，通过块摘要诊断任务识别并优先保留擅长分层信息聚合的注意力头，在保持长上下文推理性能的同时减少高达 75% 的内存使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 KV 缓存压缩技术通常依赖简单启发式方法，忽略了不同注意力头的功能差异，导致长上下文推理中存在严重内存瓶颈。

Method: 提出 SGD-KV 框架，利用新颖的块摘要诊断任务系统性地识别和优先处理专注于分层信息聚合的注意力头，基于注意力头的摘要分数分布策略性分配 KV 缓存预算。

Result: 在 Qwen2.5-7B-1M 和 Qwen3-32B 模型上的实验表明，SGD-KV 在长达 1M token 的上下文中实现了最先进的性能，同时减少高达 75% 的 KV 缓存内存使用。

Conclusion: 基于注意力头摘要分数分布策略性分配 KV 缓存预算，可为长上下文推理提供更优的效率 - 准确率权衡。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) face severe memory bottlenecks in long-context inference due to the linearly growing size of key-value (KV) caches. Existing KV cache compression techniques typically rely on simple heuristics, overlooking the distinct functional roles of different attention heads. We present SGD-KV (Summarization-Guided KV Cache Compression), a head-aware framework that leverages a novel chunk-summarization diagnostic task to systematically identify and prioritize attention heads specialized in hierarchical information aggregation. Experiments on Qwen2.5-7B-1M and Qwen3-32B across diverse long-context benchmarks demonstrate that SGD-KV achieves state-of-the-art performance with contexts up to 1M tokens, while reducing KV cache memory usage by up to 75%. Our findings show that strategically allocating the KV cache budget based on the summarization score distribution of attention heads yields a superior efficiency-accuracy trade-off for long-context inference.

</details>


### [130] [What Else Needs Fixing? Exploring Cost-Effective Test-Time Compute for Revision Propagation in Artifacts Generated Through Conversation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03254)
*Daisuke Kikuta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型（LLM）在对话生成工件中，如何根据用户指定的局部修改识别依赖关系并传播至全篇的能力，提出了新基准并评估了九种修订方法，发现基于并行采样的选择策略最具成本效益且能显著提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决LLM在迭代对话生成中，当用户仅指定局部修改时，模型难以自动识别相关依赖并将修改正确传播到工件所有受影响部分的问题，特别是当上下文和依赖关系嵌入在对话历史中时。

Method: 构建了一个针对该场景的新基准数据集，评估了包括顺序反思和并行采样变体在内的九种修订方法，使用了gpt-oss-20b/120b、gpt-5.4-mini和qwen3.5-9b/27b/122b等模型进行实验，并探索了具有成本效益的测试时计算策略。

Result: 基线方法的准确率在68.3%至93%之间；最具成本效益的方法是从三个并行样本中进行选择（采用基于LLM的选择或中位数选择），该方法将准确率提升了2.2%至9.7%。

Conclusion: 通过引入新基准和系统评估，证明了并行采样结合选择策略能有效提升LLM在复杂依赖关系下的修订传播能力，同时保持了计算成本的低廉，相关代码和数据集已开源。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) often help users generate artifacts through iterative cycles of generation and revision in conversation. A challenge here is that, when users specify only a local change during revision, LLMs must instead identify the relevant dependencies and propagate the revision to all affected parts of the artifact. This paper studies this ability of LLMs on conversationally generated artifacts, where the artifact context and its dependencies may be embedded in the conversation history. Toward practical use, we also explore cost-effective test-time compute for this new setting. Specifically, we introduce a new benchmark for this setting, and evaluate nine revision methods, including sequential reflection and parallel sampling variants, using gpt-oss-20b/120b, gpt-5.4-mini, and qwen3.5-9b/27b/122b on the benchmark. The results show that baselines achieve accuracies of 68.3--93%, and the most cost-effective method is selecting from three parallel samples using either LLM-based or medoid selection, which improves accuracy by 2.2--9.7%. Our code and dataset are available at https://github.com/ntt-dkiku/llm-revision-propagation.

</details>


### [131] [Contextual Tamil Spelling and Grammar Correction Using Progressively Fine-Tuned Sequence-to-Sequence Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03273)
*Karthikeyan A,Jaya Nirmala S,Sangeetha Sivanesan,Indhu R,Pranav Kumar,Bharat Jude Johnson,Vishnu Ram*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种端到端的序列到序列方法，通过四阶段渐进式训练策略微调mT5和mBART模型，有效解决了泰米尔语中复杂的上下文语法错误和跨词Sandhi（连声）规则问题，显著优于传统的基于规则或统计的方法及零-shot大语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 泰米尔语作为一种黏着语和低资源语言，具有丰富的动词形态、复杂的词界Sandhi规则和庞大的字符集。现有工作多集中于词级表面错误，无法可靠处理需要句子级理解的上下文错误（如主谓一致、时态一致性）和跨词Sandhi问题。

Method: 提出端到端序列到序列 formulation，在包含高达657,720对噪声 - 清洁泰米尔语句子（涵盖十类错误）的合成语料上微调mT5-small和mBART-50。采用四阶段渐进式训练计划，分别针对表面噪声、上下文语法、单点Sandhi和多点跨词Sandhi进行优化。

Result: 最佳模型mBART-50 v5在1000句平衡诊断集上达到69.3%的Top-1精确匹配准确率，其中Sandhi错误纠正率达87.5%，主谓一致纠正率达43.5%。渐进式训练显著提升了性能（主谓一致从1.0%升至52.5%，Sandhi从0%升至87.5%）。同时发现Sandhi召回率与身份准确率之间存在精度 - 召回权衡。相比之下，Tamil-LLaMA-7B-Instruct的零样本和少样本表现均未超越复制基线。

Conclusion: 针对特定错误类型的渐进式训练计划是提升泰米尔语拼写和语法纠正性能的关键。通用的泰米尔语指令模型若缺乏任务特定的监督，无法有效迁移至句子级纠正任务，且该研究揭示了Sandhi纠正中尚未被报道的精度 - 召回权衡现象。

Abstract: Tamil spell and grammar correction is challenging because Tamil is an agglutinative low-resource language with rich verbal morphology, complex sandhi (phonetic transformation) rules at word boundaries, and a script of 247 distinct letters. Prior work targets word-level surface errors with rule-based methods, statistical n-gram models, Minimum Edit Distance, or hybrid pipelines with a transformer re-ranker; such methods cannot reliably handle contextual errors - subject-verb agreement, tense consistency, or cross-word sandhi - which require sentence-level understanding. We propose an end-to-end sequence-to-sequence formulation and fine-tune mT5-small and mBART-50 on a synthetic corpus of up to 657,720 noisy-clean Tamil sentence pairs spanning ten error categories. Both backbones follow the same four-stage progressive schedule, each stage targeting one weakness: surface noise (v2), contextual grammar (v3), single-site sandhi (v4), and multi-site cross-word sandhi (v5). On a 1,000-sentence balanced diagnostic set verified disjoint from all training data, our best model, mBART-50 v5, reaches 69.3% top-1 exact-match accuracy, with 87.5% on sandhi and 43.5% on subject-verb agreement. The schedule is what produces these gains: subject-verb accuracy rises from 1.0% to 52.5% once contextual pairs are introduced, and sandhi from 0% to 87.5% once multi-site sandhi pairs are. We additionally quantify a precision-recall trade-off this literature has not reported: sandhi recall is paid for monotonically in identity accuracy. Finally, Tamil-LLaMA-7B-Instruct reaches 19.0% zero-shot and 24.7% with three demonstrations against a 20.0% copy baseline, showing that a Tamil-adapted instruction model does not transfer to specialised sentence-level correction without task-specific supervision.

</details>


### [132] [PACE: Towards Surfacing Hidden Conflicts in User Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03293)
*Yoojin Kim,Jihyoung Jang,Hyounghun Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为PACE的数据集和一个名为PaceMaker的多智能体框架，旨在解决个性化助手在评估用户请求是否与其当前情境（隐式知识库事实）冲突方面的能力不足问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的个性化助手研究主要集中在准确执行请求，而忽略了根据用户情境评估请求的适当性及处理冲突拒绝。此外，现有的冲突或安全检测依赖于显式提供的因素，而现实场景中往往涉及需要从知识库中检索的隐式因素。

Method: 1. 引入PACE数据集：将基于明确角色的用户请求与自我中心知识库（KB）事实配对，要求模型整合上下文证据来判断请求是否存在冲突。
2. 提出PaceMaker多智能体框架：通过专门化的智能体协调查询重构、多跳图遍历和冲突感知过滤，以检索具有上下文决定性的证据。

Result: 在PACE数据集上的实验评估了证据检索质量和冲突决策准确性，结果显示PaceMaker框架在各项指标上均一致优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过引入隐式检索设置和多智能体协作框架，有效提升了模型识别潜在约束和判断用户请求冲突的能力，弥补了现有研究在情境感知和隐式知识检索方面的不足。

Abstract: Personalized assistants should not only comply with user requests but also assess whether those requests are appropriate given the user's current circumstances. However, prior work has primarily focused on accurately executing requests, overlooking the need for assistants to account for context and engage in conflict-based refusal. Furthermore, while existing work on conflict or safety detection relies on explicitly provided factors, real-world scenarios often involve implicit factors that must be retrieved from a knowledge base (KB). To this end, we introduce Personalized Assistants for Conflict Evaluation (PACE), a dataset for evaluating whether models can identify latent constraints, expressed as egocentric knowledge or events, that render seemingly reasonable user requests inappropriate. PACE pairs user requests grounded in well-defined personas with egocentric KB facts, requiring models to integrate contextual evidence to determine whether a request is conflicting. This implicit retrieval setting hinders the direct association between user requests and conflict-inducing knowledge, making it difficult for existing models to identify relevant user-specific facts. To address this challenge, we further propose PaceMaker, a multi-agent framework in which specialized agents coordinate across query reformulation, multi-hop graph traversal, and conflict-aware filtering to retrieve contextually decisive evidence. Experiments on PACE evaluate both evidence retrieval quality and conflict decision accuracy, showing that PaceMaker consistently outperforms existing approaches.

</details>


### [133] [Decoupling Turn-Taking from Semantics: A Decoupled Data Approach for Finite-State-Machine-Based Full-Duplex Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03321)
*Yihang Li,Chenhui Chu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种解耦数据方法，通过从真实人机对话中学习轮次切换，从可配置的人机文本对话中塑造语义行为，解决了神经有限状态机（NFSM）框架因依赖合成数据而导致轮次切换不自然的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的NFSM框架虽然能以低成本实现全双工对话，但其依赖合成文本数据，导致大型语言模型（LLM）无法忠实模拟真实人类对话中细粒度的声学时间动态，从而限制了轮次切换的自然度。

Method: 1. 提出解耦数据方法：分别从真实人 - 人（HH）语音对话中学习轮次切换，从人机（HA）文本对话中塑造语义行为。2. 引入基于规则的事件引导数据转换方法：通过分类轮次切换事件并应用确定性映射规则，将HH语音对话序列化为FSM磁带，无需LLM生成的标注即可实现可扩展监督。3. 提出源感知校准（SAC）损失：联合校准状态转换标记的长尾分布，并将每个数据源导向其最擅长监督的能力。

Result: 实验表明，该方法显著提高了轮次切换的熟练度，同时恢复了基础LLM的语义能力。

Conclusion: 通过解耦数据源并引入规则化转换与源感知校准损失，有效克服了合成数据在模拟真实对话动态上的局限，实现了自然轮次切换与强大语义能力的兼顾。

Abstract: The Neural Finite State Machine (NFSM) framework offers a pragmatic path to full-duplex dialogue by serializing turn-taking control and response generation onto a single causal tape under the standard next-token prediction objective, thereby preserving semantic prowess at a low fine-tuning cost. However, its reliance on synthetic text data fundamentally limits turn-taking naturalness, as Large Language Models (LLMs) cannot faithfully simulate the fine-grained acoustic temporal dynamics of real human dialogues. In this work, we propose a decoupled data approach that learns turn-taking from real Human-Human (HH) spoken dialogues while shaping semantic behavior through configurable Human-Agent (HA) text dialogues. To operationalize this approach, we introduce a rule-based event-guided data transformation method that serializes HH spoken dialogues into FSM tapes by classifying turn-taking events and applying deterministic mapping rules, enabling scalable supervision without LLM-generated annotations. We further propose a Source-Aware Calibrated (SAC) Loss that jointly calibrates the long-tailed distribution of state transition tokens and channels each data source toward the capability it best supervises. Experiments show that our approach substantially improves turn-taking proficiency while recovering the foundation LLM's semantic capability. Our code and model are available at https://github.com/Liyht/def-fsm.

</details>


### [134] [Less Is Moral: A CHARMing Framework for Moral Foundations Detection in Endorsement Behaviour](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03330)
*Huixiang Fu,Marian-Andrei Rizoiu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CHARM框架，解决道德基础检测系统跨域泛化差、理由依据弱及依赖高成本LLM提示的问题，通过轻量微调LLM整合道德依据、理由对齐和仇恨言论信号，提升预测鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有道德基础检测系统存在跨领域泛化能力弱、缺乏理由依据支撑以及依赖高成本提示式大语言模型的问题，需要更鲁棒且低成本的解决方案。

Method: 构建基于轻量微调LLM的CHARM框架，整合MAC交叉注意力机制、理由对齐和极性感知仇恨言论信号，每个组件对应特定心理学构念，使用MFTC、MFRC、News训练池30%子样本及MFTCXplain监督数据。

Result: 域内AUC提升最高15.3%，在所有域外数据集的AUC和F1指标上均超越监督基线，应用于Twitter COVID-19话语分析显示道德价值对齐与在线认可行为强相关。

Conclusion: CHARM提供可扩展、低成本的道德检测工具，使大规模道德框架测量成为可能，为研究道德化虚假信息传播提供实用方法。

Abstract: Moral language plays a central role in shaping online endorsement and the diffusion of information, yet existing moral foundation detection systems often suffer from poor cross-domain generalization, weak rationale grounding, and reliance on costly prompting-based large language models (LLMs). We introduce CHARM, a MA\textbf{C}- and \textbf{H}ate-speech-\textbf{A}ware \textbf{R}ationale-aligned \textbf{M}oral foundation detection framework built on a lightweight fine-tuned LLM, which integrates complementary moral grounding, rationale alignment, and polarity-aware hate speech signals to support more robust and faithful moral prediction. Unlike prior dictionary-, fine-tune-, or prompt-based detectors, which decouple computation from psychological theory, CHARM is built so that each component -- MAC cross-attention, rationale alignment, and hate-speech modulation -- operationalizes a distinct psychological construct. Using a 30\% subsample of the MFTC, MFRC, and News training pools together with the richer supervision in MFTCXplain, CHARM improves AUC by up to 15.3\% in-domain, surpasses the supervised baselines on every out-of-domain dataset in both AUC and F1, and offers a scalable, low-cost alternative to prompting-based LLM detectors. We further apply CHARM to large-scale COVID-19 discourse on Twitter and show that moral value alignment is strongly associated with online endorsement behavior. By making moral framing measurable at scale, CHARM offers a practical tool for studying the spread of morally charged misinformation.

</details>


### [135] [FPCO-Dialog: A Multi-Turn False-Premise Benchmark for Correction and Cooperation in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03331)
*Jiayuan Ma,Yuqi Lu,Weiyang Guo,Chenrui Wang,Junyi Shu,Xuebo Liu,Min Zhang,Jing Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了 FPCO-Dialog 基准，用于评估视觉语言模型在持续错误前提下的纠正与合作行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估很少隔离模型在多轮对话中面对持续视觉错误前提时的响应行为。

Method: 构建包含 1,080 张图像和 10,800 个问答轮的 FPCO-Dialog 数据集，采用 10 轮对话协议，评估 20 个商业及开源 VLM，使用 CorrTP@K 指标和独立检测器进行评分。

Result: 揭示了不同模型在纠正倾向、逐轮动态及错误前提类型上的显著差异。

Conclusion: FPCO-Dialog 有效暴露了 VLM 在处理持续错误前提时的行为差异，相关资源已开源。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly deployed in multi-turn settings where users may describe visual content with incorrect assumptions. Yet existing evaluations rarely isolate how models respond when the same visually grounded false premise persists across dialogue turns. We introduce FPCO-Dialog, a benchmark for evaluating correction and cooperation behavior in VLMs under repeated false premises. FPCO-Dialog contains 1,080 images and 10,800 question turns, stratified by visual complexity, object category, and false-premise class, and uses a 10-turn protocol in which a correct dialogue prefix is followed by repeated false-premise referring expressions. We evaluate 20 commercial and open-source VLMs with a model-agnostic protocol and CorrTP@K, a correction-rate metric over false-premise turns, scored by two independent detectors. FPCO-Dialog reveals substantial and persistent cross-model differences in aggregate correction tendency, model-specific turn-wise dynamics, and systematic variation across false-premise types under the benchmark's substitution distribution. The dataset, evaluation protocol, model outputs, detector labels, and code are available.

</details>


### [136] [Accountable AI with Grounded, Faithful, Consistent, Actionable Rationales: A Case Study in Clinical Trial Matching with VERDICT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03366)
*Zikai Zhou,Yufei Jin,Yilin Xu,Yu-Chiang Wang,Chieh-Ju Chao,Monica S. Lam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出VERDICT，一种基于LLM的智能体，通过将决策任务转化为SMT逻辑约束，实现临床试验匹配中的可问责决策，在准确性和一致性上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在高风险决策任务中缺乏可问责性，其输出可能无依据、不完整或不忠实于决策过程，难以满足可验证理由、假设明确、政策一致性和关键条件分析的要求。

Method: 引入VERDICT系统，将决策任务、约束和政策转化为Satisfiability Modulo Theories（SMT）逻辑形式，利用SMT和MaxSMT求解器进行推理，确保政策一致性和决策可问责性，并通过自我忠实性测试验证关键条件变化对决策的影响。

Result: 在SIGIR 2016和TREC 2021数据集上，VERDICT在决策准确性上超越LLM-only和神经符号基线，实现政策应用的完全一致性，生成临床医生偏好的基于明确假设和关键条件的理由，并提升反事实自我忠实性。

Conclusion: VERDICT通过神经符号方法实现了可问责的AI决策，在临床试验匹配任务中证明了其在准确性、一致性和可解释性方面的优势，为高风险领域的可信AI应用提供了新范式。

Abstract: Accountability means a decision can be examined, justified, and contested. LLMs make this hard: fluent output may be ungrounded, incomplete, or unfaithful to the decision process. Achieving accountability requires verified rationales (how was the decision reached), assumptions (what was assumed rather than known), policy consistency (the same treatment for the same facts), and pivotal conditions (what would change the outcome). We introduce self-faithfulness as an automatic test of accountability: changing the pivotal conditions should change the decision.
  We examine accountable AI through clinical trial matching, a high-stakes task central to evidence-based medicine. Although LLM-based matchers match patients to trials reasonably accurately, they apply decision policies inconsistently and produce rationales that are unfaithful to their own decisions.
  We introduce VERDICT, an LLM-based agent that translates a decision task, its constraints, and its policy into Satisfiability Modulo Theories (SMT), then derives the decision with SMT and MaxSMT solvers -- so policies are applied consistently and decisions are accountable by construction.
  Across a SIGIR 2016-derived dataset and TREC 2021, VERDICT achieves the strongest decision accuracy among LLM-only and neurosymbolic baselines, applies policies with perfect consistency, and produces clinician-preferred rationales grounded in explicit assumptions and pivotal conditions, with improved counterfactual self-faithfulness.

</details>


### [137] [FrameBench:A Language Understanding Benchmark Based on Frame Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03370)
*Chihiro Yano,Ryohei Sasano*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了FrameBench基准测试，用于评估大语言模型在框架语义学中的隐式信息丰富能力，发现大型模型表现优于人类参考分数，而小型模型面临挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型在多种下游任务中表现出色，但尚不清楚它们是否能像人类一样在理解过程中进行隐式信息丰富。

Method: 基于框架语义学构建FrameBench基准测试，包含多项选择题，使用FrameNet风格资源和生成验证流程，通过母语者判断构建英语和日语数据集。

Result: 实验显示小型模型面临挑战，而多个大型模型的表现超过了人类参考分数。

Conclusion: FrameBench为评估模型在框架语义理解方面的能力提供了有效工具，并揭示了模型规模对性能的影响。

Abstract: In frame semantics, sentence comprehension is assumed to proceed by relating lexical meaning to background knowledge called semantic frames, thereby enabling readers to implicitly enrich the text with unstated information. Recent large language models (LLMs) have achieved strong performance across a wide range of downstream tasks. However, it remains unclear whether they can reproduce the kinds of implicit enrichment that humans naturally make during comprehension. To address this question, we introduce FrameBench, a benchmark grounded in frame semantics. FrameBench consists of multiple-choice questions that test whether models distinguish the frames evoked by the same verb across contexts. We construct the benchmark for English and Japanese using FrameNet-style resources and a generation-and-verification pipeline with native-speaker judgments. Our experiments on a diverse set of models reveal challenges for small models, while several large models surpass the human reference scores. We release the constructed FrameBench dataset and the code for dataset construction and evaluation at https://github.com/SasanoLab/FrameBench.

</details>


### [138] [Chiaroscuro for Emotions: A Contrastive Emotion Benchmark Grounded in Appraisal Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03394)
*Divyesh Bommana,Mohammad Saim,Tianyu Jiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CHIARO数据集，用于评估模型在对比情感推断任务上的表现，发现现有LLM和分类器表现不佳，但该数据集可作为训练信号提升情感识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感识别基准通常只预测文本中的单一情感，忽略了现实场景中同一事件可能引发不同人产生对立情感的情况。

Method: 构建包含1000个人工标注句子的CHIARO基准数据集，基于评价理论，每个场景描述一个因果触发事件，使一个人产生积极情感，另一个人产生消极情感，涵盖十类情感分类。

Result: 在七个前沿LLM和四个现成情感分类器的测试中，最强LLM的宏观F1值为67.3，远低于人类一致性水平，现有情感分类器表现接近随机猜测。

Conclusion: CHIARO不仅可作为评估基准，还可作为训练信号，与现有情感语料结合后，下游分类器在CHIARO及六个外部情感基准上均有提升，表明其是情感识别的补充信号。

Abstract: Emotion recognition benchmarks often predict one emotion per text, missing many real-world scenarios where two people arrive at opposing emotions from a single shared event. For example, a child kicks the seat in front of her in excitement while the passenger ahead grows angry. We introduce CHIARO, a 1,000 human-annotated sentence benchmark for contrastive emotion inference grounded in appraisal theory. Each scene describes one causal trigger eliciting a positive emotion in one person and a negative emotion in the other, drawn from a ten-class taxonomy. We benchmark seven frontier LLMs and four off-the-shelf emotion classifiers. The strongest LLM reaches 67.3 macro-F1, well below human agreement, while existing emotion classifiers score near chance. Beyond evaluation, CHIARO also serves as a training signal. When combined with an existing emotion corpus, the resulting downstream classifier improves on CHIARO itself and on six of ten external emotion benchmarks, which positions our dataset as a complementary signal for emotion recognition.

</details>


### [139] [TabScope: Question-Adaptive Scope Selection for Table Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03395)
*Yuxiang Wang,Junhao Gan,Jianzhong Qi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种问题自适应框架，通过动态选择局部化或全表推理来解决大语言模型在大型表格问答中性能下降的问题，并在WikiTQ和SLQA基准上验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在表格问答任务中表现出色，但随着表格规模增大，其准确率会下降，且这种下降在不同问题类型上表现不均，定位敏感型问题尤其受无关内容影响。

Method: 提出问题自适应框架，通过操作感知的表格分解构建问题特定的子表，并利用预测的问题类型决定采用局部化推理还是全表推理模式。

Result: 在WikiTQ和SLQA上的实验表明，局部化对查找和局部推理问题特别有效，而自适应选择局部化与全表推理能实现最佳整体性能。

Conclusion: 长表格问答不仅需要决定如何定位，还需要决定何时定位，自适应框架能有效提升大语言模型在长表格问答中的表现。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown strong performance on table question answering, yet their accuracy often degrades as table size increases. We find that this degradation is not uniform across question types. Localization-sensitive questions are particularly affected by irrelevant table content, while questions requiring broader evidence may still benefit from full-table reasoning. Based on this observation, we propose a question-adaptive framework that dynamically selects between localized and full-table reasoning. The framework constructs question-specific sub-tables through operation-aware table decomposition and uses the predicted question type to determine the appropriate reasoning mode. We further introduce silver reference sub-tables for evaluating evidence selection and construct SLQA, a benchmark based on real-world long tables. Experiments on WikiTQ and SLQA show that localization is particularly effective for lookup and local reasoning questions, while adaptive selection between localized and full-table reasoning achieves the best overall performance. These results highlight that long-table QA requires deciding not only how to localize, but also when to localize. Our code and datasets will be made available upon publication of the paper.

</details>


### [140] [To What Extent Do Large Language Models Understand Bangla Idioms?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03410)
*Mousumi Akter,Md. Faiyaz Abdullah Sayeedi,Nurul Labib Sayeedi,Swakkhar Shatabda*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个大规模孟加拉语习语基准数据集及合成多选题数据集，评估了多个大语言模型在习语释义、跨度检测和意义识别任务上的表现，发现不同模型在各任务中表现各异，为低资源语言习语理解研究提供了资源和分析参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 习语是自然语言的重要组成部分，反映文化细微差别，但对计算模型（尤其是低资源语言）构成独特挑战。目前缺乏针对孟加拉语习语的大规模基准数据集，限制了相关研究进展。

Method: 构建首个大规模孟加拉语习语基准数据集和合成多选题数据集；采用零样本和少样本提示策略，对多个大语言模型在三个习语相关任务（释义、习语跨度检测、意义识别）上进行综合评估。

Result: 模型性能存在显著差异，无单一模型在所有任务中 consistently 领先：Phi-4-mini-instruct 在释义任务表现最佳，Kimi-K2-32b-instruct 在跨度检测任务最优，Gemini-2.5-flash 在意义识别任务领先。

Conclusion: 所构建的数据集和分析结果为未来提升大语言模型对习语表达（尤其是孟加拉语及其他低资源语言）的理解能力提供了宝贵资源和研究指导。

Abstract: Idiomatic expressions are an integral part of natural language, reflecting cultural nuances and posing unique challenges for computational models, particularly in low-resource languages. In this paper, we present the first large-scale benchmark dataset of Bangla idioms, complemented by a synthetic multiple-choice question (MCQ) dataset for idiom meaning identification. We conduct a comprehensive evaluation of recent large language models (LLMs) across three idiom-related tasks: paraphrasing, idiom span detection, and meaning identification, leveraging zero-shot and few-shot prompting strategies. Our results reveal substantial variability in model performance, with no single LLM consistently outperforming others across all tasks. Notably, Phi-4-mini-instruct excels in paraphrasing, Kimi-K2-32b-instruct in span detection, and Gemini-2.5-flash in meaning identification. We believe that our datasets and analyses will provide valuable resources to guide future research in improving LLM comprehension of idiomatic expressions, particularly in Bangla and other low-resource languages.

</details>


### [141] [Lngram v2: Latent N-Gram Memory with Interpretable Discrete Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03426)
*Yunao Zheng,Bin Wen,Xiaojie Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Lngram v2 通过解耦路由数量、记忆维度与骨干网络宽度，并引入上下文感知分组查询注意力读取机制，实现了可扩展的独立记忆容量，显著降低了参数量和激活成本，同时保持或提升了语言建模性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决 Lngram v1 中记忆容量与骨干网络宽度耦合导致的可扩展性问题，降低高参数和激活成本，同时保留离散寻址的硬离散特性。

Method: 提出解耦路由数量、记忆维度与骨干宽度的架构，引入上下文感知分组查询注意力读取机制，采用零值 Sink 和反事实代理梯度提升读取选择性和路由可训练性。

Result: 在不同规模的视觉 - 语言模型上实验显示一致改进，成功扩展至 30B 参数模型；相比 Lngram v1 显著减少总参数和激活记忆参数，保持或提升性能；离散 ID 保留了连续隐藏状态的语义结构。

Conclusion: Lngram v2 是一种高效可扩展的潜在条件记忆机制，其离散地址为分析模型内部表示提供了结构化接口。

Abstract: Transformers lack a native lookup mechanism, requiring repeated dense computation to recognize and reuse local static patterns. Lngram v1 introduces tokenizer-independent conditional memory through discrete latent n-gram addressing, but its memory capacity is coupled with the backbone width, limiting scalability due to high parameter and activation costs. We propose Lngram v2, which decouples the number of routes, memory dimension, and backbone width, and introduces a context-aware grouped-query attention readout to scale memory capacity independently. A zero-value Sink and counterfactual surrogate gradients further improve readout selectivity and routing trainability while preserving hard discrete addressing. Experiments across vision--language models (VLMs) of different scales show consistent improvements, including successful scaling to a 30B-parameter model. Compared with Lngram v1, Lngram v2 substantially reduces both total and activated memory parameters while maintaining or improving language modeling performance. Further analysis shows that its discrete IDs preserve substantial semantic structure of continuous hidden states, enabling semantic recovery from IDs alone and stable ID--semantic associations across datasets. These results establish Lngram v2 as an efficient and scalable latent conditional memory mechanism whose discrete addresses also provide a structured interface for analyzing internal model representations.

</details>


### [142] [Random Attention: Rethinking KV Cache Eviction for Efficient Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03430)
*Heng Wang,Jielin Qiu,Wenting Zhao,Cheng Qian,Liangwei Yang,Jiawei Han,Heng Ji,Silvio Savarese,Shelby Heinecke,Huan Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出“随机注意力”（Random Attention）机制，发现在长链推理任务中，无需计算评分信号，仅需保留提示词（prompt）并在各注意力头内随机剔除令牌，即可在保持与最强现有方法相当性能的同时，将vLLM部署的吞吐量提升32-43%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在需要扩展推理的任务中表现优异，但长思维链导致KV缓存成为严重的内存瓶颈；现有压缩方法均依赖复杂的评分机制来选择保留哪些令牌，作者质疑这种评分信号的必要性。

Method: 提出“随机注意力”策略：固定保留提示词部分，对每个注意力头内的其余令牌进行均匀随机剔除，完全不计算任何选择评分信号；在四个模型和六个推理任务上进行实验，并与现有最强剔除器对比。

Result: Random Attention在性能上与最强 prior evictor 相当，但在vLLM部署中吞吐量高出32-43%；控制实验表明：1）提示词是缓存中最脆弱的部分，现有方法间的差异主要源于是否保留了提示词；2）推理轨迹通过文本重述和跨注意力头冗余实现自我保护，一旦提示词安全，随机剔除足以保留所需信息。

Conclusion: KV缓存压缩无需复杂评分机制，关键在于保护提示词并利用推理轨迹固有的冗余性；随机剔除策略简单高效，显著提升了推理吞吐量，挑战了现有基于评分的选择范式。

Abstract: Large language models achieve superior performance on tasks that require extended reasoning, but long chains of thought make the KV cache a severe memory bottleneck. Existing KV cache compression methods share one paradigm: score each cached token by some estimate of how much it will matter later, and keep the top-scoring ones. We show that the selection signal contributes almost nothing. Random Attention keeps the prompt and evicts uniformly at random within each attention head, computing no score at all; across four models and six reasoning tasks it matches the strongest prior evictor while serving 32-43% higher throughput than it in vLLM deployment. Controlled experiments explain this by showing that 1) the prompt is the fragile part of the cache, and most of the gap between selectors is just whether their selection signal happened to keep it; 2) the reasoning trace protects itself against eviction with redundancy at two levels, in the text (the model restates what it still needs as it works) and across attention heads (each keeps its own copy of the trace), so once the prompt is safe, a random draw retains enough copies of what the model still needs, and no score is required to pick them. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/SalesforceAIResearch/Random-Attention.

</details>


### [143] [Decoupled Analysis-Judging: An Automated Creativity Evaluator Using LLMs in Complex Multi-step Creativity Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03432)
*Xiangyu Wang,Jin Wu,Xiaoyu Li,Chanjin Zheng,Yifeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CreaEval框架，通过将LLM-as-a-Judge解耦为分析和评判两个阶段，解决创意任务评估中的偏见问题，在CGPST及经典创意任务上平均性能提升22.74%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-Judge在创意任务评估中存在冗长偏见和宽松偏见等问题，尤其在上下文关联和程序结构化任务(CGPS T)中，由于步骤间依赖、高度主观性和宽泛评分范围导致评估更不稳定和有偏见。现有方法要么依赖任务特定训练，要么直接应用LLM-as-a-Judge，都难以确保复杂情况下的可靠评估。

Method: 提出CreaEval框架，将LLM-as-a-Judge解耦为两个阶段：1)记忆增强分析(Memory-augmented Analysis)：SoT-LLM将多步响应转换为结构化评估证据，包含跨步记忆；2)基于证据的评判(Evidence-based Judging)：Judge-LLM使用提取的证据进行评判，不访问原始响应。

Result: 综合实验表明，CreaEval在CGPST和两个经典简单创意任务上，相比第二好的基线方法平均性能提升22.74%，证明了其泛化能力。

Conclusion: CreaEval通过解耦分析和评判过程，有效减少了LLM评估创意任务时的偏见，提高了评估的稳定性和可靠性，具有良好的泛化性能。

Abstract: Automated evaluation of creativity tasks remains challenging for LLM-as-a-Judge, as LLM is susceptible to biases such as verbosity bias and leniency bias. Such limitations are particularly evident in Contextually-Grounded and Procedurally-Structured Tasks (CGPST), a complex multi-step creativity task where inter-step dependencies, highly subjectivity, and wide scoring ranges lead to more unstable and biased judgments. Existing approaches either rely on task-specific training or directly apply LLM-as-a-Judge, both of which struggle to ensure reliable evaluation under such complexity. To bridge these gaps, we propose CreaEval, an automated creativity evaluator for CGPST that decouples typical LLM-as-a-Judge into analysis and judging. Correspondingly, CreaEval involves two critical phases: Memory-augmented Analysis, a SoT-LLM converts multi-step responses into structured evaluation evidence, incorporating cross-step memory; and Evidence-based Judging, a Judge-LLM uses the extracted evidence for judging without accessing raw responses. Comprehensive experiments show that CreaEval achieves an average performance improvement of 22.74% over the second-best baselines across CGPST and two classic simple creativity tasks, demonstrating its generalizability. The code is available at https://github.com/Jaong/CreaEval.

</details>


### [144] [When Retrieval Helps: Selective Retrieval for Single-Turn Mental-Health QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03454)
*Hyunseo Oh,Chong-Kwon Kim,Yoonhyuk Choi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了检索增强生成（RAG）在单轮心理健康问答中的非均匀效应，提出了一种轻量级选择性检索策略。实验表明，无条件检索虽提升了回答特异性，却降低了整体质量并引发安全隐患；而选择性检索能在低需求场景中保持闭卷表现，避免无条件检索带来的性能退化，证明检索激活应被视为一种安全敏感的控制决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG虽能提升大模型回答的特异性和依据性，但在涉及情感困扰、治疗担忧及安全敏感需求的心理健康问答场景中，其效果并非始终有益。现有系统缺乏对何时检索能带来帮助或造成损害的细粒度控制，亟需一种机制来平衡检索带来的增益与潜在风险。

Method: 基于三个草稿条件效用维度（心理教育需求、应对需求、回答特异性）及基于规则的安全触发器来量化检索需求；构建包含应对策略、心理教育及安全资源的紧凑可控指南语料库；在MentalChat16K数据集上使用QLoRA微调指令生成模型，并在CounselBench-Eval和CounselBench-Adv基准上对比闭卷、始终检索和选择性检索三种设置。

Result: 实验显示，在该领域中检索并非普遍有益：始终检索模式虽提高了回答特异性，但降低了整体质量并引入了额外的安全敏感失败案例；选择性检索则能在低需求场景中保留闭卷行为，同时避免了无条件检索导致的性能退化。

Conclusion: 检索激活在心理健康问答中是一个安全敏感的控制决策。采用轻量级选择性检索策略能有效调控检索带来的权衡，在保障安全的前提下优化回答质量，优于无条件检索或完全闭卷的极端策略。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) can improve the specificity and grounding of large language model responses, but its effect is not uniformly beneficial in single-turn mental-health question answering, where user queries often combine emotional distress, treatment concerns, and safety-sensitive needs.
