  1. We show an efficient algorithm to recover the locations of the bands $I_1,\ldots,I_n$ in $\hat{x}$ within $\tilde{O}(n+\sum_i |I_i|)$ samples and $\tilde{O}(n+\sum_i |I_i|)$ time. Furthermore, combining this with the reconstructing algorithm by Avron et al. provides an efficient interpolation algorithm within $\tilde{O}(n+\sum_i |I_i|)$ samples.
  2. We show that every $k$-Fourier-sparse signal $x$ admits a multiband approximation $z$ whose Fourier transform is of support size $|\mathrm{supp}(\hat{z})|=\tilde{O}(k^2)$. Furthermore, we show an interpolation algorithm for $k$-Fourier-sparse signals with $\tilde{O}(k^2)$ samples and $\tilde{O}(k^5)$ time.

</details>


### [252] [SparseStack Is an Optimal Oblivious Subspace Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02978)
*Diar Heidary*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文证明了完全独立的SparseStack草图是$d$维子空间的无意识子空间嵌入，其参数达到了Nelson和Nguyen (2013)的猜想最优值，并给出了基于矩界和算子理论的显式常数证明，该证明已在Lean 4中形式化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Nelson和Nguyen (2013)提出的关于SparseStack草图构造参数的猜想，即确定在最优行计数$m$和稀疏度$s$下，该构造能否作为具有特定失真$\epsilon$和失败概率$\delta$的无意识子空间嵌入，并提供具有显式常数的严格证明。

Method: 通过界定Gram误差的偶数矩进行证明。采用条件期望耦合技术，将符号one-hot列选择器替换为具有独立三点分布的向量。利用三点律的三维$L_2$空间性质，将乘法运算转化为$3 \times 3$ Jacobi矩阵，将$2q$阶矩表示为有限张量积上确定性算子的真空矩阵元。通过共享因子不等式（shared-factor inequality）对算子的三个等级带进行界定，并结合$2q$步展开和马尔可夫不等式完成论证，且未使用高斯比较方法。

Result: 证明了当$m = O((d+\log(1/\delta))/\epsilon^2)$且$s = O(\log(d/\delta)/\epsilon)$时，SparseStack草图是以失真$\epsilon$和失败概率$\delta$嵌入$d$维子空间的无意识子空间嵌入。该结果的行计数$m$根据下界是最优的，且提供了显式常数。该定理已通过Lean 4形式化验证。

Conclusion: 该研究证实了SparseStack草图在理论参数上的最优性，提供了一种不依赖高斯比较的有限维证明框架，并展示了在作者指导下利用AI系统开发证明及通过形式化验证工具确保正确性的可行性。

Abstract: The fully independent SparseStack sketch is a vertical stack of $s$ independent CountSketch matrices, scaled by $s^{-1/2}$, so that every column has exactly $s$ nonzero entries. We prove that it is an oblivious subspace embedding for $d$-dimensional subspaces with distortion $ε$ and failure probability $δ$ when $m = O((d+\log(1/δ))/ε^2)$ and $s = O(\log(d/δ)/ε)$, with explicit constants. These are the parameters conjectured by Nelson and Nguyen (FOCS 2013) for this construction; the row count is optimal by their lower bound. The proof bounds the even moments of the Gram error. A conditional-expectation coupling replaces each signed one-hot column selector by a vector with independent three-point entries, at the cost of a constant factor per moment order. The three-point law has a three-dimensional $L_2$ space, so multiplication by an entry is a $3 \times 3$ Jacobi matrix, and the $2q$-th moment becomes a vacuum matrix element of a deterministic operator on a finite tensor product, graded by total occupation. The operator has three grade bands, and we bound each band on the grade-$ν$ sector by $C(\sqrt{(d+ν+1)/m} + (d+ν+1)/m + (ν+1)/s)$ with $C = 3+\sqrt{2}$. The key step is a shared-factor inequality: each row block of the positive operator attached to one tensor slot is rank one with trace $d$, and the sum over $\ell$ slots sharing the same external factor has norm at most $d+\ell-1$. A $2q$-step expansion and Markov's inequality complete the argument, which is finite-dimensional and does not use Gaussian comparison. The theorem has been formally verified in Lean 4. The proof was developed with AI systems under the author's direction, as disclosed in the paper.

</details>


### [253] [Assortment and Procurement Design in Dual-Mode Content Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03285)
*Garud Iyengar,Yuanzhe Ma,Jay Sethuraman*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: We study assortment and procurement design for a digital content platform offering both ad-supported and subscription access. Users are heterogeneous in content preferences and ad tolerance and self-select between the two modes or an outside option. For a fixed common subscription price and ad load, the platform chooses assortment distributions specific to each user type and access mode, together with content-family-level buy-versus-rent decisions to maximize profit. Rental costs scale with realized consumption, whereas buying provides a reusable pool of titles whose cost depends on the largest induced requirement across user types and modes. We show that the resulting problem is NP-hard. We then develop a scalable approximation framework based on a candidate buy set, a relaxation of the procurement coupling, and a decomposition into linear programs with a single equality constraint. These subproblems are solved by dual bisection with cardinality-constrained assortment optimization, followed by restricted-master postprocessing to recover primal feasibility. The method yields computable optimality-gap bounds, an interpretable threshold-based procurement heuristic, and asymptotic optimality under proportional market scaling as market size and grid resolution increase. Numerical experiments show strong performance at moderate market scales and grid sizes.

</details>


### [254] [Improved algorithm for counting spanning trees by $\ell_1$-regularized resistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03574)
*Rong-Hua Li,Yichun Yang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于$\ell_1$-正则化电阻的新算法，用于近似计算图的生成树数量，其时间复杂度为$\widetilde O(m+n^{7/4}\eps^{-3/2})$，在$m\ge n^{7/6}$时优于现有最佳算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决近似计算图生成树数量这一基础问题，旨在突破现有算法在时间复杂度上的瓶颈，特别是针对边数较多（$m\ge n^{7/6}$）的图结构，提供比Chu等人（FOCS 2018）和Liu等人（FOCS 2026）更高效的解决方案。

Method: 引入新颖的$\ell_1$-正则化电阻（$\ell_1$-regularized resistance）概念，设计了简单高效的算法来计算该指标，并将其与Durfee等人（FOCS 2017）提出的行列式稀疏化（determinant sparsifier）框架相结合，以实现对生成树数量的近似。

Result: 算法在具有$n$个顶点和$m$条边的图上实现了$\widetilde O(m+n^{7/4}\eps^{-3/2})$的时间复杂度。当$m\ge n^{7/6}$时，该性能优于之前的$\widetilde O(m+n^{15/8}\eps^{-7/4})$和$\widetilde O(m^{1.5}\eps^{-1})$算法。

Conclusion: 通过结合$\ell_1$-正则化电阻与行列式稀疏化框架，成功构建了更高效的生成树计数近似算法，证明了新概念在提升图算法效率方面的有效性。

Abstract: We study the basic problem of approximating the number of spanning trees of a graph. We propose an algorithm that approximates the number of spanning trees in $\widetilde O(m+n^{7/4}\eps^{-3/2})$ time on a graph with $n$ vertices and $m$ edges. Our algorithm improves upon the previously best known $\widetilde O(m+n^{15/8}\eps^{-7/4})$ time algorithm by Chu, Gao, Peng, Sachdeva, Sawlani, and Wang [FOCS 2018] and the $\widetilde O(m^{1.5}\eps^{-1})$ time algorithm by Liu, Peng and Yang [FOCS 2026] when $m\ge n^{7/6}$. Notably, our algorithm is based on the novel concept of $\ell_1$-regularized resistance. We propose simple and efficient algorithms for computing $\ell_1$-regularized resistance and we show that they can be used to approximate the number of spanning trees by combining with the determinant sparsifier framework of Durfee, Peebles, Peng, and Rao [FOCS 2017].

</details>


### [255] [A PTAS for Non-Adaptive Stochastic Top-$k$ Sum under General Combinatorial Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03685)
*Yu Liu*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文针对固定维度的非负打包族，提出了一种非适应性选择算法，旨在最大化$k$个最大实现值的期望和。通过结合$\alpha$-近似最大和预言机与递减剩余搜索，并利用打包线性规划（LP）处理占用直方图或混合分位数特征，该研究实现了针对任意$k \ge 1$的多项式时间近似方案（PTAS），同时证明了在该设定下不存在完全多项式时间近似方案（FPTAS）或高效多项式时间近似方案（EPTAS）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机源于在独立非负离散随机变量中选择可行集以最大化前$k$个最大实现值期望和的问题，该目标广泛应用于团队招聘（雇佣$k$名工人）及$\ell$单位拍卖中的VCG福利计算。现有单一算法无法在所有成员族上实现常数近似比，因此需要开发一种通用的、针对固定维度打包族的近似方案。

Method: 主要方法是在固定维度$d=O(1)$的非负打包族设定下，利用具有二元系数的自然线性规划（LP）。算法核心包括：1) 给定$\alpha$-近似最大和预言机，采用递减剩余搜索策略；2) 枚举$n^{f(d,1/\eps)}$个重项；3) 根据$k$的大小（$O(1/\eps^2)$或$\Omega(1/\eps^2)$），分别通过打包LP实现占用直方图或三维混合分位数类型的特征签名，替代精确和方案。

Result: 研究结果表明，对于任意$k \ge 1$及任意固定维度$d$的打包族（包括二元一维和二维背包问题），该方案均能提供一个PTAS。此外，研究确立了该方案的理论边界：除非$P=NP$，否则不存在适用于所有此类族的FPTAS；除非$W[1]=FPT$，否则不存在EPTAS（以二维背包问题为例证）。

Conclusion: 结论指出，在打包设定下，所提出的方案作为通用保证本质上是最优的。虽然存在另一类“查询权重精确和”问题（如DAG路径和匹配）也允许PTAS且与固定维度打包族不可比，但$d$维打包分类法对于识别那些允许PTAS的族是一个有效的分类工具，而非对所有此类族的完全划分。

Abstract: We study non-adaptive selection of a feasible set $S$ so as to maximize the expected sum of the $k$ largest realized values among independent nonnegative discrete random variables. The same objective arises when hiring a team of $k$ workers or when computing VCG welfare in an $\ell$-unit auction. The main setting is a fixed-dimensional nonnegative packing family: the natural LP has $d=O(1)$ packing inequalities with binary coefficients. No single algorithm achieves a constant factor on every membership family (already at $k=1$). Given an $α$-approximate max-sum oracle, a decreasing surplus search yields ratio $α/((1+α)(1+\eps))$ for every $k\ge 1$ (cuts included). Every fixed-$d$ packing family already has a deterministic max-sum PTAS, hence inherits that constant. The same signatures that drive the exact-sum scheme---occupancy histograms when $k=O(1/\eps^2)$, and a three-dimensional mixture-quantile type when $k=Ω(1/\eps^2)$---are realized by a packing LP rather than by exact-sum, after enumerating $n^{f(d,1/\eps)}$ heavy items. The result is a PTAS for every $k\ge 1$ on every fixed-$d$ packing family, including binary one- and two-dimensional knapsack. In this packing setting the scheme is essentially optimal as a generic guarantee: there is no FPTAS that works for every such $\F$ unless $P=NP$, and no EPTAS unless $W[1]=FPT$ (two-dimensional knapsack is a witness, already at $k=1$). A separate boundary is query-weight exact-sum, which includes DAG paths and matchings and is incomparable with fixed-$d$ packing. That oracle also yields a PTAS for every $k$, so $d$-dimensional packing is a useful taxonomy, not a partition of every family that admits a PTAS.

</details>


### [256] [The 11/6 supremum of the Wang-Sitters rounding scheme for graph balancing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03890)
*Adam Y. Shavit*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文分析了 Wang 和 Sitters 提出的图平衡问题的 11/6 近似算法，发现该算法实际上是一组允许的执行方案，其近似比可以无限接近但无法达到 11/6，并确定了在不同大作业阈值 beta 下的最坏情况保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wang 和 Sitters 的 11/6 近似算法并非单一算法，而是一组允许的执行方案，需要明确该算法在不同参数设置下的精确性能界限，特别是大作业阈值 beta 对近似比的影响。

Method: 通过分析算法中各步骤的允许执行范围，确定在不同 beta 值下的最坏情况保证函数，并将分析范围从原有的 1/2 < beta < 1 扩展到 0 < beta <= 1/2。

Result: 在 1/2 < beta < 1 范围内，保证为 max{3/2 + beta/2, 5/2 - beta}；在 0 < beta <= 1/2 范围内，保证为 3/2 + (1-beta)floor(1/beta)，且当 beta 减小时无界。最优阈值为 2/3，保证在 1/2 处跳跃而非平滑退化。

Conclusion: 11/6 是约束所有允许运行的最小常数，但没有任何运行能达到该值。最坏情况比率在 (0,1) 内的每个阈值处都已知，且 2/3 是唯一最优阈值。

Abstract: Wang and Sitters' 11/6-approximation for graph balancing is not one algorithm but a set of permitted executions: Step 1 may return any feasible solution of the relaxation and Step 3 any of the many ways to match the remaining jobs into the slots the rounding opens. We determine exactly what that latitude permits: ratios arbitrarily close to 11/6, and none reaching it, so 11/6 is the least constant that bounds every permitted run, and no run attains it. We then determine the worst-case guarantee as a function of the big-job threshold beta, measured against the optimum itself. On Wang and Sitters' own range 1/2 < beta < 1 the guarantee is exactly max{3/2 + beta/2, 5/2 - beta}. We then extend the same eligibility rule to 0 < beta <= 1/2 -- outside the range they state, and where a big job's two shares can both reach the threshold, so Step 2 acquires a third choice -- and determine the guarantee there as well: exactly 3/2 + (1-beta)floor(1/beta), hence unbounded as beta falls. The worst-case ratio is therefore known at every threshold in (0,1), and attained at none. Consequently 2/3 is the unique optimal threshold, and the guarantee jumps at one half rather than degrading smoothly. A companion note asks what does not fix the constant.

</details>


### [257] [Batched Pandora's Box](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04059)
*Shaddin Dughmi,Yusuf Hakan Kalayci,Vasilis Livanos,Aditya Prasad*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文针对并行化随机搜索问题（特别是LLM推理扩展），提出并研究了Weitzman的Pandora's Box问题的批处理版本，通过线性规划松弛和随机/Pipage舍入方法，为可重用和不可重用场景提供了常数近似算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受众多可并行化随机搜索问题的启发，特别是LLM推理时的扩展问题，需要解决容量受限的批处理场景下的搜索优化问题。

Method: 1. 提出Pandora's Box问题的批处理版本，考虑设置成本和批量揭示奖励；2. 分析可重用和不可重用两种变体；3. 使用线性规划松弛结合随机舍入或Pipage舍入方法设计双标准近似算法。

Result: 1. 证明了大多数简单启发式方法无效；2. 形式化证明了传统意义下的近似NP难性；3. 在双标准近似框架下，为两种场景都获得了常数近似比的算法。

Conclusion: 通过放松问题允许双标准近似，成功为Pandora's Box的批处理版本设计了有效的近似算法，解决了LLM推理扩展等实际应用中的优化问题。

Abstract: Motivated by numerous parallelizable stochastic search problems, most notable and timely among them being LLM inference-time scaling, we propose and study batched versions of the Pandora's Box problem of Weitzman. In particular, boxes are opened in capacity-constrained batches, each batch has a setup cost, and all rewards in a batch are revealed together. We consider two different variants, motivated by different application environments: one where boxes are reusable (i.e., can provide multiple i.i.d.~samples) and another where they are not. For both variants we rule out most ``simple'' natural heuristics, and also formally prove NP-hardness of approximation in the traditional sense. We then relax the problem to allow bi-criteria approximations, with respect to both rewards and setup costs, where we exhibit constant approximation algorithms for both the reusable and non-reusable settings. This is obtained through a linear-programming relaxation of Pandora's Box problem, followed by randomized or Pipage rounding.

</details>


### [258] [Diffuse Gaussian Truncation For Deterministic Approximate Counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04079)
*Zihong Yi*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种针对稠密计数问题的确定性完全多项式时间近似方案（FPTAS），将已知算法的拟多项式时间复杂度降低至多项式时间。核心方法基于“高斯截断原理”，通过积分变换、项的抵消与重求和，以及大偏差率界限，实现了对Hafnian、积和式及零场Ising配分函数的高效近似，并给出了更精确的渐近公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在稠密图上计算Hafnian、积和式（Permanent）及零场Ising配分函数等计数问题的效率瓶颈。现有的基于零-free插值的确定性算法仅能达到拟多项式时间复杂度，作者旨在通过新算法将其提升至确定性FPTAS（多项式时间），同时改进相关渐近公式的误差界限（如将Cuckler-Kahn的$o(n)$误差改进为$O(1)$）。

Method: 采用统一的“高斯截断原理”（Gaussian truncation principle）。具体步骤包括：1) 将问题转化为固定整函数在高斯坐标上的积分；2) 抵消线性项并精确重求和二次项，使剩余项至少以三阶速度消失；3) 对小支撑集使用复伸缩（complex dilation）处理；4) 对大支撑集利用大偏差率（large-deviation rate）界限来控制重组后的尾部，使其超过子集的熵；5) 利用快于几何级数的截断误差衰减特性进行多项式规模的枚举。

Result: 1. 针对最小度至少为$(1/2+γ)n$且非零项在$[θ,1]$范围内的对称矩阵，给出了Hafnian的FPTAS；同样条件适用于积和式。2. 针对满足特定谱范数和元素界限的零对角实对称矩阵，给出了零场Ising配分函数的FPTAS，且无需单独的下特征值条件。3. 证明了$\log\mathrm{haf}(A)$和$Z(J)$的新渐近公式，其中Hafnian公式将无权重图类的误差从$o(n)$提升至$O_γ(1)$，并扩展至加权情形。

Conclusion: 通过高斯截断原理，成功为两类稠密计数问题构建了确定性FPTAS，显著优于现有的拟多项式时间算法。该方法不仅提供了高效的多项式时间近似算法，还通过精确的渐近分析改进了理论误差界限，证明了在处理具有小阶矩矩阵项的积分问题时，利用高阶项消失和大偏差界限是实现快速收敛的关键。

Abstract: We give deterministic FPTASes for two dense counting problems on which the known deterministic algorithms, based on zero-free interpolation, run in quasipolynomial time. For fixed $0<γ<1/2$ and $0<θ\leq1$, the first approximates $\mathrm{haf}(A)$ for a symmetric matrix $A$ when its support graph $G$ has minimum degree at least $(1/2+γ)n$ and its nonzero entries lie in $[θ,1]$. It also approximates permanents under the analogous bipartite condition, including full-support matrices in $[θ,1]$. For fixed $β>0$ and $0<κ\leq1$, the second approximates the zero-field Ising partition function $Z(J)$ for zero-diagonal real symmetric matrices $J$ satisfying $\max_{i,j}|J_{ij}|\leqβ/n$ and $λ_{\max}(J)\leq1-κ$. No separate lower-eigenvalue condition is imposed. We further prove $\log\mathrm{haf}(A)=h_A(G)-n/2+O_{γ,θ}(1)$ and $Z(J)=2^n\det(I-J)^{-1/2}(1+O_{β,κ}(1/n))$. Here $h_A(G)$ is the maximum weighted fractional-matching entropy. For unweighted graphs, the first formula improves the Cuckler--Kahn error from $o(n)$ to $O_γ(1)$ on the fixed-margin class and extends it to weights in $[θ,1]$.
  Both algorithms use a common Gaussian truncation principle. Each problem becomes an integral of a product of a fixed entire function over Gaussian coordinates, with possibly indefinite moment matrix entries of order $1/n$. Cancelling the linear term and exactly resumming the quadratic term leaves a coordinate remainder vanishing to order at least three. Complex dilation handles small supports. For large supports, we bound the recombined tail by a large-deviation rate that beats the entropy of the subsets. The truncation error is at most $(CR/n)^{R/2}+e^{-cn}$. This faster-than-geometric decay permits $R\log(en/R)=O(\log n+\log(1/ε))$ and hence polynomial enumeration.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [259] [Direct Satellite-to-Device Communications: From Cooperative Task Offloading to Non-Cooperative Access Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02955)
*Sai Huang,Wanli Ni,Ke Lv,Pengcheng Zhang,Yurui Zheng,Menghan Zhang,Zihui Gong,Zhiyong Feng*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向6G非地面网络的多功能直连卫星到设备（DS2D）系统，通过D3QN算法优化协作场景下的任务卸载，并利用Transformer模型实现非协作场景下的盲信号检测与自动调制分类，显著降低了延迟并提升了信号检测与分类精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决DS2D通信在实际部署中面临的双重挑战：一是协作场景下动态卫星信道条件（如多普勒频移、快衰落）及受限的计算资源；二是非协作场景下未经授权的卫星接入带来的频谱安全威胁。

Method: 1. 协作场景：集成信道估计模块与决斗双深度Q网络（D3QN），动态优化任务卸载策略。
2. 非协作场景：提出基于Transformer的模型，实现盲信号检测和自动调制分类（AMC）。

Result: 1. D3QN算法相比静态关联策略，平均延迟降低高达225%。
2. 信号检测模型对DS2D信号的平均存在检测概率达到90.5%。
3. AMC算法在不同信噪比下表现优异，低信噪比区域准确率较现有方法提升9.4%。

Conclusion: 所提出的多功能DS2D系统能有效应对动态信道与安全威胁挑战，显著提升了6G非地面网络中的连接性、边缘计算能力及频谱安全性。

Abstract: Direct satellite-to-device (DS2D) communication is emerging as a transformative paradigm for extending ubiquitous connectivity and edge computing capabilities to remote and underserved regions within 6G non-terrestrial networks. However, practical deployment faces dual critical challenges: i) dynamic satellite channel conditions (e.g., severe Doppler shifts, fast fading) and constrained satellite computing resources in cooperative scenarios; and ii) unauthorized satellite access introduces significant spectrum security threats in non-cooperative scenarios. To address these challenges, we propose a versatile DS2D system that supports cooperative task offloading and non-cooperative access monitoring. For cooperative DS2D communications, we integrate a channel estimation module with a dueling double deep Q-network (D3QN) to dynamically optimize task offloading strategy. For non-cooperative DS2D communications, we propose Transformer-based models to enable blind signal detection and automatic modulation classification (AMC). Simulation results show that: 1) The D3QN algorithm reduces average latency by up to 225\% compared to static association policies. 2) Our signal detection model achieves an average presence detection probability of 90.5\% for DS2D signals. 3) The proposed AMC algorithm achieves superior performance across different signal-to-noise ratios (SNRs), with a 9.4\% accuracy gain in low-SNR regimes compared to existing methods.

</details>


### [260] [Adaptive Beam Hopping and Power Control for Dual-Layer Over-the-Air Online Federated Learning in LEO Satellite Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03202)
*Zhendong Li,Shaojie Wang,Zhou Su,Zihao Zhang,Haixia Peng,Nan Cheng,Ying Wang,Wen Chen*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于近地轨道卫星网络的双层空中计算架构，用于支持在线联邦学习，通过深度强化学习联合优化波束跳变和功率控制，以最大化长期数据利用率并满足聚合精度要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低轨卫星网络中实现联邦学习面临数据新鲜度随时间衰减、卫星波束资源有限、传输功率受限以及端到端聚合失真约束等挑战，且问题具有组合优化和非凸特性，传统方法难以有效求解。

Method: 构建双层空中计算聚合架构，将问题建模为马尔可夫决策过程，并开发基于近端策略优化（PPO）的深度强化学习框架，联合优化自适应波束跳变和功率控制，采用均方误差感知奖励函数平衡数据利用率与聚合精度。

Result: 数值仿真结果表明，所提算法在满足均方误差约束的前提下，长期数据利用率和联邦学习收敛速度均优于其他基准方案。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了低轨卫星网络中在线联邦学习的资源调度难题，实现了数据新鲜度与聚合精度的平衡，为卫星边缘智能提供了可行方案。

Abstract: This paper investigates over-the-air (OTA) computation enabled online federated learning (FL) in low-Earth orbit (LEO) satellite networks. Specifically, we consider a dual-layer OTA aggregation architecture, where ground devices upload analog model updates to serving satellites via uplink OTA aggregation, and satellites forward the aggregated signals to a data processing center through the second round OTA aggregation. Then, we formulate a long-term data-utilization maximization problem in which devices continuously collect new data and untrained samples gradually lose freshness. The problem is subject to the satellite beam budget, transmit-power limit, and global mean squared error (MSE) constraint that governs end-to-end aggregation distortion. This yields a coupled mixed-integer nonlinear programming (MINLP) problem, involving tightly coupled discrete beam-hopping decisions and continuous power control. Due to the combinatorial action space and nonconvex constraints, the problem is NP-hard and computationally intractable. Furthermore, the time-varying satellite topology and dynamic data generation render it a sequential decision-making problem, necessitating adaptive online scheduling. To address these issues, we cast the problem as a Markov decision process and develop a proximal policy optimization (PPO)-based deep reinforcement learning framework that jointly optimizes adaptive beam hopping and power control, using an MSE-aware reward to balance data utilization and aggregation accuracy. Numerical simulation results verify that the proposed algorithm consistently outperforms other benchmark schemes, achieving superior long-term data utilization and faster FL convergence while satisfying the MSE requirement.

</details>


### [261] [Variational Probabilistic Quantization for Secret Key Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03205)
*Xinyang Li,Vlad C. Andrei,Peter J. Gu,Yiqi Chen,Ullrich J. Mönich,Holger Boche*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出变分概率量化（VPQ）框架，利用神经编码器将相关信源直接映射为离散密钥，通过变分对抗目标函数优化密钥率，并结合线性码偏移安全草图实现密钥协调，无需单独的隐私放大步骤，在性能上优于现有经典及学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的物理层安全密钥生成流程存在手工量化、事后隐私放大以及缺乏与密钥率直接相关的优化目标等缺陷，导致效率受限且无法端到端优化。

Method: 提出变分概率量化（VPQ），利用神经编码器将相关观测直接映射到离散密钥字母表；设计变分对抗目标函数，其熵、不匹配和泄露项分别对应单向密钥率的三项；采用线性码偏移安全草图进行密钥协调，省略独立的隐私放大步骤。

Result: 理论证明了VPQ损失函数是诱导信源单向密钥容量的下界，并推导了给定字母表大小和不匹配概率下最优最坏情况密钥率的闭式解；在高斯衰落信道实验中，VPQ相比经典基线及两种最新学习基线向相关窃听者泄露更少，且Reed-Solomon协调方案在预测的有限块长间隙内运行。

Conclusion: VPQ框架成功实现了从相关观测到离散密钥的端到端优化，理论保证了其逼近密钥容量，实验验证了其在降低信息泄露和协调效率方面优于现有方法，为物理层安全提供了新的学习范式。

Abstract: Secret key generation from correlated observations at Alice and Bob, in the presence of an eavesdropper Eve, underpins physical-layer security. Classical pipelines quantize by hand, amplify privacy afterwards, and optimize no objective tied to a key rate. We propose Variational Probabilistic Quantization (VPQ): neural encoders that map the correlated sources directly into a discrete key alphabet, trained by a variational adversarial objective whose entropy, mismatch, and leakage terms match the three terms of the one-way secret key rate. A linear code-offset secure sketch then reconciles the encoder outputs into an identical key without a separate privacy amplification step. We prove that the VPQ losses lower-bound the one-way secret key capacity of the induced source, and derive in closed form the optimal worst-case key rate over the source class of a given alphabet size and mismatch probability, attained by finite-field linear sketches. On Gaussian fading channels, VPQ leaks less to a correlated eavesdropper than one classical and two recent learning-based baselines, and Reed--Solomon reconciliation operates within the predicted finite-blocklength gap.

</details>


### [262] [A Joint Power-Privacy Control Framework for Decentralized Learning over Heterogeneous Wireless Multicasting Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03245)
*Amir Ziaeddini,Yauhen Yakimenka,Joerg Kliewer*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合功率控制与隐私保护的分布式学习框架，在无线多播网络中实现模型联合训练的同时控制隐私泄露，并通过功率分配策略平衡学习性能与隐私性，理论证明累积遗憾界为 O(logT)，实验验证了其在 CIFAR-10 数据集上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无线多播网络中进行分布式学习时，需要同时考虑通信约束（如不对称信道增益、异构发射功率）和隐私保护需求，现有方法难以在保证隐私的同时优化学习性能，因此需要一种能联合控制功率与隐私的框架。

Method: 提出分布式学习框架，使用行随机邻接矩阵表示通信网络以捕捉不对称信道增益和异构发射功率；通过显式功率分配策略将节点最大发射功率分配给模型系数和高斯噪声，实现差分隐私；理论分析证明算法的累积遗憾界。

Result: 理论证明算法达到 O(logT) 的累积遗憾界；在 CIFAR-10 数据集上的实验显示，在 IID 和非 IID 数据分布、不同隐私级别、客户端数量和图拓扑结构下均表现良好，性能优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的算法在现实无线通信约束下能有效平衡学习性能与隐私保护，通过功率控制策略实现了隐私泄露的可控性，实验验证了其在多种场景下的优越性和实用性。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a decentralized learning framework that incorporates both power control and privacy guarantees. Specifically, we enable a set of clients in a wireless multicast network to jointly train a common model while maintaining a prescribed per-iteration maximum privacy leakage level. The communication network is represented by a rowstochastic adjacency matrix, allowing us to capture asymmetric channel gains as well as heterogeneous maximum transmit power levels. Differential privacy is enforced through an explicit powersplitting strategy that allocates each node's limited maximum transmit power between model coefficients and injected Gaussian noise, thereby jointly controlling learning performance and privacy leakage. We further prove that the proposed algorithm achieves a cumulative regret bound of O(logT), whereTdenotes the time horizon. To evaluate the practical performance of our approach, we perform comprehensive experiments on the CIFAR-10 dataset under both IID and non-IID data distributions, considering different privacy levels, diverse numbers of clients, and various graph topologies. The results demonstrate strong performance across the considered settings and improved performance over existing methods, highlighting the effectiveness of the proposed algorithm under realistic wireless communication constraints.

</details>


### [263] [Iterative Semantic Decoding for Short Block Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03256)
*Jiafu Hao,Chentao Yue,Mingcheng Nie,Branka Vucetic,Yonghui Li*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对噪声无线信道的迭代增强语义接收机，利用短块码和语言模型协同工作，显著降低了误块率并保持了高文本质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决自然语言文本在噪声无线信道传输中的可靠性问题，传统短块码在低信噪比下表现不佳，需利用语义信息辅助解码以提升性能。

Method: 发送端采用字符级交织器对句子置换并分段编码；接收端设计包含信道解码器和语言模型的迭代解码器，利用去交织器分散突发错误，并通过语言模型去噪及反馈语义一致的字符至信道解码器进行迭代优化。

Result: 在SNLI语料库和AWGN信道上的仿真显示，相比传统短块码编码，该接收机获得了约1.5 dB的误块率（BLER）增益，且在信噪比超过1.0 dB时，BLEU和ROUGE评分均保持在99%以上。

Conclusion: 所提出的迭代增强语义接收机有效结合了信道编码与语义信息，显著提升了噪声环境下自然语言文本传输的鲁棒性和重建质量。

Abstract: This paper proposes an iteratively enhanced semantic receiver for natural-language text transmission over noisy wireless channels using multiple short block codes. At the transmitter, each sentence is permuted by a character-level interleaver, partitioned into segments, and independently encoded by short block codes. At the receiver, we develop an iterative decoder consisting of a channel decoder and a language model, where a de-interleaver between them disperses the burst decoding errors within each segment across the sentence. In each iteration, the language model denoises the channel decoding output, and the denoised characters verified to be consistent with the channel observations are fed back to the channel decoder as semantic information for the next iteration. Simulation results on the Stanford Natural Language Inference (SNLI) corpus over the additive white Gaussian noise (AWGN) channel show that the proposed receiver achieves approximately 1.5 dB block error rate (BLER) gain over conventional short-block coding, while maintaining BLEU and ROUGE scores above 99% at SNRs beyond 1.0 dB.

</details>


### [264] [Finite-SNR Closed Form for the Square Marčenko-Pastur MIMO Capacity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03356)
*Mohamed Akrout,Robert W. Heath*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文通过经典三角参数化方法解决了方形MIMO信道容量计算中水填充截距的数值求解问题，实现了信噪比和容量的显式表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在方形MIMO信道中，Marčenko-Pastur分布的支持集触及原点，导致水填充截距始终位于支持集内部，水填充水平只能通过数值方法求解，缺乏闭式解。

Method: 采用经典三角参数化方法对方形Marčenko-Pastur定律进行参数化，消除边缘奇异性，将移动的水填充截距转换为单一角度参数。

Result: 信噪比和容量成为该角度的显式函数，通过扫描角度即可追踪容量曲线，无需在每个工作点求解标量约束；同时获得了低信噪比下的闭式行为表达式。

Conclusion: 该参数化方法填补了方形MIMO信道容量计算的空白，提供了低信噪比下容量斜率与极限谱上边缘的明确关系。

Abstract: For the canonical independent and identically distributed (IID) single-user multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) channel with full channel state information at the transmitter (CSIT), the asymptotic capacity per receive antenna is a waterfilling integral over the Marčenko-Pastur law, whose shape depends only on the ratio of transmit to receive antennas. Closed forms are known for every ratio other than the square one, and there only above a finite signal-to-noise ratio (SNR). The square ratio behaves differently because the Marčenko-Pastur support reaches the origin. The waterfilling cutoff then stays strictly inside the support at every finite SNR, and the water level has been available only numerically. This letter closes that gap with a classical trigonometric parametrization of the square law, which removes the edge singularity and replaces the moving cutoff by a single angle. Both the SNR and the capacity become explicit functions of that angle, so the capacity curve is traced by sweeping the angle rather than by solving a scalar constraint at each operating point. The same parametrization delivers the low-SNR behavior in closed form, where the slope of capacity in SNR equals the upper edge of the limiting spectrum.

</details>


### [265] [TokenComSR: Task-Sensitivity-Guided Token Communication for Wireless Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03735)
*Ye Wang,Li Qiao,Zhen Gao,Hua Wang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出TokenComSR框架，针对资源受限无线边缘设备在带宽受限衰落信道下的图像传输问题，通过任务敏感功率分配（TSPA）和信噪比条件化令牌细化（TRM）模块，解决了传统分离编码的“悬崖效应”及现有深度联合信源信道编码（JSCC）无法保持块级结构的问题，显著提升了重建保真度和感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分离编码在无线图像传输中存在“悬崖效应”崩溃问题；现有的基于卷积神经网络的深度联合信源信道编码（JSCC）虽能缓解该问题，但通常无法保持块级（patch-level）结构，导致无法进行自适应的每令牌（per-token）功率分配，并限制了令牌域内的超分辨率（SR）补偿能力。

Method: 提出TokenComSR框架，包含两个核心模块：1）任务敏感功率分配（TSPA）模块，将训练阶段的任务敏感性估计蒸馏为推理阶段的令牌功率权重；2）信噪比条件化令牌细化（TRM）模块，在解码前估计信噪比条件化的残差以校正令牌域中的信道失真。该框架结合基于Swin Transformer的令牌收发器与接收端超分辨率模块。

Result: 仿真结果证实了TSPA和TRM模块的有效性，在重建保真度和感知质量方面，该方案均优于传统的分离编码方案及现有的JSCC-SR基线方法。

Conclusion: TokenComSR框架成功解决了资源受限无线图像传输中的结构保持与自适应功率分配难题，通过令牌域的处理机制有效提升了恶劣信道条件下的图像传输质量，为边缘设备的高效通信提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: For resource-constrained wireless edge devices over bandwidth-limited fading channels, wireless image transmission using traditional separate coding suffers from the cliff-effect collapse. Prevailing deep joint source-channel coding (JSCC) based on convolutional neural networks can mitigate this issue but usually fail to preserve patch-level structures, thereby preventing adaptive per-token power allocation and limiting token-domain compensation for super-resolution (SR). To address these challenges, we propose a token communication framework with SR (TokenComSR). Specifically, we conceive a task-sensitive power allocation (TSPA) module and a signal-to-noise ratio (SNR)-conditioned token refinement module (TRM). TSPA distills training estimates of task sensitivity into inference token power weights, while TRM estimates an SNR-conditioned residual to correct channel-induced distortion in the token domain before decoding. Building on TSPA and TRM, the proposed TokenComSR pairs a Swin Transformer-based token transceiver with a receiver-side SR module for resource-constrained wireless image transmission. Simulation results confirm the effectiveness of the proposed TSPA and TRM, demonstrating improvements over separate coding and JSCC-SR baselines in both reconstruction fidelity and perceptual quality.

</details>


### [266] [A Quantization Problem Posed by Adaptive Streaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03745)
*Yuriy A. Reznik*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文揭示了自适应码率（ABR）流媒体中编码阶梯（encoding ladder）的选择本质上是标量量化问题，但受限于客户端“左边缘”选择逻辑和单向质量损失度量。研究发现最优阶梯的质量间隙收敛率为 $Θ(1/n)$ 而非传统的 $Θ(1/n^2)$，并推导出基于带宽密度和质量 - 码率曲线的最优码率分布密度 $\sqrt{p\,Q'}$，据此提出了闭式设计的量化分位放置规则及经济最优阶梯规模公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决 ABR 流媒体中如何科学设计编码阶梯（即选择哪些码率版本）以最大化平均交付质量的问题。现有的经典量化理论（如 Panter-Dite 公式）假设平方误差和中心值重构，无法直接应用于 ABR 场景中客户端总是选择带宽可支撑的最高码率（左边缘重构）且损失为单向质量差异的特殊约束。

Method: 将编码阶梯选择建模为具有特殊约束的标量量化问题：重构值固定在量化单元的左边缘，失真度量定义为单向质量损失。通过数学推导分析最优 $n$ 阶梯的质量间隙 $\mathcal{G}_n^*$ 的渐近行为，计算极限常数 $C_w$，并推导使该常数最小化的最优码率分布密度函数。

Result: 1. 最优 $n$ 阶梯的质量间隙衰减率为 $Θ(1/n)$，显著慢于经典理论的 $Θ(1/n^2)$。
2. 当 $n$ 很大时，$n\,\mathcal{G}_n^*$ 收敛于常数 $C_w=\frac{1}{2}(\int\!\sqrt{p\,Q'}\,\,d B)^2$。
3. 最优码率分布遵循密度 $\sqrt{p\,Q'}$（其中 $p$ 为带宽密度，$Q$ 为质量 - 码率曲线），取代了经典的 $p^{1/3}$ 分布。
4. 导出了闭式设计规则：基于分位数的阶梯放置、达到特定容忍度 $\varepsilon$ 所需的阶梯数量 $n(\varepsilon)\approx C_w/\varepsilon$，以及考虑运营成本 $\kappa$ 和质量定价 $\lambda$ 的经济最优阶梯规模 $n^*=\sqrt{λC_w/κ}$。

Conclusion: 该研究重构了 ABR 编码阶梯设计的理论基础，证明了在特定客户端逻辑下经典量化理论不再适用，并提供了基于带宽分布和内容质量特性的精确数学设计法则。该框架具有通用性，可扩展至不同分辨率、编解码器、感知质量指标及现代 Web 播放器的二维自适应逻辑设计中。

Abstract: In adaptive bitrate (ABR) streaming, the delivery technology behind most Internet video, each title is encoded at several bitrates, forming an \emph{encoding ladder} of \emph{renditions}. Each client plays the highest rendition that its network bandwidth can sustain. We show that choosing the ladder is a problem of \emph{scalar quantization} of the bandwidth distribution. However, this quantization problem is of an unusual kind: the client's logic pins each quantization cell's reproduction value to the cell's \emph{left edge}, and the distortion measure is a one-sided quality loss rather than a squared error. The constraint reshapes the classical theory. The \emph{quality gap} of a ladder is the distance between its average delivered quality and the \emph{quality limit} that infinitely many renditions would attain. For optimal $n$-rendition ladders the gap $\mathcal{G}_n^*$ decays as $Θ(1/n)$, not the textbook $Θ(1/n^2)$. Moreover, with large $n$, $n\,\mathcal{G}_n^*\to C_w=\frac{1}{2}(\int\!\sqrt{p\,Q'}\,\,d B)^2$, with the limit attained when the ladder's rates follow the density $\sqrt{p\,Q'}$, which replaces Panter--Dite's $p^{1/3}$; here $p$ is the bandwidth density and $Q$ the quality--rate curve of the content. The law yields closed-form design rules: quantile rung placement, the rendition count $n(\varepsilon)\approx C_w/\varepsilon$ needed to reach tolerance $\varepsilon$, and the economic ladder size $n^*=\sqrt{λC_w/κ}$ when a rendition costs $κ$ to operate and the quality gap is priced at $λ$. The same analysis extends to the design of ladders employing different video resolutions, codecs, perceptual quality metrics, and the two-dimensional adaptation logic of modern web players.

</details>


### [267] [Hierarchical Beam Training and Codebook Design for Movable Antenna-Assisted Near-Field Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03776)
*Meihui Liu,Qian Zhang,Xuejun Cheng,Yuhui Jiao,Yunxiao Li,Ju Liu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对6G近场通信中波束训练开销过大的问题，提出了一种基于可移动天线（MA）的分层波束训练策略及相应码本设计，通过联合角度 - 距离域的波束聚焦，在显著降低训练开销的同时实现了高波束增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着6G系统向高频段和大孔径阵列演进，近场范围迅速扩大，使得近场信道估计至关重要。然而，由于球面波的传播特性，传统的波束训练需要在角度和距离域进行联合搜索，导致难以承受的训练开销。

Method: 利用可移动天线（MA）技术灵活重构天线位置以 exploit 无线信道的空间变化，开发了一种分层波束训练策略。该策略设计了专用的分层码本，在联合角度 - 距离域形成聚焦波束，旨在最大化目标区域内的波束增益并抑制非目标区域的能量泄漏。

Result: 仿真结果证实，该方法相较于传统的固定位置天线（FPA）系统和远场波束训练方法，取得了显著的性能增益。

Conclusion: 基于MA辅助的近场系统，所提出的分层波束训练策略成功结合了低训练开销与高波束增益，有效解决了近场通信中因球面波特性导致的复杂搜索难题。

Abstract: As sixth-generation (6G) communication systems evolve toward higher frequency bands and larger array apertures, the near-field range expands rapidly, making near-field channel estimation increasingly important and challenging. Beam training has been recognized as an effective approach for channel state information (CSI) acquisition. However, because of the propagation characteristics of spherical waves, beam training needs to perform a joint search in the angle and distance domains, which results in unaffordable beam training overhead. By flexibly reconfiguring antenna positions, movable antenna (MA) technology can fully exploit the spatial variations of wireless channels and achieve more accurate beam focusing, thereby providing additional flexibility for efficient beam training design. Therefore, based on MA-assisted near-field systems, we develop a hierarchical beam training strategy that combines reduced training overhead with high beam gain and design a corresponding hierarchical codebook. This codebook forms focused beams over the joint angle-distance domain, maximizing beam gain within the target region while suppressing energy leakage into non-target regions. Simulation results confirm substantial performance gains of the method over the conventional fixed-position antenna (FPA) system and the far-field beam training method.

</details>


### [268] [On the Impact of Site-Specific Training for a Real-World 5G NR System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04004)
*Reinhard Wiesmayr,Nuri Berke Baytekin,Chris Dick,Christoph Studer*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了在真实5G NR测试床上，对三种接收器架构（全可训练神经网络、模型驱动神经网络、模型驱动）进行站点特定微调的效果，发现微调能显著提升前两种接收器性能，且在单/双层传输及跨时间测量中均有效；结合站点特定信道估计与迭代检测解码可实现最低误码率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多集中于全可训练神经接收器和单层传输，缺乏对多种接收器架构在真实双场景及长时间跨度下的站点特定微调效果的系统评估。

Method: 使用ETH Zurich符合标准的5G NR测试床进行双层层上行传输测量，跨越六个月以上，对三种接收器架构进行站点特定微调，并对比合成信道与站点特定测量信道的线性最小均方误差信道估计效果。

Result: 站点特定微调显著提升全可训练和模型驱动神经接收器性能，对模型驱动接收器提升有限；单一神经接收器联合微调可匹配分别微调的性能；微调效果在六个月后仍有效；结合站点特定信道估计与迭代检测解码实现最低误码率。

Conclusion: 站点特定微调是一种有效且持久的提升无线接收器性能的方法，尤其适用于神经接收器架构，结合站点特定信道估计可进一步优化系统性能，相关代码与数据已开源。

Abstract: Site-specific training can improve wireless receiver performance without increasing computational complexity. However, real-world results have so far focused on fully trainable neural receivers and single-layer transmissions. We study site-specific finetuning of three receiver architectures: fully trainable neural, model-driven neural, and model-based. We train and evaluate these receivers using new measurements from a standard-compliant 5G NR testbed at ETH Zurich with dual-layer uplink transmission, including measurement campaigns conducted more than six months apart. Our results show that site-specific finetuning (i) substantially improves fully trainable and model-driven neural receivers, while resulting in only marginal gains for the less tunable model-based receiver; (ii) enables a single neural receiver jointly finetuned for single- and dual-layer transmission to closely match receivers finetuned separately for each configuration; and (iii) remains effective across measurement campaigns separated by more than six months. We also investigate site-specific linear minimum mean-square error channel estimation using covariance matrices estimated from either synthetic channels or site-specific measurements. When combined with iterative detection and decoding, site-specific channel estimation achieves the lowest error rate observed in our datasets. Our finetuning code and measurement datasets are publicly available at https://github.com/IIP-Group/site_specific_training

</details>


### [269] [Synchronization Strings over the Optimal Alphabet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04122)
*Huibo Xu,Shi Fu,Youming Qiao,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文解决了同步字符串的三元情况，证明了三元字母表是构造同步字符串的最小常数大小，并提出了基于局部熵转移定理的构造方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Haeupler和Shahrasbi引入了同步字符串，后续工作证明四个符号足够，两个符号不足，但三元情况尚未解决。本文旨在确定同步字符串所需的最小字母表大小。

Method: 提出局部熵转移定理，使用Brinkhuis家族中的出现分支实例化该定理，结合删除球估计和非对称Lovasz局部引理来确保所有区间约束。

Result: 证明了每个长度都存在三元2001/2002-同步字符串，三元是精确的最小常数字母表大小。对于每个epsilon>226/227，都存在三元epsilon-同步字符串。

Conclusion: 本文框架不仅解决了三元情况，还产生了指数级数量的有效词、Las Vegas多项式时间构造、同步圆以及在极平方自由词类中的同步。

Abstract: Synchronization strings attach deterministic position labels to a stream that survive insertions and deletions. Haeupler and Shahrasbi introduced these objects, and subsequent work proved that four symbols suffice for some fixed parameter epsilon < 1, whereas two symbols cannot support arbitrarily long synchronization strings. We resolve the remaining ternary case: every length admits a ternary 2001/2002-synchronization string. Thus three is the exact minimum constant alphabet size. A computer-assisted refinement based on a larger 54-uniform family yields ternary epsilon-synchronization strings for every epsilon > 226/227.
  The previous four-symbol construction uses a ternary square-free backbone to exclude short repetitions and a fourth symbol to carry long-range synchronization marks. Our main technical contribution is a local-entropy transfer theorem: every square-free block-local source with positive conditional min-entropy supports synchronization strings with a fixed gap. We instantiate this theorem using occurrence-wise branching in a Brinkhuis family. Every outcome remains ternary and square-free, while every long interval retains linear conditional min-entropy after all choices outside it are exposed. A deletion-ball estimate converts this entropy into anti-concentration for high longest common subsequences, and an asymmetric Lovasz Local Lemma enforces all interval constraints simultaneously. The same framework also yields exponentially many valid words, a Las Vegas polynomial-time construction, synchronization circles, and synchronization within a class of extremal square-free words.

</details>

### [270] [Promise Systems of Equations over Magmas with Identity and over Algebras in Congruence Modular Varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03469)
*Nick Jamesson*

Main category: cs.CC

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限代数上承诺方程组的计算复杂性，推广了Larrauri等人的结果，获得了包含所有幺半群的代数类上的P-NP难二分性结果，以及同余模类代数上的二分性结果，并给出了同余模类代数上承诺方程组元问题的拟多项式时间算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究承诺方程组在有限代数上的计算复杂性，推广已有结果以获得更广泛的代数类上的复杂性分类。

Method: 推广Larrauri, Mottet和Živný的结果，研究包含所有幺半群的代数类和同余模类代数上的承诺方程组问题，并分析其元问题。

Result: 获得了包含所有幺半群的代数类上的P-NP难二分性结果，同余模类代数上的二分性结果，以及同余模类代数上承诺方程组元问题的拟多项式时间算法。

Conclusion: 承诺方程组在特定代数类上具有明确的计算复杂性分类，且其元问题可在拟多项式时间内解决。

Abstract: We study the computational complexity of solving promise systems of equations over finite algebras. Given two algebras $\mathbf{A}$ and $\mathbf{B}$ with a homomorphism from $\mathbf{A}$ to $\mathbf{B}$, the promise system of equations problem is to determine if an input system of equations has a solution in $\mathbf{A}$ or not even in $\mathbf{B}$. We generalize the results of Larrauri, Mottet, and Živný [ACM ToCL'26] to obtain a $\mathbf{P}-\mathbf{NP}$-hard dichotomy result for promise systems of equations over a class of algebras which contains all monoids, and a dichotomy result for promise systems of equations over algebras in a congruence modular variety. We then consider the metaproblem for promise systems of equations over algebras in a congruence modular variety: given finite algebras $\mathbf{A}$ and $\mathbf{B}$ such that $\mathbf{A}$ is in a congruence modular variety, we show there is a quasi-polynomial time algorithm for determining whether or not the associated promise system of equations problem is in $\mathbf{P}$.

</details>


### [271] [Random Garbage Separates XOR from Forward-Only Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03628)
*Khaled Elbassioni,Rishikesh Gajjala,Saurabh Ray*

Main category: cs.CC

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: We give exponential quantum query separations between the standard XOR interface and two forward-only interfaces that supply neither an adjoint nor an inverse oracle. Let $X=\F_2^n$, $N=|X|$, and $f_{h,r}(x)=(h(x),x,r_x)$, where $h:X\to X$ is promised to be either a permutation or a Simon two-to-one function, and $r$ is a fixed table of $n$-bit tags, unrestricted by the promise and reused on every query. The resulting problem is solvable with at most $n+2$ standard XOR queries, but has forward-erasing query complexity $Θ(\sqrt N)$. This answers affirmatively open question 11 in [Scott Aaronson. Open problems related to quantum query complexity. ACM Transactions on Quantum Computing, 2(4):14:1-14:9, 2021] . We also embed these instances into permutations. The detailed construction retains the copy of $x$ in each prescribed output, but for these promises that copy can be replaced by one bit that distinguishes the two inputs in every Simon pair. This gives a permutation domain of size $L=4N^2$ and a permutation problem with the same standard-query upper bound and forward-only in-place query complexity $Θ(\sqrt N)=Θ(L^{1/4})$. Both lower bounds remain valid with a clean coherent bypass. The common lower bound uses an analysis-only recording replacement. In the replacement computation, tracing out the fixed random tag table after $T$ calls gives a sum of positive-semidefinite operator contributions, each depending on $h$ at no more than $T$ addresses. On such a set, the restrictions induced by random permutations and random Simon functions differ only if the set contains a hidden Simon pair, an event of probability $O(T^2/N)$.

</details>


### [272] [The Head Complexity of Boolean Functions in Single-Layer Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04046)
*Rajmohan Rajaraman,Ravi Sundaram,Amanuel Tesfaye*

Main category: cs.CC

TL;DR: 本文研究了单层自注意力模型中计算函数所需的最小注意力头数（头复杂度），建立了精确的层级关系：$k$ 个头可计算 $k$ 比特奇偶校验但无法计算 $(k+1)$ 比特奇偶校验。研究证明了即使嵌入维度和数值精度无界，也无法替代注意力头的作用，并给出了通用布尔函数的近乎匹配的头数上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究单层自注意力机制的计算能力边界，特别是明确“注意力头数量”这一资源在计算布尔函数时的核心作用，厘清其与嵌入维度、数值精度等其他资源的关系。

Method: 通过“交替和阻碍”（alternating-sum obstruction）技术证明下界：在清除 softmax 分母后，生成的决策多项式中的每一项都至少缺失一个输入比特，导致其与奇偶校验函数的相关性为零。同时利用紧性定理（compactness theorem）和计数论证（counting argument）分别建立资源界限和通用函数的头数需求。

Result: 1. 确立了头复杂度下的精确层级：$k$ 个头能计算 $k$ 比特奇偶性，但不能计算 $(k+1)$ 比特奇偶性，该下界在无界嵌入维度和精度下依然成立。
2. 证明了紧性界限：任何可计算函数均可由受限于任务离散数据（头数、字母表大小、长度）的嵌入维度和精度实现，表明无界维度或精度无法替代头数。
3. 推导了通用二元函数的界限：$2^n$ 个头足以计算所有 $n$ 比特二元函数，而几乎所有此类函数需要 $Ω(2^n/n^2)$ 个头，上下界在多项式因子内匹配。

Conclusion: 研究完整刻画了单层注意力模型中布尔计算对注意力头数的需求，证明了注意力头是独立且不可替代的计算资源，无界的嵌入维度或数值精度无法弥补头数的不足，从而明确了该模型的计算能力边界。

Abstract: What can a single layer of self-attention compute? We study head complexity: the minimum number of attention heads required to compute a function in a one-layer attention-only model. We establish an exact hierarchy under this measure: $k$ heads compute $k$-bit parity but cannot compute $(k+1)$-bit parity.
  The lower bound is unconditional in the two resources a transformer might otherwise exploit; it holds at unbounded embedding dimension and unbounded numerical precision. The proof rests on an alternating-sum obstruction: after clearing the softmax denominators, every monomial in the resulting decision polynomial omits at least one of the $k+1$ input bits, forcing its correlation with parity to vanish. The same obstruction yields lower bounds for related tasks, including the well-studied multi-hop induction-head task.
  We also establish compactness bounds for embedding dimension and numerical precision. Specifically, a compactness theorem shows that any function computable at all can be computed with embedding dimension and precision bounded by the discrete data of the task, namely, head count, alphabet size, and length. Thus, potentially unbounded dimension or precision provably cannot substitute for heads. Finally, we derive nearly matching universal bounds for general binary functions: $2^n$ heads suffice to compute every $n$-bit binary function, with one head per monomial in its multilinear expansion, while a counting argument shows almost all such functions require $Ω(2^n/n^2)$ heads. This lower bound matches the upper bound to within a $\operatorname{poly}(n)$ factor, even when dimension and precision are unbounded. Together, these results characterize head requirements for Boolean computation in this model.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [273] [The Geometry of Ignorance: LLMs Know When to Temper Bayesian Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02959)
*Toni J. B. Liu,Jiajun Bao,Yizhou Liu,Gurbir Arora,Nicolas Boullé,Raphaël Sarfati,Christopher J. Earls*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现语言模型存在"无知方向"，即unembedding矩阵中的一个方向编码了训练语料的unigram分布，作为模型不确定时的贝叶斯先验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索语言模型在信息不足时的预测机制，理解模型如何处理不确定性以及先验知识在预测中的作用。

Method: 分析unembedding矩阵的几何结构，识别编码unigram分布的"无知方向"，通过投影最终预测状态得到先验加载因子λ，并验证其在不同模型中的表现及因果活性。

Result: 在四个模型家族(Llama、Qwen、Gemma、Pythia)中均发现"无知方向"；λ随上下文信息增加而下降；大模型在高上下文限制下对先验依赖更低；操纵λ可因果性地影响预测向unigram先验的偏移。

Conclusion: 语言模型的预测可分解为先验和似然的温度贝叶斯更新，"无知方向"提供了跨模型大小和家族的可比较校准，揭示了模型不确定性处理的几何-概率机制。

Abstract: What does a language model predict when it has few clues? The answer lurks in its unembedding geometry: a single direction of the unembedding matrix encodes the unigram distribution of the training corpus, which serves as the Bayesian prior the model falls back on when uncertain. This structure --- which we term the \emph{direction of ignorance} --- appears in all four model families examined (\texttt{Llama}, \texttt{Qwen}, \texttt{Gemma}, and \texttt{Pythia}), ranging from 0.4B to 405B parameters. Projecting the final prediction state onto this direction yields a per-token \emph{prior loading factor} $λ$, which, empirically, declines steadily as the context becomes more informative. Formally, the same projection decomposes the prediction state into two orthogonal vectors that correspond exactly to the two factors of a tempered Bayesian update: a unigram prior raised to the exponent $λ$ and a context-driven likelihood. This geometric-probabilistic interpretation calibrates $λ$, making it meaningfully comparable across model sizes and families, with larger models generally exhibiting lower prior reliance in the high-context limit. Finally, we show that the direction of ignorance is causally active: raising or lowering $λ$ at the final prediction state steers the prediction toward or away from the unigram prior in KL divergence.

</details>


### [274] [Equation Recast for Canonical Operator Learning Across Parametric PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02982)
*Qiyun Cheng,Valentin Duruisseaux,Cesar F. Clauser,Md Hossain Sahadath,Huihua Yang,Shaowu Pan,Nathaniel Ferraro,Anima Anandkumar,Wei Ji,Cristina Rea*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"equation recast"的方法，将参数化算子学习重构为单一规范算子的学习，通过解析推导参数引起的算子变化并将其吸收为有效源项，实现了跨新参数域的零样本预测，并在核聚变托卡马克模拟中成功统一了四种设备几何结构的电子温度数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决纯数据驱动参数化模型在广泛参数范围内学习解算子时需要大量输入函数和物理参数覆盖的问题，以及模型在训练分布外可能静默失效的风险。

Method: 将参数化算子学习重构为单一规范算子学习，从控制方程解析推导参数引起的算子变化并吸收为有效源项，使用收敛损失作为重铸迭代失败的内部警告信号，通过规范域映射实现多设备数据统一。

Result: 在 multi-parameter、nonlinear 和 singular PDE 设置中支持外推，整合稀疏异构数据集，在核聚变高保真托卡马克模拟中统一四种设备几何结构的电子温度数据。

Conclusion: Equation recast 提供了一种结合方程引导迁移、数据效率和可监控推理的可重用神经 PDE 求解器路径。

Abstract: Learning solution operators across broad parameter ranges can require substantial coverage of both input functions and physical parameters, particularly for purely data-driven parametric models. In addition, the resulting models may fail silently outside the training distribution. We introduce equation recast, which reformulates parametric operator learning as the learning of a single canonical operator. Parameter-induced operator variations are derived analytically from the governing equation and absorbed into effective sources, enabling zero-shot prediction across new parameter regimes. Across multi-parameter, nonlinear, and singular PDE settings, equation recast supports extrapolation, integrates sparse heterogeneous datasets in a shared canonical representation, and uses loss of convergence as an internal warning signal for failure of the recast iteration. In high-fidelity tokamak simulations for nuclear fusion, the framework unifies electron-temperature data across four device geometries through canonical-domain mapping within one jointly trained operator. Equation recast provides a route toward reusable neural PDE solvers combining equation-guided transfer, data efficiency, and monitorable inference.

</details>


### [275] [From Euclidean to Graph-Structured Data: A Survey of Collaborative Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02984)
*Rémi Bourgerie,Šarūnas Girdzijauskas,Viktoria Fodor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了从欧几里得数据到图结构数据的协作学习（包括联邦学习和去中心化学习），旨在解决传统集中式机器学习在可扩展性和隐私方面的局限。文章回顾了欧几里得数据的基础原则，提出了图结构数据的分类体系、统计异质性特征及算法框架，并系统性地指出了该领域的开放挑战与未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的集中式机器学习面临可扩展性和隐私保护的根本性限制。虽然协作学习（如联邦学习）在欧几里得数据（如图像、文本）上已有广泛研究，但现有方法难以捕捉现实世界中广泛存在的图结构数据中的关系模式。鉴于图学习依赖的消息传递机制天然适合协作环境，目前亟需系统性地梳理和探索协作环境下图结构数据学习的机遇与挑战。

Method: 采用综述研究方法：首先回顾欧几里得数据协作学习的三个核心维度（学习有效性、效率和隐私保护）；随后将讨论扩展至图结构数据，构建图分布场景的分类体系，刻画相关的统计异质性，并制定标准化的问题定义和算法框架；最后系统识别该领域的开放挑战和潜在研究方向。

Result: 建立了一个从欧几里得数据到图结构数据的协作学习综合知识体系，提出了针对图分布场景的分类学和标准化问题公式，明确了不同场景下的统计异质性特征，并梳理了相应的算法框架。

Conclusion: 协作学习在图结构数据领域尚处于起步阶段，存在大量未探索的机遇与挑战。通过整合现有理论、分类场景及明确未来方向，本文为推动该新兴领域的发展提供了坚实的基础和清晰的路线图。

Abstract: The conventional approach to machine learning, that is, collecting data, training models, and performing inference in a single location, faces fundamental limitations, including scalability and privacy, that restrict its applicability. To address these challenges, recent research has explored collaborative learning approaches, including federated learning and decentralized learning, where individual agents perform training and inference locally, with limited collaboration. Most collaborative learning research focuses on Euclidean data with regular, grid-like structure (e.g., images, text). However, these approaches fail to capture the relational patterns in many real-world applications, best represented by graphs. Learning on graphs relies on message-passing mechanisms to propagate information between connected nodes, making it conceptually well-suited for collaborative environments where agents must exchange information. Yet, the opportunities and challenges of learning on graph-structured data in collaborative settings remain largely underexplored. This survey provides a comprehensive investigation of collaborative learning from Euclidean to graph-structured data, aiming to consolidate this emerging field. We begin by reviewing its foundational principles for Euclidean data, organizing them along three core dimensions: learning effectiveness, efficiency, and privacy preservation. We then extend the discussion to graph-structured data, introducing a taxonomy of graph distribution scenarios, characterizing associated statistical heterogeneities, and developing standardized problem formulations and algorithmic frameworks. Finally, we systematically identify open challenges and promising research directions.

</details>


### [276] [Modern Transformers Are Implicit Hybrids: From Functional Differentiation to Principled Hybrid Architecture Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02986)
*Runlin Shi,Bojian Yin,Guoqi Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于RoPE频率重要性和位置依赖性的头级功能分类法，发现全局位置带（GPBand），并据此设计了头级混合架构（HwH），通过无位置编码的全注意力（NoPE FA）处理全局检索、线性注意力（LA）处理局部位置建模，在保持语言建模能力的同时显著提升了零样本长上下文外推能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合全注意力（FA）与线性注意力（LA）架构日益流行，但其分配策略仍依赖启发式方法，缺乏基于证据的理论基础；现有行为探针无法构建完整的头级功能分类体系，且零样本长度外推失败的原因尚不明确。

Method: 提出两种干预指标：RoPE频率重要性分数（RFIS）衡量各频率对注意力分布的影响，RoPE位置依赖性（RPD）隔离旋转位置调制的影响；在Qwen3系列和Llama3.1模型上验证分类法，并基于发现设计头级混合架构（HwH），采用NoPE FA用于全局检索、LA用于局部位置建模。

Result: RFIS与RPD验证了检索头与位置头由显著的中低频带（全局位置带，GPBand）分隔的完整分类法；HwH在FA-to-LA比例低于1:3时，保留了强大的语言建模与常识推理能力，同时显著提升检索性能及零样本长上下文外推能力，优于Transformer、纯LA及层級混合基线。

Conclusion: 位置建模应仅局部操作，全局访问需通过位置无关检索实现；两类功能应以头粒度分配并依层特异性配置。HwH验证了该原则的有效性，表明基于原理的混合架构设计是未来基础模型的重要方向。

Abstract: Hybrid architectures combining Full Attention (FA) and Linear Attention (LA) are increasingly prominent, yet their allocation remains heuristic. We seek an evidence-grounded basis in head-level functional organization learned by RoPE-based Transformers. Behavioral probes do not yield a complete taxonomy, so we propose two intervention metrics: RoPE Frequency Importance Score (RFIS), measuring how each frequency affects a head's attention distribution, and RoPE Positional Dependence (RPD), isolating dependence on rotary positional modulation. On Qwen3-series models and Llama3.1, RFIS suggests and RPD verifies a complete taxonomy of retrieval and positional heads separated by a salient mid-low-frequency band. Controlled Transformers show that this boundary follows the training-length positional scale; we term it the Global Positional Band (GPBand). The analysis suggests a potential cause of zero-shot length-extrapolation failure and yields two principles: positional modeling should operate only locally, with global access through position-independent retrieval; and both functions should be assigned at head granularity with layer-specific allocation. We instantiate them in Head-wise Hybrid Architecture (HwH), using NoPE FA for global retrieval and LA for local positional modeling. With an FA-to-LA ratio below 1:3, HwH retains strong language modeling and commonsense reasoning while improving retrieval and substantially strengthening zero-shot long-context extrapolation over Transformer, LA, and a layer-wise hybrid baseline. Ablations validate both principles and component roles, highlighting principled hybrid architecture design as a promising route toward future foundation models.

</details>


### [277] [Tail-Likelihood Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02987)
*Shrinivas Ramasubramanian,Daman Arora,Fahim Tajwar,Guanning Zeng,Qingyang Wu,Zhongzhu Zhou,Chenfeng Xu,Haiwen Feng,Yuda Song,Aarti Singh,Ruslan Salakhutdinov,J. Andrew Bagnell,Jeff Schneider,Andrea Zanette*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TailRL算法，通过直接优化奖励分布的“尾部覆盖率”（即超过特定阈值的概率），解决了传统强化学习优化平均奖励时忽略稀有高回报轨迹的问题，从而在推理采样时获得更好性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习通常优化平均奖励，但这掩盖了策略分布的关键差异：两个策略可能有相同的均值，但产生稀有高回报轨迹的概率截然不同。随着训练和推理中采样次数的增加，保留高回报结果的概率质量至关重要，而现有方法未能直接优化这一覆盖能力。

Method: 提出Tail-Likelihood Reinforcement Learning (TailRL)。该方法将连续奖励转化为一系列二元成功事件（针对每个奖励阈值，计算策略超过该阈值的概率）。算法通过最大化超过随机选择奖励阈值的对数概率来优化，其梯度给予稀有高回报轨迹更高权重，可解释为Best-of-k梯度的混合。实现上仅需对优势函数（advantage function）进行简单修改，兼容现有RL流程。

Result: 在物体定位、迷宫导航、GUI接地和代码优化等多个任务中，TailRL能够利用稀有的高回报训练样本避免陷入次优解。实验表明，经TailRL训练的模型在推理阶段能通过增加采样次数获得更显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: TailRL通过直接优化奖励分布的上尾概率，有效解决了平均奖励优化的局限性。该方法不仅提升了模型利用高价值样本的能力，还显著增强了生成策略在大规模采样下的表现，为生成式策略的优化提供了新范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning typically optimizes average reward. For generative policies, the average can hide an important distinction: two policies can achieve the same mean reward while having very different chances of producing a rare but high-reward rollout. This matters as sampling increases during training and inference, since its benefit depends on retaining probability mass on high-reward outcomes. We propose to optimize this coverage directly. Rather than considering only expected reward, we consider all of its upper tails: for each reward threshold, how likely is the policy to exceed it? This turns a continuous reward into a family of binary success events. We introduce Tail-Likelihood Reinforcement Learning (TailRL), which maximizes the log-probability of exceeding a randomly chosen reward threshold. Its gradient gives more weight to rare, high-reward rollouts and can be interpreted as a mixture of Best-of-(k) gradients. TailRL requires only a simple modification to the advantage function, making it compatible with existing reinforcement learning pipelines. Across object localization, maze navigation, GUI grounding, and code optimization, TailRL leverages rare high-reward training samples to avoid suboptimal solutions and yields models that benefit more from additional samples at inference time.

</details>


### [278] [Neural-Network Maxent: a general extension with learned nonlinearity, applied to time-series for Desert Locust distribution modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03603)
*Alessandro Grassi,Edoardo Kimani Bellotto,Wassim El Azami,Sabrina Outmani,Maximilien Houel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RNN Maxent模型，利用门控循环单元（GRU）替代传统Maxent的手动特征变换，以捕捉环境变量中的非线性时序关系，显著提升了沙漠蝗虫栖息地预测的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Maxent模型依赖线性组合和手动选择的特征变换，将时间序列协变量扁平化处理，丢弃了关键的时序结构信息，难以有效捕捉降水、土壤湿度等生态监测数据中快速演变的非线性动态关系，限制了其对沙漠蝗虫等破坏性害虫繁殖动态的预测能力。

Method: 提出RNN Maxent框架，保留Maxent的仅存在数据统计基础、背景归一化和概率校准特性，但将固定特征字典替换为端到端训练的门控循环单元（GRU）神经网络。该模型利用ERA5 Land、MODIS和Sentinel 3-derived的50天环境时间序列数据，并在协变量与存在记录之间设置7天间隙以实现预测行为。

Result: 在沙漠蝗虫适宜栖息地映射任务中，RNN Maxent相比标准Maxent在各项指标上均有显著提升：ROC AUC从0.792提升至0.862（标准差0.036），F1分数从0.590提升至0.671（标准差0.056）。

Conclusion: 通过引入深度学习架构学习数据中的非线性时序特征，RNN Maxent克服了传统Maxent在处理动态环境变量时的局限性，为生态监测和害虫预警提供了更精准的建模工具。

Abstract: Species Distribution Modelling (SDM) is essential for understanding how environmental conditions shape biodiversity, particularly for destructive pests such as the Desert Locust (Schistocerca gregaria), whose breeding dynamics are tightly coupled to rapidly evolving environmental conditions. Maxent has become the dominant method for presence-only data, but its reliance on a linear combination of hand chosen feature transforms limits its ability to capture the nonlinear, temporal relationships common in ecological monitoring, where covariates such as precipitation, soil moisture, and vegetation indices evolve meaningfully over time. Standard implementations flatten time-series covariates into independent features, discarding sequential structure that carries critical signal. We introduce RNN Maxent, an extension of the Maxent framework that replaces the fixed feature dictionary with a neural network, specifically a Gated Recurrent Unit (GRU), trained end to end via backpropagation. The approach preserves Maxent's presence only statistical foundations, background normalization, and probability calibration, differing only in that the nonlinearity is learned from data rather than fixed in advance. We apply RNN Maxent to map suitable habitat for the Desert Locust using 50 day environmental time series derived from ERA5 Land, MODIS, and Sentinel 3, maintaining a 7 day gap between covariates and presence records to yield forecasting behavior. Compared against standard Maxent, RNN Maxent improves performance across metrics (ROC AUC 0.862 std 0.036 vs. 0.792; F1 0.671 std 0.056 vs. 0.590).

</details>


### [279] [Mesh-Native Physics-Informed Graph Surrogates for TCAD-in-the-Loop Design Space Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02988)
*Leonid Popryho,Ayoub Sadeghi,Inna Partin-Vaisband*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于物理信息的图注意力网络（GAT）代理模型，直接作用于TCAD四面体网格，预测漂移 - 扩散系统中的电势及准费米能级。该模型结合了数据损失与有限体积电流连续性残差，具备尺寸泛化能力，并通过深度集成的不确定性驱动主动学习循环。在多大 FinFET 器件上的基准测试显示，其精度接近 Sentaurus Device，但推理速度快数个数量级，实现了大规模器件设计空间的高效探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真 TCAD 漂移 - 扩散模拟计算成本高昂，尤其在 3D 结构中随网格复杂度急剧增加，限制了多目标设计空间探索；现有机器学习代理模型仅映射固定设计参数到少数标量指标，丢弃了底层物理信息且缺乏跨器件几何和家族的泛化能力。

Method: 提出一种物理信息的图注意力网络（GAT）代理模型，直接在四面体 TCAD 网格上操作，预测每个网格节点的电势、电子和空穴准费米能级。训练结合数据损失与有限体积电流连续性残差，将载流子输运物理嵌入目标函数；利用图结构实现尺寸泛化，并通过深度集成的逐节点不确定性驱动主动学习循环筛选候选设计。

Result: 在多大 FinFET 器件上与 Sentaurus Device 基准测试表明，该代理模型以亚伏特级每场 RMSE 复现三个漂移 - 扩散场，每设计吞吐量比全模拟器高数个数量级；在大型多 Fin 阵列上，推理时间仍低于 1 秒/器件，而直接模拟则因耗时过长不可行。

Conclusion: 该物理信息 GAT 代理模型成功克服了传统 TCAD 模拟的计算瓶颈和现有机器学习模型的泛化局限，通过嵌入物理约束和图结构泛化能力，实现了跨器件尺度的高效、高精度设计空间探索，为复杂 FinFET 器件优化提供了可行工具。

Abstract: High-fidelity TCAD simulation of drift-diffusion transport remains the workhorse of emerging FinFET device design, but it is computationally expensive, especially for 3D structures where runtime escalates steeply with mesh complexity. This sharply limits multi-objective design space exploration. Existing machine-learning surrogates map a fixed set of design parameters to a few scalar device metrics, discarding the underlying physics and losing transferability across device geometries and families. A physics-informed graph attention network (GAT) surrogate is proposed. It operates directly on the tetrahedral TCAD mesh and predicts, at every mesh node, the electrostatic potential together with the electron and hole quasi-Fermi levels, the fundamental unknowns of the drift-diffusion system. Training combines a data loss with finite-volume current-continuity residuals, embedding carrier-transport physics into the objective. Operating on the mesh as a graph, the surrogate inherits size generalization: a model trained on few-fin meshes applies unchanged to substantially larger arrays, bounded at inference only by GPU memory. Per-node uncertainty from a deep ensemble drives an active-learning loop that screens large candidate pools in seconds and forwards only the most informative designs for full simulation. Benchmarked against Sentaurus Device on multi-fin tri-gate FinFETs, the surrogate reproduces the three drift-diffusion fields with sub-volt per-field RMSE and reaches a per-design throughput orders of magnitude higher than the full simulator. The advantage grows with device size: on large multi-fin arrays that are prohibitively slow to simulate directly, inference still completes in under a second per device, enabling Pareto-front exploration across device scales infeasible for direct TCAD sweeps.

</details>


### [280] [TRACE: Spatiotemporal Contact Memory Graph Network Simulator for Granular Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02991)
*Changjian Zhou,Negin Yousefpour,Jie Qi,Junfeng Fang,Guillermo A. Narsilio,Hans Petter Jostad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了TRACE，一种将相互作用历史直接存储在接触边上的图网络模拟器，通过边级记忆和物理结构化解码器，显著提升了颗粒动力学长时程模拟的精度与稳定性，同时在计算速度上远超传统物质点法（MPM）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的学习型图模拟器主要将时序信息存储在节点特征或节点级记忆中，难以有效保留颗粒运动中至关重要的接触历史（inter-granular contact history），特别是在接触形成、断裂和重排频繁发生的场景下，导致长时程模拟精度不足。

Method: 提出TRACE模型，其核心创新包括：1) 在接触边上维护持久记忆，利用基于注意力的消息传递和门控循环单元（GRU）进行更新；2) 引入边身份字典（edge-identity dictionary）以在接触图变化时保持记忆连续性；3) 采用物理结构化解码器预测法向和切向接触力，强制库仑摩擦极限并施加大小相等方向相反的内力；4) 训练策略采用单步预训练后接自回归展开微调。

Result: 在2D和3D颗粒柱坍塌基准测试中，TRACE生成了稳定且物理一致的长时程展开，精确复现了最终沉积几何形状和释放的动能。相比GNS和NMGNS，TRACE将长时程位置误差降低了31-62%，最终沉积误差降低了58-89%，且粒子间穿透率接近零。计算效率方面，相比MPM参考求解器，TRACE在2D和3D场景下分别实现了12.2倍和8.9倍的加速。

Conclusion: TRACE通过直接在边级存储交互历史并结合物理约束解码器，有效解决了颗粒动力学模拟中接触历史丢失的难题，在显著降低误差的同时保持了极高的计算效率，为高保真颗粒动力学模拟提供了一种高效且可靠的替代方案。

Abstract: Learned graph simulators provide an efficient alternative to high-fidelity solvers for granular dynamics. However, granular motion depends strongly on inter-granular contact history, which is difficult to preserve when particle contacts form, break, and rearrange. Existing simulators mainly store temporal information in node features or node-level memory. Here we introduce TRACE, a graph-network simulator that stores interaction history directly on contact edges. Each edge maintains a persistent memory updated by attention-based message passing and a gated recurrent unit, while an edge-identity dictionary preserves this memory as the contact graph changes. A physics-structured decoder predicts inter-granular normal and tangential contact forces, enforces the Coulomb friction limit, and applies equal-and-opposite internal forces. The model is trained with single-step pretraining followed by autoregressive rollout fine-tuning. We evaluate TRACE on 2D and 3D granular column-collapse benchmarks. In both cases, TRACE produces stable, physically consistent long-horizon rollouts, closely reproducing the final deposit geometry and the kinetic energy released during collapse. Compared with graph network simulator (GNS) and node-memory graph neural simulator (NMGNS), TRACE reduces long-rollout position error by 31-62% and final-deposit error by 58-89% across the two benchmarks, while using fewer parameters and maintaining near-zero particle interpenetration. TRACE also achieves 12.2$\times$ and 8.9$\times$ speedups over the material point method (MPM) reference solver in 2D and 3D, respectively. Our code is available at https://github.com/Data-Driven-Computational-Geotechnics/TRACE.

</details>


### [281] [No-Regret Bayesian Optimization with Finite-Library Input-Warped Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02993)
*Edvin Ketabati Augustinsson,Robert A. Bridges*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了有限库输入扭曲贝叶斯优化（FLIWBO）方法，通过从有限的平滑输入映射库中动态选择扭曲变换，解决了高斯过程贝叶斯优化（GP-BO）在输入几何结构不匹配（如对数缩放参数或局部峰值）时效率低下的问题，同时保留了高概率收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的GP-UCB收敛性保证依赖于固定核函数，但当原始坐标无法准确反映输入邻近性与目标值相似性之间的几何关系时（例如超参数对数缩放或存在局部峰值），样本效率会显著降低。虽然输入扭曲可以改善这一问题，但已知的理论证明要求核函数固定，导致自适应扭曲与理论保证之间存在矛盾。

Method: 提出FLIWBO算法，该算法允许通过任意依赖历史的规则，从一个有限的平滑输入映射库中选择最佳的输入扭曲变换。该方法在温和假设下，通过显式的$\sqrt{N_\varepsilon}$库大小代价，实现了输入几何结构的自适应调整，同时保持了高概率收敛保证。

Result: 在四个基准测试（扭曲合成目标函数、置信围栏陷阱、Fashion-MNIST超参数优化及20维多智能体系统设计）中，FLIWBO-UCB在几何结构错配情况下优于原始坐标GP-UCB；能够逃离即使使用Oracle扭曲的期望改进法（Expected Improvement）也会陷入的陷阱；恢复了大部分手动对数缩放带来的增益；并且在具有匹配遗憾保证的方法中表现领先。

Conclusion: FLIWBO成功弥合了自适应输入扭曲与理论收敛保证之间的鸿沟，证明了在昂贵且 noisy 的黑盒优化任务（如超参数优化和多智能体系统设计）中，通过有限库选择输入扭曲能显著提升样本效率并具备理论可行性。

Abstract: Gaussian-process Bayesian optimization (GP-BO) excels at black-box optimization of costly functions, e.g., hyperparameter optimization (HPO) and multi-agent system (MAS) design. Convergence-rate guarantees exist for select methods, notably GP upper confidence bound (GP-UCB), but require a fixed kernel. Critically, the kernel encodes how input proximity affects objective value similarity. When raw coordinates poorly match this geometry - as with log-scaled hyperparameters or localized peaks - input warping can greatly improve sample efficiency, yet known GP-UCB proofs require a fixed kernel. We propose Finite-Library Input-Warped Bayesian Optimization (FLIWBO), which selects warps from a finite library of smooth input maps by any history-dependent rule. It adapts the input geometry to accelerate learning while retaining high-probability convergence guarantees under mild hypotheses, with an explicit $\sqrt(N_\varepsilon)$ library-size cost. Controlled diagnostics show that finite-library warping repairs planted geometry mismatches and identify FLIWBO failure cases. Across four repeated benchmarks - warped synthetic objectives, a confidence-fence trap, and Fashion-MNIST HPO - FLIWBO-UCB beats raw-coordinate GP-UCB under misspecified geometry, escapes traps that defeat even oracle-warp expected improvement, and recovers much of the gain from manual log scaling, while leading the tested methods that admit a matching regret guarantee. A 20-dimensional MAS design study further shows feasibility under costly noisy evaluations. Code for experiments is available: https://github.com/edvin-ketabati/bogp-paper-experiments.

</details>


### [282] [Evaluating Graph Neural Networks for Change-Criticality Classification in Maritime Navigation Charts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02996)
*Abhishek Potnis,Jacob Arndt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用图神经网络（GNN）将电子海图（ENC）表示为图结构，通过图对分类任务自动识别海图更新对航行安全的关键性风险，实验证明该方法能有效提升分类性能并优化维护流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子海图（ENC）的维护具有挑战性，目前尚不清楚何种消息传递机制、架构配置及图表示方法最适合分类ENC中对象的变化，特别是区分这些变化对航行安全的关键性（critical）与非关键性（non-critical）风险。

Method: 将矢量导航数据集构建为图结构，其中空间对象作为节点，空间及语义关系作为边；将旧版和新版ENC数据集编码为一对图，构建图对分类问题；训练并评估多种GNN架构及模型配置，以分类ENC更新是否构成航行安全风险。

Result: 实验结果表明，基于图的表示方法显著改善了对ENC更新的分类效果，且该方法在处理由海事专家审核的ENC变化数据时表现良好。

Conclusion: 图神经网络为ENC维护提供了一种可扩展的自动化或辅助方案，能够有效提升对海图更新中航行安全风险的识别能力。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) are a class of neural networks suitable for learning on graph-structured data. Their application to spatial data is a natural extension, however its relatively unclear which message-passing operations, architectural configurations, and graph representation is best suited for classifying changes to objects in electronic navigational charts (ENCs)--geospatial vector datasets used for marine navigation. Maintaining these datasets is a challenge, and categorizing changes to objects in the ENC based on their significance to navigational safety is of particular importance. Here, we propose to represent these vector navigation datasets as a graph structure where the spatial objects serve as nodes and their spatial and semantic relationships form edges. We encode both the old ENC dataset and new ENC dataset into a pair of graphs and frame the task as a graph-pair classification problem. Building on this representation, we investigate the use of GNN architectures to classify whether the encoded graphs constitutes a critical or non-critical risk to navigational safety. We train and evaluate several GNN architectures and model configurations on ENC changes reviewed by maritime experts. Our results demonstrate that graph-based representations improve the classification of ENC updates, providing a scalable approach for automating or improving ENC maintenance workflows.

</details>


### [283] [Causal Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03003)
*Christopher Stith,Hossein Rahmani,Jesse C. Cresswell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 因果基础模型（CFMs）将基础模型范式引入因果推断，通过预训练神经网络使用上下文学习估计因果量，无需模型更新即可应用于新数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统因果推断需要为每个新问题定制流程（提出因果机制、选择估计器、训练），而机器学习已转向基础模型范式（一次大规模预训练，无需微调即可应用于新任务），本文旨在将这一范式引入因果推断领域。

Method: 介绍因果基础模型（CFMs）的概念，总结因果推断和机器学习的必要背景，并提供示例代码和Jupyter notebooks作为实践指导。

Result: 提出了CFMs框架，使预训练神经网络能够通过上下文学习在完全新的数据集上估计因果量（如平均处理效应），无需模型更新。

Conclusion: 本文为因果基础模型这一新兴领域提供了实用的入门指南，推动了因果推断向基础模型范式的转变。

Abstract: Causal inference is the practice of estimating the effect of a treatment or intervention from data. It traditionally requires a bespoke pipeline for every new problem: first proposing a causal mechanism, selecting a compatible estimator, and finally training it. Meanwhile, across diverse settings and modalities, much of machine learning has shifted to the paradigm of foundation models: networks pretrained once at scale and applied to new tasks without fine-tuning. Causal foundation models (CFMs) bring this paradigm to causal inference. CFMs are pretrained neural networks that estimate causal quantities, such as the average treatment effect, on entirely new datasets using in-context learning without requiring model updates. This work provides a practical introduction to this emerging area. We summarize the necessary background in causal inference and machine learning before discussing CFMs. Throughout, we include example code and Jupyter notebooks.

</details>


### [284] [Verify Before You Distill: Prompt-Level Teacher Gating for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02998)
*Zhiwei Zhang,Zechen Sun,Fei Zhao,Kang Peng,Bin Liang,Huayu Deng,Yao Hu,Kam-Fai Wong,Mu Chuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出教师门控在线策略蒸馏（TGOPD），通过在提示词级别验证教师可靠性，动态路由至密集蒸馏或验证器引导的GRPO，解决了传统OPD中教师错误置信导致误导更新的问题，显著提升了多领域训练效果及教师端GPU利用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的在线策略蒸馏（OPD）对所有提示词均匀应用监督，未检查教师模型的可靠性。由于反向KL散度的模式寻求特性，一个自信但错误的教师会产生强烈且误导性的更新。现有的分布代理（如熵或一致性）仅衡量不确定性或一致性，无法直接验证结果的正确性。

Method: 提出教师门控在线策略蒸馏（TGOPD），核心原则是在接受密集监督前在提示词级别验证教师可靠性。该方法利用少量验证器评分的教师探针来估计可靠性，并将每个提示词独家路由：若通过可靠性检查则进行密集OPD，否则转向基于验证器的GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization）。

Result: 在4B和35B规模的学生模型上，涵盖数学、代码和指令遵循任务，TGOPD在所有六个单领域设置中均优于Vanilla OPD，并在多领域训练下于七个基准测试中取得更高的平均分。此外，在4B单领域运行中，教师节点GPU利用率从9.8%提升至78.9%。

Conclusion: TGOPD通过引入提示词级别的可靠性验证机制，有效避免了错误教师信号的误导，不仅提升了模型在单领域及多领域场景下的性能，还通过利用闲置的教师计算资源进行可靠性估计，显著减少了异步OPD中的计算浪费，提高了硬件利用率。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) accelerates post-training by providing dense token-level supervision from a frozen teacher on the student's own rollouts. Vanilla OPD applies this supervision uniformly across prompts, without checking whether the teacher is reliable for each prompt. Because reverse KL is mode-seeking, a confidently wrong teacher can induce a strong yet misleading update. Distributional proxies, such as entropy or teacher-student likelihood agreement, measure uncertainty or agreement but do not directly verify outcome correctness. We introduce Teacher-Gated On-Policy Distillation (TGOPD), built on the principle that teacher reliability should be verified at the prompt level before dense supervision is admitted. TGOPD estimates reliability from a small set of verifier-scored teacher probes and routes each prompt exclusively to dense OPD when the reliability check passes or to verifier-grounded GRPO otherwise. Across 4B and 35B students in mathematics, code, and instruction following, TGOPD outperforms Vanilla OPD in all six single-domain settings and achieves higher seven-benchmark averages at both scales under multi-domain training. By using otherwise-idle teacher capacity for reliability estimation, TGOPD also reduces teacher-side compute waste in asynchronous OPD, increasing teacher-node GPU utilization from 9.8% to 78.9% in the measured 4B single-domain run.

</details>


### [285] [ObserverBench: Testing Mechanistic Estimates for Intervention and Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03026)
*Vijay Erramilli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ObserverBench基准框架，旨在评估机械可解释性中的内部估计器（观察者）是否足以指导干预、控制或安全任务。研究发现，估计精度高并不等同于决策损失低，两者需分开评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械可解释性常被用于指导激活引导、电路移除和安全监控等干预措施。然而，一个平均准确的内部估计器仍可能做出糟糕的决策动作。现有的评估方法往往混淆了“估计准确性”与“动作导致的损失”，缺乏统一的框架来验证估计器是否真正适合其指导的具体任务。

Method: 提出ObserverBench基准框架，通过固定模型、信息边界、允许动作、决策规则、保留测试用例和损失函数来定义具体任务。该框架将“估计准确性”与“动作导致的损失”分开报告。在GPT-2-small、Qwen2.5-7B、Gemma-2-9B-it等模型上，针对闭环控制、电路干预和安全分流任务进行理论与实验验证，并对比了稀疏自编码器（SAE）读出与稠密控制的性能。

Result: 1. 在闭环控制中，观察者误差在起始点及干预可达方向上至关重要。
2. 在电路干预任务中，成对观察者能更准确预测未见效应，但不总能选择更低损失的动作；而基于动作损失训练的观察者能选择更优动作。
3. 在安全分流中，即使能完美区分违规的评分，若违规成本不同，也可能导致干预预算分配不当。
4. AUROC排名与部署损失排名不一致，且不同模型的最佳信息源不同。
5. 稀疏SAE读出在特定条件下表现不如层匹配的稠密控制。

Conclusion: ObserverBench通过提供固定的任务契约、可运行的基线和基于表格的提交方式，确立了通过“动作赋能”来评估可解释性方法的新范式。研究证明，仅凭估计精度不足以判断解释方法的有效性，必须结合具体任务的动作损失进行综合评估。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability is increasingly used to guide interventions such as activation steering, circuit removal, and safety monitoring. Yet an internal estimate that is accurate on average can still choose a poor action.
  We present ObserverBench, a benchmark framework for testing whether an internal estimator---an observer---is adequate for the intervention, control, or safety task it directs. Each task fixes the model, information boundary, allowed actions, decision rule, held-out cases, and loss. The benchmark reports estimation accuracy separately from the loss caused by the chosen action.
  Theory and experiments show why both are needed. In closed-loop control, observer errors matter at the starting point and along directions the allowed intervention can reach. On circuit-intervention tasks in GPT-2-small and Qwen2.5-7B, pairwise observers predict unseen effects more accurately without always choosing better actions; observers trained on action loss choose lower-loss actions. In safety triage, a score that perfectly separates violations can allocate a fixed intervention budget poorly when violations have different costs. Across Qwen2.5-7B, Gemma-2-9B-it, and prospectively frozen Qwen3.5-9B APPS tasks, AUROC can rank monitors differently from deployment loss, and the best information source changes across models. Sparse SAE readouts also trail their layer-matched dense controls on the reported Qwen panels, under disclosed activation-density or checkpoint mismatches.
  ObserverBench provides fixed task contracts, runnable baselines, and table-based submissions for evaluating interpretability methods through the actions they enable.

</details>


### [286] [Spectral characteristics of autoencoder parameters as a vector representation of data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03495)
*Maria Nikitina,Anton Bishuk,Oleg Bakhteev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了自编码器模型参数与训练数据统计特性之间的关系，提出模型参数可视为样本的稠密向量表示，并通过理论和实验验证了这一假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索自编码器参数与训练数据之间的内在联系，验证模型参数是否能作为数据样本的有效表示，以简化模型分析和数据表征方法。

Method: 基于自编码器参数矩阵的谱特性构建向量表示，通过理论分析参数矩阵奇异值与训练数据协方差矩阵特征值的关系，并在CIFAR-10和FashionMNIST数据集上进行实验验证。

Result: 理论分析表明模型参数矩阵的奇异值与训练数据协方差矩阵的特征值相关，确保了数据空间与参数空间之间的信息传递；实验结果显示，基于参数生成的向量表示能够高精度地区分不同数据子集训练的模型。

Conclusion: 训练后的自编码器参数可被视为样本的有效表示，无需复杂向量生成算法或原始样本即可实现模型区分，为模型参数分析提供了新视角。

Abstract: This paper examines the relationship between the parameters of autoencoder models and the statistical properties of the data on which they are trained. Autoencoders are defined as models with an encoder-decoder architecture, trained to reconstruct input data through a compressed latent representation. It is proposed that the model parameters can be viewed as a dense vector representation of the corresponding sample.
