To test this hypothesis, a theoretical and experimental study is conducted in which a vector representation is formed based on the spectral characteristics of the autoencoder parameter matrices. Theoretical analysis shows that the singular values of the model parameter matrices are related to the eigenvalues of the covariance matrix of the training data, ensuring the transfer of information between the data space and the parameter space. Experimental results on the CIFAR-10 and FashionMNIST datasets confirm that the resulting vector representations allow for a high degree of accuracy in distinguishing between models trained on different data subsets, without resorting to complex vector generation algorithms or using the original samples. These results suggest that the parameters of trained autoencoders can be viewed as sample representations.

</details>


### [287] [Learnable composition for neural operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03069)
*Zituo Chen,Baiming Zhang,Sili Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LatentDDM方法，通过在小子域上预训练神经算子并冻结权重，仅训练轻量级模块来组合局部预测，从而显著降低神经算子在新几何或工况下的部署成本并提升精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子作为物理模拟的代理模型，在域几何、尺寸或运行条件与训练数据不同时精度会下降。虽然监督适应可以恢复精度，但即使少量目标数据也需要昂贵的高保真模拟，因此需要一种协同设计预训练和迁移学习的方法来降低部署成本。

Method: 提出LatentDDM框架：首先在小子域上预训练神经算子以预测局部场；在新场景下，冻结该预训练算子，仅训练一个轻量级模块来组合这些局部预测，实现从局部预训练到组合级迁移的协同设计。

Result: 在稳态Darcy流和非定常不可压缩绕流两个问题上评估：在大尺寸Darcy域上，经16次目标模拟适应后，LatentDDM误差比全域处理模型低36-56%；在高频俯仰翼型流动中，无论是零样本还是少样本校准，其20步场滚动预测精度均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 协同设计的局部预训练与组合级迁移是构建物理基础模型的有效设计原则，能显著降低适应新物理场景的成本并提高泛化能力。

Abstract: Neural operators are fast, differentiable surrogates for physical simulation, but their accuracy often degrades when domain geometry, size, or operating conditions differ from training. Supervised adaptation can recover accuracy, but even a small target set requires costly high-fidelity simulations. We therefore ask how pretraining and transfer can be designed together to reduce this deployment cost. LatentDDM first pretrains a neural operator to predict fields on small subdomains. For a new setting, it freezes this operator and trains only a lightweight module that composes the local predictions. We evaluate our method on two complementary problems: steady Darcy flow, where long-range pressure coupling must extend across increasingly large porous domains, and unsteady incompressible flow around a pitching airfoil, where rollout errors compound as target pitching frequencies exceed the training range. Compared with the capacity-matched models that process the full domain at once, LatentDDM's error is 36-56% lower on larger Darcy domains after adaptation with 16 target simulations. It also improves 20-step field rollouts in fast-pitching airfoil flow, both zero-shot and after few-shot calibration. These results identify the co-designed local pretraining and composition-level transfer as a promising design principle for physical foundation models.

</details>


### [288] [High-Dimensional Learning Dynamics of Attention-Indexed Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03858)
*Yizhou Xu,Margarita Sagitova,Lenka Zdeborová,Florent Krzakala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了注意力索引模型的训练动力学，揭示了高维极限下总体损失景观由有限迹序参数刻画，而在线随机梯度下降（SGD）受无限矩阵矩层次支配但可被有限截断系统近似；同时发现注意力参数化本身构成一种架构隐式偏差，其中绑定注意力（Tied Attention）能通过自动对称性破缺实现弱恢复，而未绑定注意力（Untied Attention）则表现出快慢动力学机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管注意力机制是现代基础模型的核心，但当注意力矩阵具有高秩时，其训练动力学仍缺乏深入理解，亟需理论框架来解析其优化行为与收敛特性。

Method: 提出“注意力索引模型”这一广义框架以表征多层多头注意力架构；在高维极限下分析总体损失景观的迹序参数特征；推导在线SGD的无限矩阵矩层次并证明其可被有限截断系统指数级近似；对比分析绑定注意力（$S=WW^\top$）与未绑定注意力（$S=UV^\top$）参数化下的动力学行为与对称性破缺机制。

Result: 1. 总体损失景观由有限集迹序参数刻画，而在线SGD由无限矩阵矩层次支配，但后者可被有限截断系统高效近似。2. 直接优化注意力矩阵$S$易陷入无信息状态；绑定注意力$S=WW^\top$诱导自动对称性破缺，在$Θ(d^2\log d)$样本量下实现弱恢复。3. 未绑定注意力$S=UV^\top$呈现快慢机制：预激活均值在快时间尺度演化，重叠度在慢时间尺度演化；当快动力学选择的状态打破初始对称性时，可在$Θ(d^2\log d)$尺度实现弱恢复。

Conclusion: 注意力参数化形式本身构成一种架构隐式偏差，显著影响优化轨迹与恢复能力；绑定结构通过内在对称性破缺促进有效学习，而未绑定结构依赖快慢动力学协同实现对称性破缺，为理解高秩注意力模型的训练机制提供了理论基石。

Abstract: Attention mechanisms are central to modern foundation models, yet their training dynamics remain poorly understood, especially when the attention matrices have extensive rank. In this work, we study attention-indexed models, a broad framework that can represent multi-layer and multi-head attention architectures. First, we show that, in a suitable high-dimensional limit, the population-loss landscape is characterized by a finite set of trace order parameters. In contrast, online stochastic gradient descent (SGD) is governed by an infinite hierarchy of matrix moments, which we show can be exponentially well-approximated by a finite truncated system. Second, this framework reveals that attention parameterization itself can act as an architectural implicit bias. Direct optimization of an attention matrix $S\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}$ can remain trapped in an uninformative state. Tied attention ($S=WW^\top$) induces an automatic symmetry-breaking mechanism and yields weak recovery in $Θ(d^2\log d)$ samples. For untied attention, $S=UV^\top$, we uncover a fast-slow mechanism: the pre-activation mean first evolves on a fast timescale, while the overlaps evolve on a slower one. Weak recovery on the $Θ(d^2\log d)$ scale occurs when the state selected by the fast dynamics breaks the initial symmetry.

</details>


### [289] [LeanStream: A Speculate-and-Refine Streaming Framework for Efficient on-Device LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03079)
*Renyuan Liu,Yuyang Leng,Kaiyan Liu,Yuzhou Zhong,Shaohan Hu,Chun-Fu,Chen,Peijun Zhao,Heechul Yun,Shuochao Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LeanStream框架，通过流式推测与细化机制，实现GPU执行与存储I/O的细粒度重叠，显著降低内存占用并提升推理吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在移动和嵌入式设备上进行LLM推理面临模型权重远超可用DRAM的挑战，现有系统在稀疏执行决策的准确性与计算-I/O重叠效率之间存在根本性权衡。

Method: LeanStream采用流式推测与细化框架，利用部分GPU结果逐步优化计算、加载和缓存保留优先级，实现细粒度的GPU执行与存储I/O重叠。

Result: 在移动和嵌入式平台上实现，相比现有系统，内存使用减少4.8×至7.5×，token生成吞吐提升1.6×至2.1×。

Conclusion: LeanStream有效解决了设备端LLM推理的内存与效率问题，为资源受限场景提供高效解决方案。

Abstract: On-device LLM inference is attractive for privacy and responsiveness, but remains challenging on mobile and embedded devices because model weights far exceed available DRAM. Prior systems exploit activation sparsity and offload weights to SSD or flash storage, but face a fundamental systems trade-off: accurate sparse execution decisions require the latest context, whereas efficient computation-I/O overlap requires early prediction. As a result, existing designs either serialize execution or incur redundant weight fetches, extra computation, and large cache overheads. We present LeanStream, a streaming speculate-and-refine framework for efficient on-device LLM inference. LeanStream progressively refines computation, loading, and cache-retention priorities using partial GPU results, enabling fine-grained overlap between GPU execution and storage I/O. We implement LeanStream on both mobile and embedded platforms. Compared with prior on-device LLM inference systems, LeanStream reduces memory usage by 4.8$\times$ to 7.5$\times$ at the best throughput achieved by prior work, while further improving token generation throughput by 1.6$\times$ to 2.1$\times$.

</details>


### [290] [The Gradient Does Not See Rank: Rank-Indifference in Matrix-CODI on ProsQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03090)
*Samuel Larson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现矩阵值思维链模型中的潜在矩阵秩（rank）与推理性能无关，低秩截断不影响准确率，且不同读出机制下秩-k消融曲线均平坦，表明秩盲性可能源于位置无关性而非矩阵结构本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证矩阵潜在变量是否通过叠加承载并行推理路径，假设若如此，矩阵的秩应能追踪这些路径，且低秩截断会损害需要多组件解决方案的任务性能。

Method: 在四种训练制度下训练矩阵-CODI模型，进行秩-k投影消融实验；测试四种不同读出机制（双线性、双线性+GELU、SVD增强、二次型）；使用线性探针评估潜在矩阵Z的预测能力；设置 vanilla GPT-2 SFT 作为负对照。

Result: 秩-k投影消融曲线在0.6个百分点内保持平坦；有效秩跨度为{4, 12, 13}但损失函数不奖励特定秩；所有四种读出机制下的秩-k曲线均平坦（Spearman p值不显著）；线性探针在Z上的表现优于原始预训练隐藏状态；负对照实验复现了平坦曲线，表明秩-k消融混淆了秩盲性与位置无关性。

Conclusion: 矩阵潜在变量的秩并不反映并行推理路径的数量，秩盲性并非由特定的读出机制引起，而是可能源于干预范式本身将秩盲性与位置无关性混淆，暗示当前矩阵瓶颈结构未有效编码结构化推理路径。

Abstract: Continuous chain-of-thought models compress reasoning into latent tokens. Matrix-valued variants, which route each latent token through a d x d matrix bottleneck, introduce rank as a single-sample structural observable on the latent matrix Z. If matrix latents carry parallel reasoning paths via superposition, rank should track them, and truncating Z to low rank should hurt accuracy on tasks whose solutions plausibly require multiple components. Across four training regimes of a matrix-CODI model (three on ProsQA, one on GSM8K-Aug below the learning threshold), the rank-k projection ablation curve is flat to within 0.6 percentage points. A three-seed replication yields 81.0 +/- 2.0 percentage points accuracy while the final effective rank of Z spans {4, 12, 13}; the loss does not reward any particular rank. To test whether rank-blindness arises from the flatten-then-project readout alone, we trained four readouts: a bilinear reparametrization, a bilinear-plus-GELU readout nonlinear in Z, an SVD-augmented readout feeding singular values through an MLP, and a quadratic readout in Z Z^T. All four rank-k curves remain flat (Spearman p-values 0.63, 0.14, 0.82, 0.46). The flat curves persist for readouts nonlinear in Z. A linear probe on Z underperforms a raw pretrained hidden state at target prediction (AUC 0.673 vs. 0.846). A negative control on vanilla GPT-2 SFT (no matrix bottleneck, no Z, three seeds, n=500) reproduces a flat rank-k curve under the same intervention paradigm with pooled-mean range 0.20pp, and a random-h sensitivity floor lands at the same accuracy: the rank-k ablation alone conflates rank-blindness with position-irrelevance.

</details>


### [291] [Distilling deep optical flow stereo methods to retrieve dense three-dimensional wind fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03100)
*Thomas J. Vandal,Dong L. Wu,James L. Carr,Derek J. Posselt,Elise Penn,Tristan Ballard,August Posch,Kate Duffy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用深度光流替代传统立体匹配中的窗口追踪，并通过知识蒸馏将多星立体风场模型迁移至单星模型，实现了全球静止轨道卫星影像的高效、高精度风场反演，在水汽波段表现优于业务化AMVs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统大气运动矢量（AMVs）依赖窗口互相关追踪和数值天气预报（NWP）背景场估算高度，存在循环依赖导致高度不准、计算成本高及反演稀疏的问题；现有的立体风场虽能几何解算高度消除NWP依赖，但计算繁重且覆盖受限。

Method: 1. 采用深度光流（deep optical flow）替代立体匹配中的窗口追踪以提升效率；2. 结合自监督几何残差损失与有监督无线电探空仪重建损失进行微调；3. 利用知识蒸馏将多星立体“教师”模型迁移至单星“学生”模型，消除多星重叠需求；4. 学生模型模仿教师模型的卡方检验和高度不确定性以进行质量控制。

Result: 通过三重碰撞验证，立体风场模型在水汽波段（6.2, 6.9, 7.3 μm）的性能优于业务化AMVs，但在长波红外波段（11.2 μm）性能有所下降；学生模型成功实现了全球全圆盘静止轨道影像的风场生成。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了传统AMVs的高度估算依赖问题和立体风场的计算及覆盖局限，通过深度学习与知识蒸馏实现了单星高效、高精度的全球风场反演，显著提升了水汽波段的风场数据质量。

Abstract: Geostationary atmospheric motion vectors (AMVs) provide the dense horizontal wind vectors (u,v) and heights ingested into data assimilation systems. Traditional AMVs track features using window-based cross-correlation and estimate heights via infrared brightness temperatures paired with numerical weather prediction (NWP) background states, creating a circular dependency that yields inaccurate heights, high computational cost, and sparse retrievals. Stereo winds from GEO-GEO and GEO-LEO geometrically resolve heights from parallax shifts across different poses, eliminating NWP dependence and improving accuracy, but they remain computationally heavy with limited coverage. In this work, we replace window-based tracking in stereo matching with deep optical flow for efficient, improved retrieval. Fine-tuning balances a self-supervised geometric residual loss with supervised radiosonde reconstruction. To eliminate multi-satellite overlap requirements, we distill the stereo teacher into a single-satellite student model. Chi-square and height uncertainties from the teacher are emulated by the student for quality assurance. The student generates winds across full-disk GEO imagery globally. Validation compares stereo and student models against radiosondes, operational AMVs, ERA5 reanalysis, and EarthCARE cloud profiles. Results through triple collocation show that stereo winds improve performance beyond operational AMVs for water vapor bands (6.2, 6.9, and 7.3 μm), wit degradation in the long-wave infrared (11.2 μm) band.

</details>


### [292] [Scaling Laws, Tabular Data and Actuarial Ratemaking Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03106)
*Ronald Richman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了精算定价中的缩放定律，发现不同模型家族的数据缩放指数差异显著：TabM 表现出比纯监督表格 Transformer 和标准 MLP 更强的数据缩放能力，而 Transformer 变体若无额外归纳偏置则参数缩放效果微弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在现代深度学习中常见的缩放定律（即随着模型容量、训练数据和计算量的增加，持有损失按幂律趋势改善）是否同样适用于数据具有表格化、异质性和噪声特征的精算定价领域，尤其是在经典广义线性模型（GLMs）仍是强基线的背景下。

Method: 利用真实的机动车保险投资组合数据，训练不同家族的模型，并在不断增加的训练数据比例和多个随机种子下进行实验；使用泊松偏差（Poisson deviance）作为评估指标，衡量模型在泊松计数预测任务中的样本外拟合优度。

Result: 所有模型家族均随数据增加而改善，但缩放指数差异巨大：TabM 展现出显著更强的数据缩放能力；纯监督表格 Transformer 和标准 MLP 基线较弱；Transformer 变体除非结合额外的归纳偏置（如 TabM 式适配或自监督），否则参数缩放效果微弱。

Conclusion: 精算表格任务的有效缩放依赖于架构设计和损失函数目标的选择，单纯增加 Transformer 规模收益有限；研究结果为根据不同数据 regimes 进行模型选择提供了定量指导。

Abstract: Scaling laws in modern deep learning describe how held-out loss improves as model capacity, training data, and compute increase, often following power-law trends. We investigate whether analogous scaling regularities arise in actuarial ratemaking, where data are tabular, heterogeneous, and noisy, and where classical models such as GLMs remain strong baselines. Using a real-world motor insurance portfolio, we train models from different families across increasing fractions of the training data and multiple random seeds, evaluating out-of-sample Poisson deviance, a likelihood-based loss for Poisson count predictions in which lower values indicate better held-out fit. We find that all model families improve with additional data, but scaling exponents differ substantially: TabM exhibits markedly stronger data scaling than purely supervised tabular Transformers and standard MLP baselines. Transformer variants show weak parameter scaling unless augmented with additional inductive biases (TabM-style adaptation or self-supervision). These results provide quantitative guidance on model selection by data regime and suggest that effective scaling on actuarial tabular tasks depends on architecture and loss function objective design, with simple increases in Transformer size providing limited gains.

</details>


### [293] [Kernel Reboot: Breaking the Boundaries of Neural Tangent Kernels for Neural Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03117)
*Amir Mallak,Alaa Maalouf,Lior Wolf,Daniela Rus,Dan Rosenbaum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出三种算法（NTK-KIP、MetaQuill、MetaQuill-KIP）解决神经场在稀疏观测下快速高质量重建的难题，通过结合NTK回归与元学习，实现非线性、可复用先验的轻量级适配，在极少观测数据下实现高PSNR重建与合理语义修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经场（NFs）虽能映射连续坐标到信号，但在稀疏观测下实现快速高质量重建仍具挑战；经典NTK回归虽提供闭式解，但本质为线性且无法积累可复用任务先验，限制了其在实际少样本场景中的应用。

Method: 1. NTK-KIP：学习蒸馏的坐标支持集（及可选标签），使有限NTK能从少量观测数据修复大缺失区域，生成紧凑非线性表示；2. MetaQuill：元学习共享INR初始化，新场景仅需更新小规模任务特定权重偏移，实现真正特征学习与可复用先验；3. MetaQuill-KIP：融合前两者，以KIP风格非线性热启动 seeding 任务，再围绕元学习初始化微调小偏移。

Result: MetaQuill-KIP在极稀疏观测下实现高PSNR重建与语义合理的图像修复，仅需轻量级每实例适配；相比依赖大型预训练生成先验与昂贵每图像调优的扩散模型基线，本方法更高效且实用。

Conclusion: NTK驱动的神经场可同时具备非线性与元学习能力，弥合了分析性核方法与实用少样本重建之间的差距，为稀疏数据下的高效神经场重建提供了新范式。

Abstract: Neural fields (NFs) map continuous coordinates to signals such as color or density, but fast high-quality reconstruction from sparse observations remains difficult. Classical Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) regression gives closed-form fits, yet it is fundamentally linear and cannot accumulate reusable task priors. We develop three algorithms that address these gaps. NTK-KIP learns a distilled support set of coordinates (and optional labels) so that a finite NTK can inpaint large missing regions from little observed data, yielding a compact non-linear representation instead of a raw kernel solve. MetaQuill meta-learns a shared initialization for an INR so that new scenes can be adapted by updating only a small task-specific weight offset, which provides true feature learning and a reusable prior. Finally, MetaQuill-KIP fuses both ideas: it seeds the task with a KIP-style non-linear warm start, then refines only that small offset around the meta-learned initialization. MetaQuill-KIP achieves high-PSNR reconstructions and semantically plausible inpainting under very sparse observations, while requiring only lightweight per-instance adaptation, whereas diffusion-style baselines typically depend on large pretrained generative priors and costly per-image tuning. This shows that NTK-driven neural fields can be made both non-linear and meta-learnable, narrowing the gap between analytic kernels and practical few-shot reconstruction.

</details>


### [294] [Routing Is Not Enough: Diagnosing Intra-Adapter Subspace Contention in MoE+LoRA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03150)
*Mehreen Hossain Chowdhury,Nowshin Mahjabin,Ahmed Shafin Ruhan,Md Azam Hossain,Abu Raihan Mostofa Kamal,Md Tahmid Rahman Laskar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出MoE路由分离不足以防止多域微调中的负迁移，提出SpawnLoRA通过在专家内动态添加门控子适配器来解决梯度冲突问题，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究假设MoE的token级路由能自动分离域特定更新，但实验发现即使路由近乎不重叠，引入新域数据仍会导致原有域性能下降（负迁移），需探究其根本原因并解决。

Method: 引入Jaccard路由重叠率和适配器梯度余弦相似度作为诊断指标，定位到负迁移源于正交域梯度在低秩适配器子空间内的竞争；提出SpawnLoRA，在检测到适配器级冲突时动态在MoE专家内部添加门控子适配器，同时保持路由器固定。

Result: 在Phi-tiny-MoE-instruct和OLMoE-1B-7B模型上的多混合设置评估显示，SpawnLoRA相比标准LoRA和秩自适应LoRA有效减少了负迁移。

Conclusion: 专家内部的结构化分离（如动态子适配器）比单纯依赖路由分离或秩扩展更能有效缓解多域微调中的负迁移问题。

Abstract: Multi-domain fine-tuning often combines MoE routing with LoRA, assuming that token-level routing separates domain-specific updates. We test this assumption in MoE+LoRA using Python code paired with biomedical text and mathematical reasoning. Although these domains show near-disjoint expert routing, adding biomedical data substantially increases code perplexity, indicating that routing separation alone may not prevent negative transfer. To localize the failure, we introduce Jaccard routing overlap and adapter-gradient cosine similarity, which measure expert sharing and update compatibility, respectively. These diagnostics indicate that interference arises mostly from nearly orthogonal domain gradients competing within the same low-rank adapter subspace. We address this issue with SpawnLoRA, which dynamically adds gated sub-adapters inside MoE experts when adapter-level contention is detected, while keeping the router fixed. We evaluate SpawnLoRA on Phi-tiny-MoE-instruct and OLMoE-1B-7B across multiple mixture settings and find that it effectively reduces negative transfer compared with standard and rank-adaptive LoRA. These results demonstrate that structural separation inside experts provides benefits beyond routing or rank expansion alone.

</details>


### [295] [Frontier LLMs are effective batch optimizers: Assessing reasoning models in continuous and discrete settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03177)
*Frank Hu,Shriram Chennakesavalu,David Graff*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 前沿大语言模型（LLM）在语义丰富的离散优化场景中表现优异，但在数值测试函数等连续场景中，其批量优化性能不如传统方法且较为脆弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型因大规模预训练而成为有吸引力的优化先验，但现代推理型LLM在批量优化设置（batch optimization settings）中的有效性尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 研究调查了当前一代前沿LLM在连续和离散设置中作为批量优化器的性能，将其与经典非LLM优化方法进行了对比评估。

Result: LLM在数值测试函数上作为零样本批量优化器具有竞争力，但相比传统方法表现脆弱；而在语义丰富的设置中，LLM先验显著优于传统方法。

Conclusion: LLM的批量优化行为在与预训练数据结构最相似的离散空间中导航和推理时最为有效，表明其优势高度依赖于任务的语义丰富度。

Abstract: Frontier large language models (LLMs) have become attractive priors for optimization due to their large-scale pretraining that enables them to navigate a variety of optimization settings. However, the effectiveness of modern reasoning LLMs in batch optimization settings remains underexplored. Here we investigate the performance of the current generation of frontier LLMs as batch optimizers in both continuous and discrete settings. We find that while LLMs are competitive zero-shot batch optimizers for numerical test functions, their performance is brittle compared to classical non-LLM optimization approaches. However, LLM priors are significantly better in semantically rich settings, indicating that their batch optimization behavior is highly effective when navigating and reasoning over the discrete spaces most similar in structure to their pretraining data.

</details>


### [296] [Portable Causal Fairness Across Synthetic Data Generator Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03180)
*Steven Golob,Sikha Pentyala,Martine De Cock*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在生成合成数据时，通过因果图边缘切割（edge cuts）来消除不公平路径的机制是否适用于不同类型的生成器（包括边际分布、GAN 和扩散模型），并验证了该机制在不同隐私保护级别下的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 之前的研究（DECAF）仅在一个非隐私 GAN 上验证了通过因果图边缘切割实现公平性的方法，但未测试该机制是否属于 DECAF 特有还是因果分解本身的特性，也未在多种生成器家族和不同隐私保护级别下进行验证。

Method: 将三种公平性定义应用于来自三个不同家族（基于边际分布、GAN 和扩散模型）的九个生成器，每个生成器包含不同隐私保护级别的变体，在 Adult 和 COMPAS 数据集上进行了 2,520 次匹配对实验。

Result: 该机制在所有测试的生成器中均有效；新提出的因果扩散主干模型在所有家族中产生了最公平的发布结果，且保真度接近边际分布层级；应用边缘切割对保真度影响很小，仅导致下游分类器 AUC 平均下降 0.07 到 0.15；添加隐私保护并不会降低数据的公平性。

Conclusion: 通过因果图边缘切割消除不公平路径的机制具有普适性，可广泛应用于不同类型的生成器；因果扩散模型在公平性和保真度之间取得了最佳平衡；隐私保护与公平性可以兼容。

Abstract: When a statistical agency or regulator releases synthetic data in place of sensitive records, it chooses the generator that produces the table, and can shape that generator so unfair pathways are absent. DECAF made this concrete on one non-private GAN: three fairness definitions become three sets of edge cuts on the generator's causal graph. Whether the mechanism belongs to DECAF, or to causal factorisation itself, was untested. We port all three definitions to nine generators from three unrelated families (marginals-based, GAN, and diffusion, each with differentially private variants), across three levels of formal privacy guarantee, over 2,520 matched-pair runs on Adult and COMPAS datasets. The mechanism transfers everywhere, and our new causal diffusion backbone yields the fairest release of any family we tested, at fidelity close to the marginals tier. Applying the cut barely moves fidelity, only costs a downstream classifier about $0.07$ to $0.15$ AUC on average, and adding privacy guarantees don't make the data less fair.

</details>


### [297] [Language-encoded network topology enables large language models to reason about complex networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03229)
*Ucchwas Talukder Utsha,Sakib Mostafa,James Zou,Md Tauhidul Islam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BioGlyph通过将网络拓扑编译成可解释的结构角色语言，显著提升了大语言模型（LLM）在跨领域网络结构推理任务中的表现，尤其在稠密且具社区结构的网络中效果显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型（LLM）擅长自然语言处理，但在处理以边列表、句子或测量表形式给出的网络数据时表现不佳，因为需要推断其结构含义。现有的方法难以让LLM理解网络中的中心元素、连接不同社区的桥梁以及移除元素后的变化等结构性问题。

Method: BioGlyph结合图划分和结构测量来识别如枢纽、社区核心和跨社区连接器等角色，并通过固定规则将其翻译成通用词汇。该方法为每个元素描述其结构角色、支持证据和语义后果，且无需修改网络或LLM本身。

Result: 在跨越五个领域的二十个网络上，BioGlyph显著提高了开源LLM回答结构推理问题的能力，相比基于边、数值和学习的表示方法，系统准确率最高提升了26个百分点。消融实验表明，增益来自于以语义可解释的术语显式编码结构角色。

Conclusion: BioGlyph为语言模型和科学家提供了一种可解释的表示方法，用于推理网络结构，特别是在稠密且具有社区结构的网络中表现突出，并能在生物网络中揭示生物学组织特征。

Abstract: Networks describe systems in biology and beyond, from protein interactions and social relationships to power grids and citation records. Reasoning about such systems requires understanding their structure: which elements are central, which connections bridge separate communities, and how it changes when elements are removed. Although large language models (LLMs) excel at natural language, they struggle with such questions when networks are given as edge lists, sentences or measurement tables, because their structural meaning must be inferred. Here we introduce BioGlyph, which compiles network topology into an interpretable and transferable language of structural roles. BioGlyph combines graph partitioning and structural measurements to identify roles such as hubs, community cores and cross-community connectors, and fixed rules to translate them into a universal vocabulary. The representation describes each element through its structural role, supporting evidence and semantic consequences, leaving both the network and the LLM unchanged. Across twenty networks spanning five domains, BioGlyph substantially improves open LLMs' ability to answer structural reasoning questions, outperforming edge-based, numerical and learned representations by up to 26 percentage points in system accuracy. Ablations show that the gain comes from explicitly encoding structural roles in semantically interpretable terms. The gain is more prominent in dense, community-structured networks and diminishes in sparse networks whose topology is more readily inferred from text. In a budding-yeast protein-interaction network, BioGlyph exposes biological organization: cross-community connectors are enriched for essential genes, whereas peripheral proteins are depleted. BioGlyph thus provides an interpretable representation for both language models and scientists to reason about network structure.

</details>


### [298] [The 2026 PNPL Competition: Word Classification and Efficient Cross-Subject Generalisation in LibriBrain100](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03231)
*Francesco Mantegna,Gereon Elvers,Dulhan Jayalath,Gilad Landau,Tasha Kim,Miran Özdogan,Luisa Kurth,Teyun Kwon,SungJun Cho,Benjamin Ballyk,Alex Fung,Anna Greer,Pratik Somaiya,Christian Herff,Yorguin Mantilla Ramos,Hamza Abdelhedi,Karim Jerbi,Greg Farquhar,Brendan Shillingford,Mark Woolrich,Oiwi Parker Jones*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 2026年PNPL竞赛推出LibriBrain100数据集，通过“深度”和“广度”双赛道，分别致力于提升受试者内单词分类的规模性能及受试者间的泛化能力，旨在解决非侵入式脑机接口（BCI）在临床可行时间窗口内的跨用户通用性难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管2025年竞赛在受试者内任务上取得了显著进展，但实用的脑机接口必须具备跨用户泛化能力，且仅需几分钟而非数小时的校准数据，以满足临床可行性并帮助瘫痪患者恢复交流。

Method: 发布扩展数据集LibriBrain100，包含32名新受试者（每人约40分钟数据）及额外的受试者内数据（约80小时）；设立双赛道机制：“深度赛道”聚焦大规模受试者内单词分类，“广度赛道”聚焦跨受试者泛化，逐步将微调数据量从40分钟递减至10分钟。

Result: 构建了支持单词分类任务的大规模多受试者MEG数据集，并确立了从受试者内高性能向受试者间少样本泛化过渡的竞赛评估框架，将校准时间压缩至临床可行范围。

Conclusion: 2026年PNPL竞赛通过 LibriBrain100 数据集和双赛道设计，有效推动了非侵入式语音解码从实验室规模向临床实用化的跨越，特别是解决了跨用户泛化和快速校准的关键瓶颈。

Abstract: The ambition of the 2025 PNPL competition (Landau et al., 2025) was to launch a multi-year curriculum for non-invasive speech decoding. Designed to progress from foundational tasks toward the linguistic complexity required for a practical brain-computer interface (BCI), it set the stage with speech detection and phoneme classification tasks. Winning submissions reached F1-macro scores of 95.6% and 73.6% on the respective tasks (Elvers et al., 2026), highly significant advances. This success was built on the LibriBrain dataset (Özdogan et al., 2025), the largest within-subject MEG dataset recorded at the time with ${\sim}50$ hours of data for one subject. However, while within-subject scale drives strong decoding performance, a practical BCI must generalise to new users from minutes of data, not hours.
  The 2026 PNPL competition responds to this challenge with LibriBrain100 (Mantegna et al., 2026), an extended LibriBrain dataset with 32 additional subjects (${\sim}40$ minutes each) plus even more within-subject data (${\sim}80$ hours). Advancing the curriculum of tasks to focus on word classification, two complementary tracks are presented in this competition: the Deep track targets within-subject word classification at scale, aiming at the best possible performance; the Broad track targets cross-subject generalisation, progressively reducing the amount of subject-specific fine-tuning data from ${\sim}40$ to ${\sim}20$ to ${\sim}10$ minutes, a duration that falls within a clinically feasible range and brings us a step closer to a non-invasive BCI capable of restoring communication to people living with profound paralysis.

</details>


### [299] [B2B Customer Conversion Prediction: A Document Representation, Graph Theory, and CatBoost Driven Methodology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03239)
*Tianqi Wang,Sheikh Shams Azam,Wan Eih Huang,Anton Wiranata,Christopher G. Brinton,Jan P. Allebach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究B2B一次性销售场景下的客户转化预测问题，提出了一种基于多键聚合客户数据、聚类非标准化键（如公司名称）并生成特征的框架，使用CatBoost模型实现91%的预测准确率，并据此提供个性化营销建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在B2B一次性销售中，购买周期可能长达数月甚至数年，因此识别高潜力客户并推荐个性化营销活动对提高营销效率至关重要。

Method: 提出一种基于多键的客户数据聚合算法；针对公司名称等非标准化键，设计新颖的架构进行聚类以处理拼写错误和变体；定义并生成客户特征集；应用CatBoost模型进行转化预测。

Result: 框架实现了91%的预测准确率；基于预测结果和模型分析，讨论了促进转化的个性化活动建议。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了B2B客户数据聚合与转化预测问题，能够支持精准营销决策，提升销售漏斗转化效率。

Abstract: In the one-time selling B2B context, the buying cycle may last months or even years. During the long process, targeting customers that have a high potential to make purchases and recommending personalized campaigns accordingly are important for effective marketing. For this goal, we study the following problems, B2B customer data aggregation, customer feature generation, and prediction of whether a B2B customer would show interest in making a purchase (i.e., prediction of conversion into sales funnel). We propose an algorithm to aggregate individual contacts to the B2B customer level based on multiple keys. For non-standardized keys such as company names, we propose a novel architecture to cluster them in a domain encompassing irregularities such as spelling mistakes and spelling variants. We then define and generate a set of features and apply the CatBoost model for customer conversion prediction. Our framework achieves 91\% prediction accuracy. Based on the prediction results and analysis of the model, we then discuss personalized campaign recommendations to foster conversion.

</details>


### [300] [FlowBalance: Verifier-Grounded Self-Improvement from On-Policy Reasoning Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03241)
*Zixun Huang,Kishan Panaganti,Haitao Mi,Leowei Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FlowBalance方法，通过验证器校准的自引导机制改进推理模型的自我提升过程，在数学推理任务上优于FlowRL，提升训练速度、稳定性及策略多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模型的自我提升内循环脆弱：终端验证器监督可靠但稀疏，密集的同模型引导可能强化错误自信或导致学习模式单一。

Method: FlowBalance学习完整响应的归一化分布，利用冻结策略视图生成token级对数概率增益并聚合为轨迹级自引导分数，通过验证器导出的群体优势校准该分数，采用轨迹平衡拟合归一化目标。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B和Qwen3-8B上数学推理平均性能优于FlowRL，训练速度更快、更稳定，避免响应长度崩溃，在AIME24诊断中展现更高的正确策略多样性。

Conclusion: FlowBalance实现了通过轨迹平衡的结果校准自引导，无需单独的token级模仿损失，建立了组内对比保持、最小变化反向KL表征、单调验证器控制等理论保证。

Abstract: A reasoning model can improve from its own on-policy experience, but this inner loop is fragile: terminal verifiers provide reliable yet sparse supervision, while dense same-model guidance can reinforce false confidence or overconcentrate learning on a narrow solution mode. We introduce FlowBalance, a verifier-grounded self-improvement method that learns a normalized distribution over complete responses. For each on-policy trajectory, a frozen training-time view of the same policy uses privileged context to produce token-level log-probability gains, which are aggregated into a trajectory-level self-guidance score. FlowBalance calibrates this score with the verifier-derived group advantage: guidance is retained on positive-advantage trajectories, reversed on negative-advantage trajectories, and disabled when the rollout group provides no outcome preference. The resulting energy exponentially reweights a reference policy, and profiled trajectory balance fits the normalized target with one log-partition estimate per rollout group. This realizes outcome-calibrated self-guidance via trajectory balance, without a separate token-level imitation loss. Our analysis establishes within-group contrast preservation, a minimum-change reverse-KL characterization, monotonic verifier control of target reward, and an exact correction against false-positive self-guidance on rejected responses. On mathematical reasoning, FlowBalance improves average performance over FlowRL on both Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-8B, while also improving training speed and stability, avoiding direct OPSD's response-length collapse, and exhibiting higher correct-strategy diversity in a controlled AIME24 diagnostic.

</details>


### [301] [Selective Hypergraph Refinement for Frozen Graph Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03265)
*Zimo Si*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出选择性超图细化（SHR）方法，在不更新模型参数、节点表示或图结构的前提下，利用属性超图对已冻结的图聚类结果进行后处理优化，仅对具有充分证据支持的节点进行微调，从而在极少改变聚类分配的情况下提升整体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图聚类方法主要通过优化模型参数和节点表示来提升性能，但对于已训练完成且参数冻结的模型，缺乏有效的后处理手段进一步改善聚类结果。

Method: 提出选择性超图细化（SHR）：构建属性超图以捕捉高阶关系，生成候选残差方向，并基于图结构、节点属性及匹配零证据评估其可靠性；仅更新具有足够支持度的节点，其余保留原始分配。

Result: 在15个骨干-数据集组合中，13个取得正向宏观增益，1个无变化，1个负向；单元均衡宏观增益为0.066个百分点（95%置信区间[0.030, 0.107]），平均仅0.209%的硬分配被修改；扩展评估显示宏观增益达0.137个百分点，平均变更率为0.375%。

Conclusion: 冻结的聚类输出仍存在有限但可测量的优化空间，优化效果因骨干-数据集组合而异，扩大覆盖范围可能增加负迁移风险。

Abstract: Existing graph-clustering methods typically improve clustering performance by optimizing model parameters and node representations. Effective means of further improving the clustering results of an already trained and frozen model, however, remain limited. We study post-processing for frozen graph clustering. After checkpoint fixation, the procedure uses no labels and updates neither model parameters, node representations, nor the original graph structure. Instead, it exploits an attribute hypergraph to supplement higher-order relations that ordinary graphs cannot readily express, thereby refining existing cluster assignments. Because global hypergraph refinement can yield both performance gains and erroneous updates, we propose Selective Hypergraph Refinement (SHR). The method generates candidate residual directions from the hypergraph and evaluates their reliability using graph structure, node attributes, and matched-null evidence. It updates only nodes with sufficient support and otherwise retains their original assignments. Further analysis shows that whether a node changes cluster is jointly governed by its native assignment gap and the directional strength of the refinement. In a controlled common-suite evaluation, 13 of 15 backbone-dataset cells had a positive mean macro gain, one produced exact no-action, and one was negative. The cell-equal macro gain was 0.066 pp (95% bootstrap CI, [0.030, 0.107] pp), while only 0.209% of hard assignments changed on average. A broader 15-combination native-interface evaluation yielded a macro gain of 0.137 pp at a mean change ratio of 0.375%. These results indicate that frozen clustering outputs retain a limited but measurable refinement space after training. The effect is heterogeneous across backbone-dataset pairs, and broader coverage also increases exposure to negative transfer.

</details>


### [302] [Latent Energy Action Planning with World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03294)
*Phu Pham,Aniket Bera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了潜在能量动作规划（LEAP）方法，通过将完整动作视界视为可微变量并在冻结的LeWorldModel上进行优化，解决了潜在世界模型中单目标优化导致的解码器预测终端描述符与目标不匹配的问题，在四个控制域中将平均成功率从77.5%提升至94.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的潜在世界模型支持从高维观测进行高效模型预测控制，但优化单一学习到的潜在目标往往会导致动作序列的解码器预测终端描述符与目标描述符不匹配，从而影响控制效果。

Method: 提出LEAP框架，将终端潜在目标匹配与终端窗口状态能量耦合。该方法利用冻结的目标条件提议初始化搜索，通过准牛顿求解器在自回归展开中优化动作，并在优化后通过投影强制执行可行动作范围，确保预测的终端潜在状态和描述符均与目标一致。

Result: 在四个控制域中使用官方发布的LeWM检查点进行实验，LEAP规划系统将平均成功率从LeWM+CEM（交叉熵方法）的77.5%提升至94.8%，实现了17.3个百分点的显著增长。

Conclusion: LEAP通过引入能量约束和高效的优化策略，有效解决了潜在空间与观测空间目标不一致的问题，在保持冻结表示和预测器不变的前提下，显著提升了模型预测控制的性能和成功率。

Abstract: Latent world models support efficient model predictive control from high-dimensional observations, yet optimizing a single learned latent objective can favor action sequences whose decoder-predicted terminal descriptor does not match the goal descriptor. We introduce Latent Energy Action Planning (LEAP), which treats the complete action horizon as a differentiable variable and optimizes it through a frozen LeWorldModel (LeWM). LEAP couples terminal latent goal matching with a terminal-window state energy. Low energy requires the predicted terminal latent to agree with the goal latent and the decoder-predicted terminal descriptor to agree with the goal descriptor. A frozen goal-conditioned proposal initializes the search, a quasi-Newton solver refines actions through the autoregressive rollout, and post-optimization projection enforces the admissible action range. Across four control domains using the officially released LeWM checkpoints, the complete LEAP planning system raises mean success from 77.5% for LeWM planned with the cross-entropy method (LeWM+CEM) to 94.8% under a matched protocol, a 17.3-percentage-point improvement, while retaining the frozen LeWM representation and predictor.

</details>


### [303] [Geometry-Aware Graph Construction via Adaptive Spectral Bandwidth Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03306)
*Ecem Bozkurt,Antonio Ortega*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自适应带宽选择方法，通过匹配核的有效秩与局部内在维度，优化核化图方法的谱复杂度，从而提升半监督学习中的分类和标签传播精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯核带宽的选择对核化图方法（如谱聚类、扩散映射等）至关重要。带宽过小会导致核高估局部复杂性，带宽过大则会导致几何区分能力丧失，因此需要一种自适应的带宽选择策略。

Method: 提出基于节点带宽的标准，通过最小生成树估计局部内在维度，并将核的有效秩与其匹配，在流形一致的 log-log 缩放范围内搜索最优带宽。

Result: 在 CIFAR-100 数据集上评估了六种编码器的 SSL 嵌入，结果显示自适应带宽方法在留一法分类和标签传播精度上优于固定带宽及其他自适应方法。

Conclusion: 自适应带宽选择能有效提升核化图方法在半监督学习中的性能，确保核的谱复杂度与底层流形的内在复杂度一致。

Abstract: Kernelized graph methods - spectral clustering, diffusion maps, and sparse kernel -regression graphs - that use Gaussian kernels depend on the choice of Gaussian bandwidth sigma, which governs the spectral character of the local kernel operator. When sigma is too small, the kernel overestimates local complexity and treats each sample as an independent direction; when sigma is too large, the kernel collapses multiple directions together, the condition number diverges, and all geometric discrimination is lost. We propose a choice of scale to make the spectral complexity of the kernel consistent with the intrinsic complexity of the underlying manifold. We propose a per-node bandwidth criterion that operationalizes this principle by jointly matching the kernel's effective rank to the local intrinsic dimension estimated via minimum spanning tree, anchoring the search in the manifold-consistent log-log scaling regime. We evaluate SSL embeddings from six encoders on CIFAR-100, showing that adaptive bandwidth consistently improves leave-one-out (LOO) classification and label propagation (LP) accuracy over fixed-bandwidth methods and competing adaptive methods.

</details>


### [304] [Risk and Anomaly Identification for Distribution Network Optimal Operation Based on Reinforcement Learning and Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03308)
*Ziqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种显式感知不确定性的深度强化学习框架，通过将总不确定性分解为偶然性（aleatoric）和认知性（epistemic）分量，分别用于表征配电网络的固有风险和分布外异常，从而优化网络运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代配电网络在普遍存在的不确定性下运行，操作员需同时识别随机但分布内条件下的固有风险，以及由异常负载、极端天气或网络物理攻击引起的分布外异常行为。现有方法难以在统一框架下有效区分并处理这两类问题。

Method: 提出了一种结合分布式和贝叶斯深度强化学习的框架，实施二阶不确定性量化方案。该方案将总不确定性分解为偶然性不确定性和认知性不确定性：前者用于刻画固有风险，后者用于驱动训练过程中的探索以及在部署阶段进行分布外检测并触发fallback控制策略。

Result: 仿真结果验证了所提出的深度强化学习智能体的性能，并证明了该不确定性量化方案在区分风险和检测异常方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过显式量化并分解不确定性，该框架能够同时实现对配电网络固有风险的准确评估和对分布外异常的及时检测，为复杂不确定环境下的电网最优运行提供了可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Reliable operation of modern distribution networks requires timely identification of operational risks and anomalous events under pervasive uncertainty. In practice, operators must identify risks that are inherent in stochastic yet in-distribution conditions, and anomalies that correspond to out-of-distribution behaviors such as unusual load patterns, extreme weather or cyber-physical attacks. This paper addresses this joint risk and anomaly identification problem for optimal distribution network operation and proposes a deep reinforcement learning framework that is explicitly uncertainty aware. We integrate distributional and Bayesian deep reinforcement learning to realize a second- order uncertainty quantification scheme that decomposes total uncertainty into aleatoric and epistemic components, which are respectively used to characterize inherent risk and out-of- distribution anomalies. The resulting epistemic estimates drive both exploration during training and out-of-distribution detec- tion with fallback control during deployment, whereas aleatoric estimates are used to characterize intrinsic operational risk. Simulation results demonstrate the performance of our DRL agent and the effectiveness of the uncertainty quantification.

</details>


### [305] [DE-Venus: A Data-Efficient RLVR Framework for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03324)
*Shenzhi Yang,Guangcheng Zhu,Kai Tang,Zhengqing Zang,Xing Zheng,Haobo Wang,Yingfan Ma,Bowen Song,Bo Han,Bo An,Lei Feng,Weiqiang Wang,Junbo Zhao,Gang Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DE-Venus 是一个统一框架，旨在解决强化学习验证奖励（RLVR）扩展中数据标注昂贵和采样效率低的问题，通过模块化处理数据选择、弱监督构建和监督细化，在仅使用 10% 标签或 13% 相关数据的情况下保持或提升模型质量，并显著减少收敛步数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 RLVR 方法在处理样本选择、不完整监督或噪声标签时往往各自为政，且常将监督逻辑与分布式训练耦合，导致难以进行受控比较和复用，同时面临高昂的在线策略滚动（on-policy rollouts）和大规模可靠目标获取成本。

Method: 提出 DE-Venus 框架，将监督视为数据准备到策略优化过程中的演化状态，划分为三个模块：主动数据选择（分配预算）、弱监督构建（从无标签数据推导信号）和训练时监督细化（过滤或修正不可靠监督）。该框架通过将特定方法的决策表达为离线数据集转换或在线目标/奖励/批次/优势变换，兼容 verl 的分布式执行协议。

Result: 在公共基准和三个商业场景中，DE-Venus 的不同配置仅用 10% 的标签或 13% 的相关数据即可保持或提升模型质量；选定的商业配置将观测到的收敛步数减少了 63% 至 75%。

Conclusion: DE-Venus 通过统一且模块化的数据高效 RLVR 流程，在不牺牲可扩展强化学习执行的前提下，显著降低了标注成本和训练成本。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves large language model reasoning, but its practical scaling is constrained by expensive on-policy rollouts and the cost of obtaining reliable targets at scale. Existing methods address sample selection, incomplete supervision, or noisy labels separately, often entangling supervision logic with distributed training and hindering controlled comparison and reuse. We present DE-Venus, a unified framework for data-efficient RLVR that treats supervision as evolving state across data preparation and policy optimization. It organizes this lifecycle into three modules: Active Data Selection allocates training and annotation budgets; Weak Supervision Construction derives learning signals from unlabeled examples; and Training-Time Supervision Refinement filters or corrects unreliable supervision. DE-Venus supports seven representative methods and a data-selection pipeline by expressing method-specific decisions as offline dataset transitions or online transformations of targets, rewards, batches, and advantages while preserving verl's distributed execution contracts. Across public benchmarks and three business scenarios, separate configurations preserve or improve model quality with only 10% of labels or as little as 13% of relevant data; selected business configurations also reduce observed convergence steps by 63%--75%. DE-Venus thus reduces annotation and training costs without sacrificing scalable RL execution.

</details>


### [306] [A Large Open Multi-Energy Corpus of Soil Compaction Tests, with Machine-Learning Baselines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03337)
*Sompote Youwai,Chana Phutthananon,Warat Kongkitkul*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发布了一个包含2,854个实验室压实测试的大型数据集，涵盖162个来源组和4个Proctor能量水平，并提出了一个表格基础模型来预测最大干密度和最优含水率，R²分别为0.824和0.784。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的压实数据相关性研究通常基于少量样本（100-400个），且多来自单一实验室和单一压实能量，数据很少公开。本文旨在发布一个不受这些限制的大规模数据集，并提供更可靠的预测模型。

Method: 收集并审核了来自6个公共来源的2,854个实验室压实测试数据，涵盖162个来源组和4个Proctor能量水平。通过零空隙条件筛选去除11.8%的记录。使用表格基础模型进行预测，并通过符号回归得到封闭形式，确保预测值不超过零空隙线。

Result: 最优饱和度为0.815，变异系数为11%。表格基础模型在随机折叠下对密度和含水率的预测R²分别为0.824和0.784；按来源分组折叠时为0.727和0.696；留出整个来源时为0.520和0.614。压实能量在条件性预测中起决定性作用。

Conclusion: 该数据集和模型可用于压实数据的筛选而非验收。预测结果需满足零空隙条件，压实能量在特定条件下对密度预测有显著影响。

Abstract: Every engineered fill is specified by a maximum dry density and an optimum moisture content. Each determination needs a full Proctor test. Published correlations rest on one to four hundred specimens, usually from one laboratory at one compactive energy, and are seldom released. This paper releases a corpus without those limits. It holds 2,854 laboratory compaction tests from six public sources, across 162 provenance groups and four Proctor energy levels, with fines from 1.5 to 100%. Every record is audited to the Proctor method its source names, and no energy is inferred. Screening on the zero-air-voids condition removed 11.8% of harmonised records, and 5.7% of those with a measured specific gravity. A material share of published compaction data is physically impossible. The optimum degree of saturation over the corpus is 0.815 at a coefficient of variation of 11%. That is a baseline, not a constant. Both parameters are then estimated from one classification suite and the compaction standard. A tabular foundation model reaches R2 0.824 for density and 0.784 for water content under random folds. It reaches 0.727 and 0.696 with folds drawn around provenance, and 0.520 and 0.614 with a whole source held out. Compactive energy is negligible marginally yet decisive conditionally. Density on the 66 modified-Proctor records is predicted at R2 0.740 with it and -0.651 without. Symbolic regression yields closed forms coupled through a phase relation. No predicted pair can then exceed the zero-air-voids line. The predictions are for screening, not acceptance.

</details>


### [307] [Gradients Know What Outcomes Don't: Unlocking Reinforcement Learning for LLM Reasoning with Gradient-Aligned Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03342)
*Leqi Zheng,Jinbo Su,Fang Niu,Chaokun Wang,Weiping Wang,Jiajun Zhang,Shannan Yan,Jie Wu,Zhaolu Kang,Rong Fu,Hang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出梯度对齐奖励（GAR），利用策略梯度空间与专家梯度的余弦相似度构建密集奖励，以低成本解决RLVR中二元奖励无法区分正确推理轨迹的问题，并在数学基准测试中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）仅能提供二元结果奖励，无法区分不同的正确推理轨迹；而现有的密集奖励替代方案（如表面启发式或过程奖励模型）要么忽略了训练语料中已有的专家解，要么需要昂贵的离线标注。

Method: 提出梯度对齐奖励（GAR），在策略自身的梯度空间中操作：通过对输出投影层进行截断反向传播，提取每个rollout的紧凑梯度向量，并计算其与专家锚点梯度的余弦相似度，从而生成具有推理感知能力的密集奖励。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B和Qwen3-8B模型上，GAR在竞赛级数学基准测试中一致优于GRPO及其他基线方法，并能无需特定领域数据地迁移至GPQA Diamond和MMLU-Pro；该方法带来的额外时间开销低于9%。

Conclusion: GAR通过证明余弦相似度可分解为预测误差和激活模式因子，具体刻画了对齐信号的测量内容，提供了一种高效、无需额外标注且能利用现有专家数据的密集奖励机制，显著提升了大模型的链式推理能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) drives chain-of-thought reasoning in large language models, yet its binary outcome reward cannot distinguish among correct trajectories. Existing dense reward alternatives, from surface heuristics to process reward models, either ignore the expert solutions already present in training corpora or require expensive offline annotation. We propose Gradient-Aligned Reward (GAR), which operates in the policy's own gradient space: truncated backpropagation through the output projection layer extracts a compact gradient vector for each rollout, and cosine similarity with an expert-anchor gradient yields a dense, reasoning-aware reward with less than 9% wall-clock overhead. We prove that this cosine admits a multiplicative decomposition into prediction-error and activation-pattern factors, providing a concrete characterization of what the alignment signal measures. On Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-8B, GAR consistently improves over GRPO and other baselines on competition-level math benchmarks and transfers to GPQA Diamond and MMLU-Pro without domain-specific data. Code and data are available at https://github.com/LQgdwind/GAR.

</details>


### [308] [From Zero to Hero: An Open LLM Ecosystem for Armenian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03350)
*Erik Arakelyan,Khatun Avetisyan,Meri Davtyan,Heghine Grigoryan,Nane Khachatryan,Hayk Shahsuvaryan,Henrik Sergoyan,Vahan Martirosyan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对亚美尼亚语（一种形态丰富但资源稀缺的语言）缺乏公开大语言模型（LLM）及复现数据的问题，构建了ArmWeb（437万篇新闻文档）和ArmSTEM（37.3万道英亚平行数理问题）两个数据集。通过在Gemma-4-E4B上进行持续预训练，推出了首个具备完整训练数据和配方且开源的亚美尼亚语模型arm-gemma-e4b，其性能优于现有所有开源亚美尼亚模型及未适配的基座模型。研究还发现，仅使用新闻数据预训练虽提升流畅度但损害知识，而引入少量经验证的翻译STEM数据可逆转这一损失，并揭示了现有公共语料库与评估集存在严重重叠的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 亚美尼亚语作为一种形态丰富且低资源的语言，其预训练数据稀缺，且此前未有公开可用的亚美尼亚语大语言模型及其配套数据和训练配方，导致该领域的复现和研究受阻。

Method: 构建并发布了两个数据集：ArmWeb（经广泛验证的437万篇亚美尼亚语新闻文档语料库）和ArmSTEM（37.3万道英亚平行数理问题，包含逐步解答，经答案保持的LLM判断和人工评估双重验证）。在此基础上，对Gemma-4-E4B模型进行持续预训练（Continued Pretraining），并通过消融实验分析不同数据成分（纯新闻数据 vs. 混合STEM数据）对模型性能的影响。

Result: 生成的arm-gemma-e4b模型在性能上超越了所有现有的开源亚美尼亚语模型以及未适配的基座模型。消融实验表明，仅使用新闻数据进行持续预训练会提高流畅度但侵蚀知识储备，而加入少量经验证的翻译STEM数据可有效逆转知识损失。此外，研究发现最大的公共亚美尼亚语语料库与基于网络的评估面板存在严重重叠，FineWeb-2内部甚至存在训练/测试自重叠现象。

Conclusion: 该研究成功填补了亚美尼亚语LLM领域的空白，发布了首个拥有完整训练数据和配方的开源亚美尼亚语模型。研究结果强调了在低资源语言建模中，高质量、经验证的特定领域数据（如STEM）对于平衡流畅度与知识保留的关键作用，并警示了现有语料库与评估集重叠带来的评估偏差风险。所有数据、模型和代码均已开源。

Abstract: Pretraining data for Armenian, a morphologically rich and low-resource language, is scarce, and no open Armenian LLM has been released with the data and recipe needed to reproduce it. To address this gap, we curate and release two datasets. ArmWeb is an extensively validated corpus of 4.37M Armenian news documents. ArmSTEM is a parallel English-Armenian collection of 373K math and science problems with step-by-step solutions, translated into Armenian and verified through both answer-preserving LLM judgment and human evaluation. Continued pretraining of Gemma-4-E4B on these datasets yields arm-gemma-e4b, which outperforms every existing open Armenian model as well as its unadapted base, and is the first open Armenian LLM with complete training data and recipe. Our ablations show that news-only continued pretraining improves fluency while eroding knowledge, a pattern we also observe in existing Armenian models, and that a small share of verified translated STEM data reverses the loss. We further find that the largest public Armenian corpora overlap web-derived evaluation panels heavily, including a train/test self-overlap inside FineWeb-2. We openly release all data, models, and code.

</details>


### [309] [Time Without Timesteps: Simulating Coupled Dynamical Systems via Self-Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03358)
*Liyu Zerihun,Mark Shinyoung Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于神经代理和波形松弛的耦合动力系统仿真新方法，将时间步进仿真转化为全轨迹的不动点问题，显著减少求解迭代次数并实现内存无关的梯度计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的动力系统数值仿真通常采用因果时间步进方式，导致计算深度大、梯度回溯内存消耗高，难以高效处理耦合系统。

Method: 为每类子系统训练神经代理，将完整驱动轨迹和初始条件直接映射为完整输出轨迹；利用经典波形松弛思想，通过强制轨迹间自一致性组装耦合系统，将仿真转化为全轨迹上的不动点问题；采用牛顿迭代求解，并使用GMRES求解线性系统计算隐式梯度。

Result: 在耦合van der Pol振子和Hodgkin-Huxley神经元网络上，仅需4-10次牛顿迭代（对比传统积分器1500步）；梯度计算不再依赖时间递归，内存消耗与求解深度无关；雅可比矩阵谱半径可预测收敛性，超出边界时传统反向传播发散而隐式梯度仍保持0.04%精度。

Conclusion: 该方法在特定条件下显著提升了耦合系统仿真的效率与梯度计算稳定性，但代理误差可能限制其适用范围，需进一步评估其成功与失效边界。

Abstract: Numerical simulation of dynamical systems is usually organized as a causal march through time: each state is computed from the previous one. We explore a different formulation for coupled systems. For each subsystem type we train a neural surrogate mapping a full driving trajectory and initial condition directly to a full output trajectory; following classical waveform relaxation, coupled systems are assembled by enforcing self-consistency among these trajectories: simulation becomes a fixed-point problem over complete trajectories rather than a stepwise rollout. On coupled van der Pol oscillators and Hodgkin-Huxley neuron networks, sequential depth becomes the number of solver iterations: 4-10 Newton iterations where the reference integrator takes 1500 steps. The gradient likewise loses its time recursion: it becomes a linear system solved by GMRES at memory independent of solver depth. A single scalar measured from the learned operator, the spectral radius of its Jacobian, predicts in advance where the coupled solve will converge; past that boundary, unrolled backpropagation diverges and a Neumann adjoint fails, while the implicit gradient remains correct to 0.04%. We report where the approach succeeds and where surrogate error degrades it.

</details>


### [310] [SimpleDesign: A Joint Model for Protein Sequence and Structure Codesign](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03377)
*Jiarui Lu,Yuyang Wang,Yizhe Zhang,Jiatao Gu,Navdeep Jaitly,Joshua M. Susskind,Miguel Ángel Bautista*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了SimpleDesign，一种无需多阶段训练、直接在数据空间进行端到端训练的多模态蛋白质设计模型，通过混合Transformer架构有效协同生成蛋白质序列与结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蛋白质生成模型通常依赖繁琐的多阶段训练流程（先训练自编码器进行潜在空间表征，再在潜在空间训练生成模型），作者假设这种多阶段过程对于获得高性能的协同设计模型并非必要，旨在简化流程并直接在数据空间建模。

Method: 提出SimpleDesign模型，采用单阶段端到端目标函数，结合针对序列的离散交叉熵损失和针对结构的回归损失；架构上设计了一种混合Transformer（Mixture-of-Transformer），在保持全局自注意力的同时允许对序列和结构模态进行特异性处理。

Result: 在超过200万对序列 - 结构数据对上进行训练，该模型在协同设计（co-design）以及无条件序列/结构生成的基准测试中均表现出强劲的性能。

Conclusion: SimpleDesign证明了直接在数据空间进行单阶段端到端训练的有效性，成功克服了多模态差异，为药物发现和蛋白质工程提供了一种高效且高性能的生成建模新范式。

Abstract: Proteins are fundamental to biological processes, with their function determined by the complex interplay between the amino acid sequence and the three-dimensional structure. Developing generative models capable of understanding this intrinsically multi-modal relationship is crucial for fields like drug discovery and protein engineering. Existing models often rely on a multi-stage training process where autoencoders that tokenize data into latent representations are trained in a first stage. Secondly, a generative model is trained on the latent representation of the autoencoder(s), i.e., generative modeling in a latent space. We hypothesize that this multi-stage training is not necessary to obtain performant co-design models and thus present SimpleDesign, an effective multi-modal protein design model trained directly in the data space. SimpleDesign leverages a single-stage end-to-end objective that combines discrete cross-entropy for sequences and a regression objective for structures. In order to effectively model the difference in sequence and structure modalities, we develop a Mixture-of-Transformer architecture that allows modality-specific processing while keeping global self-attention over both modalities. We train SimpleDesign on over 2M sequence-structure pairs achieving strong performance across co-design and unconditional sequence/structure generation benchmarks.

</details>


### [311] [RecurTrace: Adaptive Latent Reasoning with Loop-Time Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03379)
*Yuxiang Wang,Kunyu Feng,Yingda Shen,Haoning Xu,Junyu Wang,Zhizheng Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RecurTrace 通过引入循环记忆注意力和提前停止机制，优化了语言模型的潜在递归结构，在相同计算预算下提升了推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的潜在递归方法存在两个主要限制：每次迭代只能看到前一次输出而无法访问更早的计算状态，且固定循环次数对简单输入浪费计算资源，对复杂输入计算不足。

Method: 提出 Loop Memory Attention 使循环层能沿循环时间轴访问自身历史状态，并引入停止头根据 Oracle 监督预测是否继续循环。

Result: 在 MathQA 任务上达到 56.9% 准确率（平均 2.0 次循环），优于固定循环深度基线 2.2 个点，且在 0.6B 至 8B 模型规模上均优于同预算微调基线。

Conclusion: RecurTrace 通过利用循环轨迹有效解决了现有递归设计的局限性，在匹配计算量下实现了更好的推理性能和可扩展性。

Abstract: Repeating a small block of middle layers increases a language model's effective inference depth without adding parameters or generating extra tokens, and recent work shows that this latent recurrence improves reasoning. However, two design choices limit these gains. Each iteration sees only the previous output and cannot directly access earlier computations. Moreover, a fixed loop count wastes depth on easy inputs while leaving hard ones with too little computation. We introduce RecurTrace, which addresses both limitations using the loop's own trajectory. Specifically, Loop Memory Attention lets each looped layer attend to its own states from previous iterations along the loop-time axis, so the model can revisit earlier computations instead of relying on the latest state alone. A halting head then reads the loop state and predicts whether to continue, with supervision from an oracle that identifies when additional depth still reduces loss. In a controlled MathQA comparison on the same looped backbone, RecurTrace achieves 56.9% accuracy with an average of 2.0 loops, exceeding the best fixed loop depth by 2.2 points at matched compute. By comparison, ACT and PonderNet collapse to one loop, and CALM reaches only 54.1% with 5.6 loops, while the stronger LoopUS-Conf and TaH-Mismatch baselines reach 55.3% at 3.2 loops and 55.7% at 2.1 loops. Finally, RecurTrace improves generation accuracy over same-budget fine-tuned baselines at 0.6B, 1.7B, 4B, and 8B, with the gain growing with model size from 0.6 to 3.4 points.

</details>


### [312] [TIGPO: Temporal Instance-Graph Policy Optimization for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03383)
*Jinwei Gan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TIGPO方法，通过维护持久化状态转移图并引入“重访”机制，将基于图的信用分配扩展至不同策略更新阶段，显著提升了长程LLM智能体的训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图的策略优化方法仅在单次策略更新内独立构建图，丢弃了早期策略发现的转移信息，且优势估计局限于小规模的局部滚动组，限制了长程任务中的信用分配效率。

Method: 提出时序实例图策略优化（TIGPO）：1) 为每个任务维护持久化转移图，整合不同策略版本发现的有效转移；2) 分配固定滚动预算，区分“探索”槽位（普通采样）与“重访”槽位（延迟重试已探索任务）；3) 将当前滚动组与历史探索组配对构建跨时序参考，以稳定优势估计并捕捉策略改进，但历史数据仅作为结构参考而不直接用于策略损失回放。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop基准测试中，TIGPO的表现一致优于先前的基于组和基于图的策略优化方法。

Conclusion: TIGPO通过跨时序整合历史转移信息与主动重访机制，有效解决了长程LLM智能体训练中的信用分配瓶颈，实现了更稳定的优势估计和显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Graph-based policy optimization improves credit assignment for long-horizon LLM agents by organizing rollout trajectories into state-transition graphs. However, existing methods construct graphs independently within each policy update, discarding transitions discovered by earlier policies and limiting advantage estimation to small, batch-local rollout groups. We propose \emph{Temporal Instance-Graph Policy Optimization} (TIGPO), which extends graph-based credit assignment across policy updates. TIGPO maintains a persistent transition graph for each task, allowing valid transitions discovered by different policy versions to jointly determine credit for current rollouts. To actively reconnect current exploration with historical experience, TIGPO allocates a fixed rollout budget between Exploration slots for ordinary task sampling and Revisit slots for delayed reattempts of previously explored tasks. For each revisit, TIGPO pairs the current rollout group with its corresponding earlier Exploration group to construct a cross-temporal reference. The enlarged reference is designed to stabilize relative advantage estimation under small rollout groups, while comparison on the same task directly captures policy improvement across training stages. Historical transitions and scores serve only as structural and detached statistical references and are never replayed in the policy loss. Experiments on ALFWorld and WebShop demonstrate that TIGPO consistently outperforms prior group-based and graph-based policy optimization methods.

</details>


### [313] [Inferred Generative-Process Diversity Predicts Correlated Failure Across Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03422)
*Ross Tieman,Evan Markou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“生成过程多样性”概念，利用算法信息论中的归一化压缩距离（NCD）量化语言模型间的差异，发现该指标比语义相似性更能有效预测模型对的关联失败风险，表明增加生成过程多样性可降低多模型系统的关联失败概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注语言模型输出的语义多样性，但忽略了生成这些输出的底层过程差异。在由多个语言模型组成的集体系统中，即使模型行为看似独立，其失败模式仍可能高度相关。仅依靠语义相似性无法捕捉这种深层的关联风险，因此需要一种更根本的多样性度量方法来评估系统韧性。

Method: 基于算法信息论，采用归一化压缩距离（Normalised Compression Distance, NCD）作为衡量“生成过程多样性”的指标。该方法直接作用于模型的原始输出，并针对排列控制进行残差化处理，以推断生成过程的差异。研究在38个语言模型和10个不重叠的基准测试族上进行了评估，并对比了语义相似性和模型能力指标。

Result: 实验显示，生成过程多样性指标识别出了语义相似性所遗漏的群体结构。在预测跨任务的关联失败（chance-corrected correlated failure）方面，该指标优于语义相似性和模型对能力。跨基准的部分秩关联系数为-0.216（95%置信区间为[-0.309, -0.122]），且在所有十个基准上均为负值，表明生成过程多样性越高，模型对的关联失败率越低。

Conclusion: 推断出的生成过程多样性提供了一种新颖且实用的方法，用于调查多模型系统在安全关键背景下的多样性。增加生成过程的多样性与减少模型对之间的关联失败显著相关，且这种效应不能归因于语义相似性或模型能力，为构建更具韧性的多模型系统提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Diversity is a widely observed factor in the resilient function of collective systems, yet the type of diversity that matters depends on the properties and failure modes of the system. This distinction is important for systems composed of multiple language models. Different models may be treated as independent components even when their behaviour and failures remain strongly correlated. Assessments of language-model populations using semantic similarity demonstrate limited semantic diversity, but this captures only differences in the meaning of observed outputs. We argue that a more fundamental notion of model diversity is generative-process diversity, the differences between processes capable of generating the observed outputs. Drawing from Algorithmic Information Theory, we use Normalised Compression Distance between raw model outputs, residualised against a permutation control, as a measure of inferred generative-process diversity. Across 38 language models, this measure identifies population structure missed by semantic similarity and predicts cross-task variation in chance-corrected correlated failure among model pairs across ten disjoint benchmark families, beyond semantic similarity and model-pair capability. The cross-benchmark partial rank association is $-0.216$ with a 95% interval of $[-0.309,-0.122]$, and the estimate is negative on all ten benchmarks. These results indicate that increased generative-process diversity is associated with reduced correlated failure in model pairs that is not attributable to semantic similarity or capability. Inferred generative-process diversity offers a novel and practical approach for investigating diversity of multi-model systems in safety-relevant contexts.

</details>


### [314] [TraveL: Transformer-based Multi-view Path Distributional Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03427)
*Fang He,Tao-yang Fu,Wang-chien Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Transformer的多视图分布表示学习框架（TraveL），通过捕捉多样化的旅行者行为和路段间的区域相关性，将路径及出发时间编码为分布表示，在旅行时间分布估计、路径相似度预测及目的地预测任务上均优于现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的路径表示学习（PRL）工作通常仅利用路段与路径的共现关系学习向量表示，忽略了旅行者行为的多样性以及路径中路段的区域相关性，导致无法充分捕捉路径相关的复杂依赖信息。

Method: 提出TraveL框架：1) 利用Transformer架构将路径和出发时间编码为分布表示，以解码可能的旅行者行为样本；2) 引入区域注意力机制（regional attention）以编码路段间的区域相关性；3) 采用Kolmogorov-Smirnov (K-S) 检验比较采样行为与真实数据，以辅助模型训练。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上的实验表明，TraveL模型在旅行时间分布估计（Mean K-S距离降低14.7%）、路径相似度预测（MAE降低16.7%）和目的地预测（MAE降低3.97%）三个任务上均显著优于最先进的方法。

Conclusion: 通过捕捉多样化的旅行者行为和区域相关性学习分布表示，能有效提升路径表示学习在多种下游应用中的性能，证明了TraveL框架的有效性和优越性。

Abstract: Path representation learning (PRL) for road networks has received increasing research attention, due to various path-related applications. Existing works on PRL typically exploit the co-occurrence relationship among road segments and paths to learn a vector as the path representation, without exploring the varied traveler behaviors and the regional correlation on the path. In this work, we propose to learn distributional representations, which provide valuable information for use in path-related applications, by capturing the varied traveler behaviors as well as the various dependencies within regions of road segments. We propose a novel Transformer-based Multi-view Distributional Representation Learning (TraveL) framework to encode a path along with a travel starting time to a distributional representation, which can be used to decode possible samples of on-path traveler behavior. Moreover, by analyzing the regional correlation which reveals various road segment relationships, we propose a regional attention to encode these correlations in a path. Also, we explore the idea of Kolmogorov-Smirnov (K-S) test to compare the sampled traveler behavior against the collected ground truth to facilitate training. Experimental results show that the proposed TraveL model outperforms the state-of-the-art methods on both synthetic and real-world datasets, by 14.7% in Mean K-S distance for travel time distribution estimation, 16.7% in Mean Absolute Error (MAE) for path similarity prediction, and 3.97% in MAE for destination prediction.

</details>


### [315] [It's the Problem, Not the Path: Budget and Difficulty Confounds in LLM Reasoning Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03436)
*Yigit Utku Bulut*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过引入反事实对照（重启控制），挑战了关于大语言模型推理轨迹中存在“突破时刻”或“早期命运”的普遍观点。研究发现，继续生成通常优于重启主要是因为计算压缩而非能力扩展，且早期内部信号在控制问题难度后并未包含可检测的结局信息，先前的高预测准确率主要源于问题难度差异而非真正的早期预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献广泛认为大语言模型的推理轨迹包含决定性的“突破”时刻或可早期预测的结局，但这些结论缺乏在声明层面必要的反事实对照（counterfactual control）。本研究旨在填补这一空白，通过严格的对照实验来验证这些“突破”是真实的认知飞跃，还是仅仅是计算资源分配或问题难度差异的产物。

Method: 研究采用了两种主要方法：1) **重启控制截断探测**：比较在匹配总生成token预算下，基于前缀继续生成的解决率与从头重启的解决率，以此区分模型是受限于计算资源还是能力上限；2) **预注册的难度控制测试**：在控制问题难度的基线上，检测早期窗口内部信号是否包含额外的结局信息，并辅以无生成分析来验证仅基于问题难度的代理变量是否能复现先前的高预测准确率。

Result: 1) 在178个问题 - 模型单元格中，仅1个表现出前缀限制；在匹配预算内，继续前缀生成在9/9的情况下优于重启，表明主要是“计算压缩”效应。2) 难度控制测试显示，早期窗口信号中无可检测的结局信息；仅基于问题难度的代理变量在192K次生成中达到了0.873的AUROC，复现了先前研究的阳性结果，但在问题内部评估中，其表现与随机猜测无异（t=4时为0.496）。

Conclusion: 高 pooled probe AUROC 值本身不足以证明单次尝试内的早期信息存在；必须引入仅基于问题的基线或问题内部评估作为对照。研究表明，所谓的“早期命运”很大程度上是问题难度差异的反映，而非模型推理过程中的内在早期信号，未来的研究必须包含严格的反事实控制以避免误判。

Abstract: Reasoning traces of large language models are widely read as containing "breakthrough" moments and early-legible fates. Both readings rest on measurements missing a counterfactual control at the level of the claim; we supply both controls. First, a restart-controlled truncation probe separates when a solution fits the continuation budget from when a prefix carries value that fresh computation cannot buy, comparing per-anchor continuation solve rates against from-scratch restart curves at matched total generated-token budget. Applied to 178 problem-model cells (89 MATH problems x two small open models, an outcome-blind but difficulty-targeted cohort), exactly 1 of 178 cells survives as prefix-limited; restart dose-response separates a compute-starved model from a capability-limited one; and wherever the matched budget lies inside the restart grid, continuing the model's own prefix beats restarting (9 of 9) -- predominantly compute compression rather than expanded reachability. Second, a pre-registered, difficulty-controlled test finds no detectable outcome information in early-window internal signals beyond a problem-difficulty baseline, and two generation-free analyses of public corpora show why this control is needed: a trace-blind difficulty proxy reaches AUROC 0.873 on 192K DeepSeek-R1 generations -- inside the published probe range -- and a closely matched reconstruction of the closest published early-window positive recovers a comparable pooled result (0.849) while within problem it is statistically indistinguishable from chance at all ten anchors (0.496 at t=4); a post-hoc within-targeted probe finds only a small average residual, concentrated in three low-failure problems. High pooled probe AUROCs cannot by themselves establish within-attempt information; a question-only baseline or within-problem evaluation is required.

</details>


### [316] [Guide, Not Bind: Why Defeasible Priors Fail in Augmented Lagrangian Causal Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03442)
*Sairam Sundararaman,Sara Girdhar,Manit Narasimha Murthy,Samrudh N,Bhaskarjyoti Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出可微因果发现中“引导而非绑定”（guide, not bind）的先验约束设计存在两个根本性缺陷：自适应松弛机制会在反事实检查前错误抑制真实边，且标准相关匹配目标因丢弃方差信息导致方向不可识别；提出DADU松弛规则并证明其失效，同时论证协方差匹配可解决方向识别问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可微因果发现方法通常通过增广拉格朗日（ALM）惩罚将专家先验编码为禁边约束，并假设数据自适应松弛机制能自动修正与数据矛盾的错误先验。然而，该“引导而非绑定”的设计假设缺乏理论验证，本文旨在揭示其失效的根本原因。

Method: 1. 理论分析：提出自适应松弛必须满足的三个必要条件，证明新提出的DADU松弛规则违反所有条件；2. 数学推导：通过闭式解证明标准相关匹配目标导致正向边与反向边成本相同（$2r^2$），而协方差匹配可利用方差信息产生至少$w_0^4$的分离边界；3. 实验验证：在4至32节点的图上进行3,072次训练运行，量化错误先验对真实边的抑制率。

Result: 1. DADU松弛机制在87%-97%的试验中因顺序惩罚递增而错误抑制真实边；2. 理论证明标准相关匹配目标因归一化丢弃方差信息，导致因果方向不可识别（成本相同）；3. 协方差匹配目标能利用方差信息有效区分因果方向，提供可证明的分离边界。

Conclusion: “引导而非绑定”的设计范式因松弛机制缺陷和目标函数信息丢失而失效，仅修复机制只能部分恢复性能；必须同时采用满足特定条件的自适应松弛策略并改用协方差匹配目标，才能有效利用专家先验并确保因果方向的可识别性。

Abstract: Differentiable causal discovery methods increasingly encode expert priors as forbidden-edge constraints enforced by an Augmented Lagrangian (ALM) penalty, on the assumption that a data-adaptive relaxation mechanism will discount and eventually override a rule the data consistently contradicts. We show this design, which we call \emph{guide, not bind}, fails for two independent, precisely characterized reasons, and that directly repairing both restores it only partially. First, sequential penalty-ramping ALM suppresses a wrongly-forbidden true edge before any counterfactual check can detect it: we give three necessary conditions any adaptive relaxation must satisfy to avoid this (Proposition~\ref{prop:conditions}), prove that DADU---the natural relaxation rule this paper introduces as the object of study---violates all three (Corollary~\ref{cor:dadu_failure}), and confirm the failure across 3{,}072 training runs spanning graphs from 4 to 32 nodes, where a single wrong prior suppresses a true edge in 87--97\% of trials under DADU. Second, and independent of any fix to the mechanism, we prove in closed form that the standard correlation-matching objective ties a true edge and its reverse to an identical cost of exactly $2r^2$ (Lemma~\ref{lem:tie}), not because the underlying equal-variance model is unidentifiable, but because normalizing to correlation discards exactly the variance information that would make it identifiable; covariance matching instead separates the two directions by a provable margin of at least $w_0^4$ (Lemma~\ref{lem:separation}).

</details>


### [317] [Beyond Straightness: Non-Crossing Flow Matching via Quantile AlignTree Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03443)
*Junyi Lin,Mengyu Li,Jingxuan Hu,Kejun He,Cheng Meng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 QAT-FM 方法，通过分位数对齐树结构实现高效结构化耦合，解决 Flow Matching 中路径交叉和速度模糊问题，降低构建成本并提升生成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Flow Matching 的性能依赖于源分布与目标分布的耦合质量，但独立耦合易导致路径交叉和局部速度模糊，而最优传输（OT）耦合构建成本过高，需寻找高效且高质量的耦合策略。

Method: 提出 Quantile AlignTree Flow Matching（QAT-FM），利用分位数对齐树结构在高斯先验与目标数据分布间构建分层耦合，实现 O(Nd log N) 时间复杂度的耦合构建和 O(d) 复杂度的每对源采样。

Result: 理论证明 QAT 耦合满足边缘一致性、诱导非交叉线性插值路径并改善中间时刻路径分离度；实验表明在多个基准数据集上实现有竞争力的生成性能，同时显著降低耦合构建成本。

Conclusion: QAT-FM 提供了一种高效结构化耦合策略，可扩展至条件生成任务，在保持全局高斯对齐的同时实现结构化条件耦合，有效平衡性能与计算效率。

Abstract: The performance of Flow Matching largely depends on the quality of the coupling between the source and target distributions. However, independent coupling often leads to path crossings and local velocity ambiguity, while OT-based couplings typically incur high construction costs. To address this challenge, we propose Quantile AlignTree Flow Matching (QAT-FM), an efficient structured coupling strategy that constructs a hierarchical coupling between a Gaussian prior and the target data distribution via a quantile-aligned tree structure. QAT-FM constructs the coupling in $\mathcal{O}(Nd\log N)$ time and supports per-pair source sampling with $\mathcal{O}(d)$ complexity, enabling scalable training for large-scale high-dimensional generative tasks. Theoretically, we prove that the QAT coupling satisfies marginal consistency, induces non-crossing linear interpolation paths, and consistently improves path separation at intermediate times compared with independent coupling, thereby alleviating local velocity ambiguity. QAT-FM further extends naturally to conditional generation, enabling structured conditional coupling while preserving global Gaussian alignment. Experiments across diverse benchmark datasets demonstrate that QAT-FM achieves competitive generative performance while substantially reducing coupling construction cost.

</details>


### [318] [A Two-Stage Forecasting System for CPU Workload Prediction in Private Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03457)
*Ashir Javeed,Anton Borg,Håkan Grahn,Lars Lundberg,Dhyey Patel,Sogand Shirinbab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种两阶段集成预测模型，通过先预测客户请求量（TPS）再预测CPU负载，结合XGBoost和自适应在线重训练，在真实云环境中实现了高精度资源预测（SMAPE<7%），优于传统直接预测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有云资源预测方法多直接从历史资源轨迹预测CPU负载，忽略了客户服务需求与后续资源消耗之间的关系，导致预测精度和可解释性不足。

Method: 采用两阶段集成预测模型：第一阶段预测每秒事务数（TPS），第二阶段基于TPS预测CPU负载；两个阶段均使用XGBoost模型，采用级联学习架构，并结合扩展窗口策略的自适应在线重训练以应对概念漂移。

Result: 在包含10个应用的私有云真实轨迹上评估，大多数应用SMAPE低于7%，最佳应用MAE为0.7372、RMSE为1.1866、SMAPE为3.57%、R²为0.9185；60步预测范围内误差累积受控，预测行为稳定。

Conclusion: 相比传统直接CPU预测方法，该两阶段模型在预测鲁棒性、计算效率和可解释性方面表现更优，适用于云环境中的主动资源管理和智能自动扩展。

Abstract: Accurate cloud resource forecasting is essential for proactive resource provisioning, maintaining Quality of Service (QoS), and reducing operational costs in dynamic cloud environments. The existing forecasting approaches predominantly estimate future CPU workload directly from historical resource traces, which often overlook the relationship between customer service demand and subsequent resource consumption. This study proposes a two-stage integrated forecasting model that explicitly models this dependency by first forecasting customer service requests, expressed as Transactions Per Second (TPS), and subsequently estimating future CPU workload from the TPS forecast. Both the forecasting component and resource prediction component employed the XGBoost model within a cascaded learning architecture, complemented by adaptive online retraining using an expanding-window strategy to address concept drift in continuously evolving cloud workloads. The proposed work was evaluated using real-world traces collected from a private cloud environment comprising ten applications. Experimental results demonstrate robust forecasting performance by achieving Symmetric Mean Absolute Percentage Error (SMAPE) below $7\%$ for most applications, with the best-performing application achieving an MAE of $0.7372$, RMSE of $1.1866$, SMAPE of $3.57\%$, and an R2 of $0.9185$. Horizon-wise drift analysis confirmed stable recursive forecasting behavior with controlled error accumulation across a 60-step prediction horizon. Compared with the conventional direct CPU forecasting method, the proposed two-stage integrated model gives improved forecasting robustness, computational efficiency, and interpretability, making it well-suited for proactive resource management and intelligent auto-scaling in cloud computing environments.

</details>


### [319] [Mind the Gap: Robustness Risks in PII Detection Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03464)
*Adeel Zafar,Slawomir Nowaczyk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 现有PII检测系统在标准基准上表现良好，但在现实分布偏移下鲁棒性不足；不同架构（编码器、规则、LLM）有互补的失败模式；论文提出混合检测流水线并发布新基准以支持OOD评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代PII检测系统虽然在标准基准测试中报告了强劲的性能，但这些评估掩盖了在实际部署中遇到的自然分布偏移（如噪声、非结构化和非正式输入）下的严重鲁棒性失败，导致潜在的隐私和安全风险。

Method: 构建了一个涵盖七类自然分布偏移的压力测试基准，评估了三种广泛部署的架构家族的代表性系统：基于编码器的NER（SpaCy）、基于规则的混合检测（Presidio）和生成式LLM提取（Qwen2.5-3B）。

Result: 所有三种系统在分布外（OOD）输入上均表现出显著性能下降，但失败模式不同且互补：编码器模型主要在未见过的表面形式和边界检测上失败；基于规则的系统在非标准格式上失败；LLM则表现出实体类型混淆和生成不稳定性。

Conclusion: 聚合基准分数掩盖了对部署至关重要的弱点，没有任何单一架构在所有PII类别中 uniformly 可靠；据此提出了一种带有QA驱动反馈循环的混合检测流水线以进行迭代风险缓解，并发布了该基准以支持对PII系统的OOD感知评估。

Abstract: Personally Identifiable Information (PII) detection is a foundational component of data protection infrastructure where missed entities constitute direct privacy and security risks. Although modern PII systems report strong performance on standard benchmarks, we show that these evaluations mask substantial robustness failures under realistic distribution shifts encountered in deployment. Rather than comparing state-of-the-art accuracy, we study how different PII detection paradigms fail under noisy, unstructured, and informal inputs. We construct a stress test benchmark spanning seven categories of natural distribution shift and evaluate representative systems from three widely deployed architectural families: encoder-based NER (SpaCy), rule-based hybrid detection (Presidio), and generative LLM extraction (Qwen2.5-3B).
  All three exhibit significant degradation on out-of-distribution inputs, but with distinct and complementary failure modes. Encoder models primarily fail on unseen surface forms and boundary detection, rule-based systems fail on non-standard formats, and LLMs exhibit entity-type confusion and generation instability. These results show that aggregate benchmark scores obscure deployment-critical weaknesses and that no single architecture is uniformly reliable across PII categories. Motivated by these findings, we propose a hybrid detection pipeline with a QA-driven feedback loop for iterative risk mitigation, and release our benchmark to support OOD-aware evaluation of PII systems.

</details>


### [320] [Restricted Eigenvalues Beyond Gaussian Width: Threshold Occupancy under Heavy Tails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03504)
*Shi Fu,Huibo Xu,Qixin Zhang,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对 Banerjee 等人 (2015) 提出的开放性问题，给出了明确的否定回答：仅凭均匀小波条件（uniform small-ball condition）不足以推导重尾设计下与次高斯测量相同的受限特征值（RE）样本量定律。研究揭示了“同时阈值占用”是主要障碍，并构造了具体的反例证明在重尾分布下 RE 会路径式失效，即便分布具有各向同性和有限矩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决 COLT 2015 提出的开放性问题，即探究在重尾设计（heavy-tailed designs）下，是否仅通过均匀小波条件就能获得与次高斯测量相同的样本量界限（$1+w(A)^2$），从而确定范数正则化估计量稳定恢复的理论边界。

Method: 通过构造具体的反例（如恒定宽度的多面体下降锥），利用“同时阈值占用”（simultaneous threshold occupancy）机制，证明在任意窄的球冠中存在精确的阈值编码。研究分析了固定阈值 VC 维下的最坏情况样本复杂度，并对比了高斯测量与重尾设计在相同锥体上的表现，同时引入了高斯平滑技术来构造具有任意差 RE 但密度光滑的分布。

Result: 1. 证明了所提出的定律在无维数、任意集合形式下失效。2. 发现对于固定阈值 VC 维 $d$，最坏情况样本复杂度为 $Θ(β^{-1}[d\log(1/β)+\log(1/δ)])$。3. 揭示了分离现象：在相同锥体上，高斯测量仅需 $O(1+\log(1/δ))$ 样本，而各向同性重尾设计在 $n\lesssim\sqrt{p/\log p}$ 时路径式失效。4. 提出了由各向同性下的仿射维数乘以平方包围半径控制的无分布回退界限。

Conclusion: 均匀小波条件本身不足以保证重尾设计下的稳定恢复，必须考虑“同时阈值占用”这一关键障碍。高斯平滑无法修复极差的 RE 性质，且在重尾情境下，基于仿射维数和包围半径的界限是尖锐且必要的替代方案。

Abstract: Restricted eigenvalue (RE) bounds govern stable recovery by norm-regularized estimators. For isotropic sub-Gaussian measurements, the benchmark sample size is $1+w(A)^2$, where $w(A)$ is the Gaussian width of the normalized descent cone. The COLT 2015 open-problem note (Banerjee et al., 2015) asked whether the same law follows for heavy-tailed designs from a uniform small-ball condition alone. We give an explicit and systematic negative answer to the general question as formulated there: the proposed law fails in its full dimension-free, arbitrary-set form, and the missing obstruction is simultaneous threshold occupancy. A constant-width polyhedral descent cone with fixed small-ball constants has zero empirical RE on every sample path up to half the ambient dimension. More generally, every finite range space admits exact threshold encoding in an arbitrarily narrow spherical cap and a lift to a full polyhedral descent-cone section. For every fixed threshold VC dimension $d$, as $β\downarrow0$, the sharp worst-case sample complexity is $Θ(β^{-1}[d\log(1/β)+\log(1/δ)])$. The separation persists under exact isotropy and all finite moments: on the same constant-width cone, Gaussian measurements succeed with $O(1+\log(1/δ))$ samples, whereas an isotropic heavy-tailed design fails pathwise for $n\lesssim\sqrt{p/\log p}$. Gaussian smoothing yields an everywhere-positive $C^\infty$ density while retaining arbitrarily poor RE. Under isotropy, a distribution-free fallback governed by affine dimension times squared enclosing radius is sharp on this family.

</details>


### [321] [An Adversarial Zero-Shot Learning Approach for Anomaly Detection in Multivariate IoT Traffic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03505)
*Mahshid Rezakhani,Tolunay Seyfi,Fatemeh Afghah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于对抗学习和对比损失的序列变分自编码器框架，用于物联网（IoT）网络中的多变量时间序列异常检测，支持零样本域适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网网络中的异常检测面临设备多样性、标注数据缺乏以及跨环境域差异等独特挑战。

Method: 提出了一种结合对抗学习和对比损失的序列变分自编码器（VAE）架构，引入编码器和解码器适配层以对齐跨域特征分布，并采用基于目的地的分段策略建模物联网通信结构，实现无需标注数据的零样本域适应。

Result: 在涵盖工业、企业、通用、智能家居和军事自动化领域的六个数据集及44个迁移场景上进行了评估，实验结果显示在多个跨域设置中具有强大的零样本泛化能力，并在现实异构和隐私受限的物联网条件下优于对比域适应基线。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了物联网异常检测中的域差异和标注数据缺乏问题，在跨域场景下表现出良好的泛化性能和竞争力。

Abstract: Anomaly detection in Internet of Things (IoT) networks presents unique challenges due to the diversity of devices, lack of labeled data, and domain variability across environments. In this paper, we propose a novel framework for multivariate time-series anomaly detection that leverages adversarial learning and contrastive loss within a sequence-based Variational Autoencoder (VAE) architecture. Our method enables zero-shot domain adaptation by jointly optimizing domain-invariant latent representations and semantically structured embedding spaces, without requiring labeled data or raw feature transfer. To address the heterogeneity of IoT deployments, we introduce encoder and decoder adaptor layers that align feature distributions across domains while preserving contextual semantics. Additionally, we propose a destination-based segmentation strategy to better model real-world communication structures in IoT traffic.
